Result: 在 1,000 句、与训练数据验证不相交的平衡诊断集上，最佳模型 mBART-50 v5 达到 69.3% 的 top-1 精确匹配准确率，其中连音 87.5%、主谓一致 43.5%。渐进式计划增益显著：引入上下文对后主谓一致从 1.0% 升至 52.5%；引入多点连音对后连音从 0% 升至 87.5%。另外量化了文献未报告的精确率-召回权衡：连音召回以恒等准确率单调降低为代价。对照方面，Tamil-LLaMA-7B-Instruct 零样本 19.0%、三次示范 24.7%，而复制基线为 20.0%，说明泰米尔语专用指令模型在缺少任务特定监督时无法迁移到专门的句子级纠错。

Conclusion: 端到端序列到序列模型配合四阶段渐进式训练，能有效解决泰米尔语词级方法无法处理的上下文语法与跨词连音错误，验证了训练计划（而非单纯的数据量）是准确率提升的关键驱动因素。研究同时首次量化了连音召回与恒等准确率之间的精确率-召回权衡，并表明泰米尔语指令模型在该专门纠错任务上不具备零样本/少样本迁移能力，需要任务特定监督。

Abstract: Tamil spell and grammar correction is challenging because Tamil is an agglutinative low-resource language with rich verbal morphology, complex sandhi (phonetic transformation) rules at word boundaries, and a script of 247 distinct letters. Prior work targets word-level surface errors with rule-based methods, statistical n-gram models, Minimum Edit Distance, or hybrid pipelines with a transformer re-ranker; such methods cannot reliably handle contextual errors - subject-verb agreement, tense consistency, or cross-word sandhi - which require sentence-level understanding. We propose an end-to-end sequence-to-sequence formulation and fine-tune mT5-small and mBART-50 on a synthetic corpus of up to 657,720 noisy-clean Tamil sentence pairs spanning ten error categories. Both backbones follow the same four-stage progressive schedule, each stage targeting one weakness: surface noise (v2), contextual grammar (v3), single-site sandhi (v4), and multi-site cross-word sandhi (v5). On a 1,000-sentence balanced diagnostic set verified disjoint from all training data, our best model, mBART-50 v5, reaches 69.3% top-1 exact-match accuracy, with 87.5% on sandhi and 43.5% on subject-verb agreement. The schedule is what produces these gains: subject-verb accuracy rises from 1.0% to 52.5% once contextual pairs are introduced, and sandhi from 0% to 87.5% once multi-site sandhi pairs are. We additionally quantify a precision-recall trade-off this literature has not reported: sandhi recall is paid for monotonically in identity accuracy. Finally, Tamil-LLaMA-7B-Instruct reaches 19.0% zero-shot and 24.7% with three demonstrations against a 20.0% copy baseline, showing that a Tamil-adapted instruction model does not transfer to specialised sentence-level correction without task-specific supervision.

</details>


### [131] [PACE: Towards Surfacing Hidden Conflicts in User Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03293)
*Yoojin Kim,Jihyoung Jang,Hyounghun Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PACE数据集与PaceMaker多智能体框架，用于评估和提升个性化助手识别用户请求与潜在约束（以自我中心知识或事件形式存在）之间冲突的能力，在隐式检索场景下显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化助手研究大多专注于准确执行用户请求，忽视了基于用户当前情境评估请求是否恰当的必要性。同时，已有的冲突或安全检测工作依赖显式提供的因素，而真实场景中冲突因素通常是隐式的，需从知识库（KB）中检索，这构成了一个未解决的挑战。

Method: (1) 构建PACE数据集：将植根于明确定义人设的用户请求与自我中心知识库事实配对，要求模型整合上下文证据以判断请求是否冲突，从而模拟隐式检索场景。(2) 提出PaceMaker多智能体框架：包含查询改写、多跳图遍历和冲突感知过滤三个专门化智能体，协同检索具有上下文决定性的证据。

Result: 实验在PACE上同时评估证据检索质量与冲突决策准确率，PaceMaker在两项指标上均一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 隐式知识库检索环境阻碍了用户请求与冲突诱发知识之间的直接关联，使现有模型难以识别相关的用户专属事实；PaceMaker通过多智能体协同检索有效解决了这一难题，证明其在基于情境的冲突评估任务上的优势。

Abstract: Personalized assistants should not only comply with user requests but also assess whether those requests are appropriate given the user's current circumstances. However, prior work has primarily focused on accurately executing requests, overlooking the need for assistants to account for context and engage in conflict-based refusal. Furthermore, while existing work on conflict or safety detection relies on explicitly provided factors, real-world scenarios often involve implicit factors that must be retrieved from a knowledge base (KB). To this end, we introduce Personalized Assistants for Conflict Evaluation (PACE), a dataset for evaluating whether models can identify latent constraints, expressed as egocentric knowledge or events, that render seemingly reasonable user requests inappropriate. PACE pairs user requests grounded in well-defined personas with egocentric KB facts, requiring models to integrate contextual evidence to determine whether a request is conflicting. This implicit retrieval setting hinders the direct association between user requests and conflict-inducing knowledge, making it difficult for existing models to identify relevant user-specific facts. To address this challenge, we further propose PaceMaker, a multi-agent framework in which specialized agents coordinate across query reformulation, multi-hop graph traversal, and conflict-aware filtering to retrieve contextually decisive evidence. Experiments on PACE evaluate both evidence retrieval quality and conflict decision accuracy, showing that PaceMaker consistently outperforms existing approaches.

</details>


### [132] [Decoupling Turn-Taking from Semantics: A Decoupled Data Approach for Finite-State-Machine-Based Full-Duplex Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03321)
*Yihang Li,Chenhui Chu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种解耦数据方法（DEF-FSM），通过从真实人与人（HH）口语对话中学习转向控制，而从可配置的人机（HA）文本对话中塑造语义行为，以改善神经有限状态机（NFSM）框架在对话中的转向自然度，同时保持语义能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NFSM框架利用合成文本数据训练转向控制，但LLM无法忠实模拟真实人类对话中细粒度的声学时间动态，导致转向自然度受限。

Method: 提出解耦数据方法：1）规则驱动的事件引导数据变换方法，将HH口语对话序列化为FSM磁带（通过分类转向事件并应用确定性映射规则），无需LLM生成注释即可扩展监督；2）提出源感知校准（SAC）损失，联合校准状态转移令牌的长尾分布，并将各数据源引导至其最优监督的能力。

Result: 实验表明该方法显著提升了转向熟练度，同时恢复基础LLM的语义能力。

Conclusion: 解耦数据来源并结合规则变换与SAC损失，能够在不依赖LLM合成数据的前提下提升全双工对话系统的转向自然度，兼顾语义性能。

Abstract: The Neural Finite State Machine (NFSM) framework offers a pragmatic path to full-duplex dialogue by serializing turn-taking control and response generation onto a single causal tape under the standard next-token prediction objective, thereby preserving semantic prowess at a low fine-tuning cost. However, its reliance on synthetic text data fundamentally limits turn-taking naturalness, as Large Language Models (LLMs) cannot faithfully simulate the fine-grained acoustic temporal dynamics of real human dialogues. In this work, we propose a decoupled data approach that learns turn-taking from real Human-Human (HH) spoken dialogues while shaping semantic behavior through configurable Human-Agent (HA) text dialogues. To operationalize this approach, we introduce a rule-based event-guided data transformation method that serializes HH spoken dialogues into FSM tapes by classifying turn-taking events and applying deterministic mapping rules, enabling scalable supervision without LLM-generated annotations. We further propose a Source-Aware Calibrated (SAC) Loss that jointly calibrates the long-tailed distribution of state transition tokens and channels each data source toward the capability it best supervises. Experiments show that our approach substantially improves turn-taking proficiency while recovering the foundation LLM's semantic capability. Our code and model are available at https://github.com/Liyht/def-fsm.

</details>


### [133] [How Perturbations Propagate: A Multi-Level Analysis of Robustness in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03322)
*Dun Li Chan,Emily Liu,Niyathi Allu,Christian Hoang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究输入扰动（打字错误、损坏文本、词替换、打乱词序）在仅解码器语言模型中的传播，从输出行为、隐藏状态几何和注意力头功能三个层面分析。发现扰动类型产生可区分的指标特征，单一行为或表征指标的鲁棒性结论可能具有误导性，因此主张多层面评估语言模型计算受扰动的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型鲁棒性研究通常仅通过输出行为来评估，忽略了扰动如何在模型内部计算（隐藏状态几何与注意力头功能）中传播。作者希望打破这种单一评估维度，探索不同扰动在模型内部各层面的可区分性特征，并检验单一指标断言的可靠性。

Method: 研究在四个GPT-2和两个Qwen2.5检查点上评估六种自然与合成输入扰动。使用居中核对齐（CKA）和固有维度分析逐层几何；检测GPT-2中的注意力头响应；采用激活修补恢复与HotFlip梯度引导扰动对比随机词替换。

Result: 扰动类型产生可区分的指标特征，且不完全被输出测度捕获；跨测试检查点仅部分一致。复制得分与词替换和打乱下的激活修补恢复关联尤为紧密。HotFlip扰动在GPT-2中比速率匹配的随机词替换造成更强的行为与表征破坏，其行为效应在全部六个检查点一致。

Conclusion: 基于单一行为或表征指标的鲁棒性声明可能产生误导，需要多层面评估来全面理解扰动如何改变语言模型的计算过程。

Abstract: Language models encounter typos, corrupted text, altered words, and disrupted token order, yet robustness is usually evaluated only through output behavior. We study how six naturalistic and synthetic input perturbations propagate through decoder-only language models at three levels: output behavior, hidden-state geometry, and attention-head function. We evaluate behavioral effects across four GPT-2 and two Qwen2.5 checkpoints by analyzing layerwise geometry using centered kernel alignment and intrinsic dimension, and examine attention-head responses in GPT-2. Perturbation types produce distinguishable metric profiles that are not fully captured by output measures and are only partly consistent across the tested checkpoints. Copying scores are especially associated with activation-patching recovery under token substitution and shuffling. Gradient-guided HotFlip perturbations also cause stronger behavioral and representational disruption than rate-matched random token substitutions in GPT-2; their behavioral effects are consistent across all six tested checkpoints. Our results show that robustness claims based on a single behavioral or representational metric can be misleading, and motivate multi-level evaluation of how perturbations alter language-model computation.

</details>


### [134] [Less Is Moral: A CHARMing Framework for Moral Foundations Detection in Endorsement Behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03330)
*Huixiang Fu,Marian-Andrei Rizoiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CHARM是一个基于轻量级微调大语言模型的道德基础检测框架，融合MAC交叉注意力、理由对齐和极性感知的仇恨言论信号三种心理构念，在域内AUC最高提升15.3%，并在所有跨域数据集上超越监督基线，为大规模道德语境内容分析提供了低成本、可扩展的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有道德基础检测系统存在三大缺陷：跨领域泛化能力差、缺乏理由依据支撑、过度依赖成本高昂的基于提示的大语言模型。同时，传统基于词典、微调或提示的检测器将计算方法与心理学理论相割裂。作者旨在构建一个既能忠实反映心理学构念、又具备低成本可扩展性的检测框架。

Method: CHARM采用轻量级微调LLM架构，包含三个模块化组件：(1) MAC交叉注意力，编码道德构念的互补性；(2) 理由对齐，利用MFTCXplain中更丰富的监督信号增强理由依据；(3) 极性感知的仇恨言论调制，引入极性感知信号。每个组件都对应一个明确的心理构念。训练采用MFTC、MFRC和News数据池30%子样本并辅以MFTCXplain增强监督。

Result: 在域内数据集上AUC最高提升15.3%；在所有跨域数据集上AUC和F1均超越监督式基线；性能可媲美或优于基于提示的LLM检测器但成本更低。应用于大规模COVID-19 Twitter语料时，发现道德价值对齐与在线点赞转发等背书行为存在强关联。

Conclusion: CHARM证明了将心理学构念以模块化方式融入轻量级模型可以有效兼顾鲁棒性、忠实性与可扩展性。该框架使道德框架化在大规模上可测量，为研究道德化错误信息的传播提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Moral language plays a central role in shaping online endorsement and the diffusion of information, yet existing moral foundation detection systems often suffer from poor cross-domain generalization, weak rationale grounding, and reliance on costly prompting-based large language models (LLMs). We introduce CHARM, a MA\textbf{C}- and \textbf{H}ate-speech-\textbf{A}ware \textbf{R}ationale-aligned \textbf{M}oral foundation detection framework built on a lightweight fine-tuned LLM, which integrates complementary moral grounding, rationale alignment, and polarity-aware hate speech signals to support more robust and faithful moral prediction. Unlike prior dictionary-, fine-tune-, or prompt-based detectors, which decouple computation from psychological theory, CHARM is built so that each component -- MAC cross-attention, rationale alignment, and hate-speech modulation -- operationalizes a distinct psychological construct. Using a 30\% subsample of the MFTC, MFRC, and News training pools together with the richer supervision in MFTCXplain, CHARM improves AUC by up to 15.3\% in-domain, surpasses the supervised baselines on every out-of-domain dataset in both AUC and F1, and offers a scalable, low-cost alternative to prompting-based LLM detectors. We further apply CHARM to large-scale COVID-19 discourse on Twitter and show that moral value alignment is strongly associated with online endorsement behavior. By making moral framing measurable at scale, CHARM offers a practical tool for studying the spread of morally charged misinformation.

</details>


### [135] [FPCO-Dialog: A Multi-Turn False-Premise Benchmark for Correction and Cooperation in Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03331)
*Jiayuan Ma,Yuqi Lu,Weiyang Guo,Chenrui Wang,Junyi Shu,Xuebo Liu,Min Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FPCO-Dialog基准，用于评估视觉语言模型在多轮对话中面对持续存在的错误前提时的纠正与合作行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言模型在多轮交互中常遇到用户对所描述视觉内容持有错误假设的情况，但现有评测很少隔离研究同一视觉错误的错误前提在多个对话轮次中持续出现时模型的行为表现，缺乏针对性评估工具。

Method: 构建FPCO-Dialog基准数据集（含1,080张图像与10,800个问答轮次），按视觉复杂度、目标类别和错误前提类别进行分层；设计10轮对话协议（前缀为正确对话，后续为重复的错误前提指代表达）；提出模型无关的评测协议与CorrTP@K指标（基于错误前提轮次的纠正率度量），由两个独立检测器进行评分。

Result: 对20个商业及开源视觉语言模型进行评估，结果表明模型间在总体纠正倾向、逐轮动态以及错误前提类型间的系统性差异方面存在显著且持续的不同。

Conclusion: FPCO-Dialog揭示了不同VLM在持续错误前提场景下纠正与合作行为的显著差异，数据集、评测协议、模型输出、检测器标签及代码均开源提供。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed in multi-turn settings where users may describe visual content with incorrect assumptions. Yet existing evaluations rarely isolate how models respond when the same visually grounded false premise persists across dialogue turns. We introduce FPCO-Dialog, a benchmark for evaluating correction and cooperation behavior in VLMs under repeated false premises. FPCO-Dialog contains 1,080 images and 10,800 question turns, stratified by visual complexity, object category, and false-premise class, and uses a 10-turn protocol in which a correct dialogue prefix is followed by repeated false-premise referring expressions. We evaluate 20 commercial and open-source VLMs with a model-agnostic protocol and CorrTP@K, a correction-rate metric over false-premise turns, scored by two independent detectors. FPCO-Dialog reveals substantial and persistent cross-model differences in aggregate correction tendency, model-specific turn-wise dynamics, and systematic variation across false-premise types under the benchmark's substitution distribution. The dataset, evaluation protocol, model outputs, detector labels, and code are available.

</details>


### [136] [Accountable AI with Grounded, Faithful, Consistent, Actionable Rationales: A Case Study in Clinical Trial Matching with VERDICT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03366)
*Zikai Zhou,Yufei Jin,Yilin Xu,Yu-Chiang Wang,Chieh-Ju Chao,Monica S. Lam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出自忠实性（self-faithfulness）作为问责制的自动测试，并介绍VERDICT系统，利用SMT/MaxSMT求解器实现临床试验匹配中的决策一致性、可核查理由和反事实自忠实性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 问责制要求决策可检查、可论证、可争议，但LLM输出可能无根据、不完整或不忠实于决策过程。现有LLM在临床试验匹配中虽准确但决策策略不一致，理由不忠实于自身决策。

Method: VERDICT是一个基于LLM的代理，将决策任务、约束和策略转化为SMT（可满足性模理论），然后利用SMT和MaxSMT求解器推导决策，从而确保策略应用一致且决策在构造上可问责。

Result: 在SIGIR 2016衍生的数据集和TREC 2021上，VERDICT在决策准确性上优于纯LLM和神经符号基线，策略一致性完美，生成的临床医生偏好的理由基于明确假设和关键条件，改进了反事实自忠实性。

Conclusion: VERDICT通过将LLM与形式化求解器结合，实现了高准确性、一致性和可问责的决策，为高风险的医疗决策提供了一种可靠范式。

Abstract: Accountability means a decision can be examined, justified, and contested. LLMs make this hard: fluent output may be ungrounded, incomplete, or unfaithful to the decision process. Achieving accountability requires verified rationales (how was the decision reached), assumptions (what was assumed rather than known), policy consistency (the same treatment for the same facts), and pivotal conditions (what would change the outcome). We introduce self-faithfulness as an automatic test of accountability: changing the pivotal conditions should change the decision.
  We examine accountable AI through clinical trial matching, a high-stakes task central to evidence-based medicine. Although LLM-based matchers match patients to trials reasonably accurately, they apply decision policies inconsistently and produce rationales that are unfaithful to their own decisions.
  We introduce VERDICT, an LLM-based agent that translates a decision task, its constraints, and its policy into Satisfiability Modulo Theories (SMT), then derives the decision with SMT and MaxSMT solvers -- so policies are applied consistently and decisions are accountable by construction.
  Across a SIGIR 2016-derived dataset and TREC 2021, VERDICT achieves the strongest decision accuracy among LLM-only and neurosymbolic baselines, applies policies with perfect consistency, and produces clinician-preferred rationales grounded in explicit assumptions and pivotal conditions, with improved counterfactual self-faithfulness.

</details>


### [137] [FrameBench:A Language Understanding Benchmark Based on Frame Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03370)
*Chihiro Yano,Ryohei Sasano*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文引入FrameBench，一个基于框架语义学的基准，用于测试大语言模型是否能区分同一动词在不同语境下所激发的语义框架，从而判断模型是否具备人类阅读理解中的隐含信息丰富能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型在众多下游任务中表现优异，但尚不清楚它们能否像人类一样在理解句子时进行基于语义框架的隐含信息丰富，因此需要专门的基准来评估这一能力。

Method: 构建FrameBench基准，包含基于FrameNet风格资源的多选题，测试模型区分同一动词在不同语境下所激发框架的能力，覆盖英语和日语，采用生成-验证流程并辅以母语者判断。

Result: 实验表明小模型面临挑战，而多个大型模型的得分超过了人类参考分数。

Conclusion: FrameBench作为评估工具揭示了模型在框架语义理解上的规模差异，并公开了数据集和代码以促进后续研究。

Abstract: In frame semantics, sentence comprehension is assumed to proceed by relating lexical meaning to background knowledge called semantic frames, thereby enabling readers to implicitly enrich the text with unstated information. Recent large language models (LLMs) have achieved strong performance across a wide range of downstream tasks. However, it remains unclear whether they can reproduce the kinds of implicit enrichment that humans naturally make during comprehension. To address this question, we introduce FrameBench, a benchmark grounded in frame semantics. FrameBench consists of multiple-choice questions that test whether models distinguish the frames evoked by the same verb across contexts. We construct the benchmark for English and Japanese using FrameNet-style resources and a generation-and-verification pipeline with native-speaker judgments. Our experiments on a diverse set of models reveal challenges for small models, while several large models surpass the human reference scores. We release the constructed FrameBench dataset and the code for dataset construction and evaluation at https://github.com/SasanoLab/FrameBench.

</details>


### [138] [Chiaroscuro for Emotions: A Contrastive Emotion Benchmark Grounded in Appraisal Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03394)
*Divyesh Bommana,Mohammad Saim,Tianyu Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CHIARO基准，一个包含1000条人工标注句子的对比情绪推断数据集，基于评估理论，每个场景描述同一事件引发两人相反情绪；评测7个前沿LLM和4个现成情绪分类器，最强LLM宏F1为67.3，低于人类一致性，现成分类器接近随机；该数据集还可作为训练信号，结合现有语料可提升自身及6/10外部基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有情绪识别基准大多为每个文本预测单一情绪，无法覆盖真实世界中同一事件引发两人相反情绪的场景，缺乏专门针对这一对比性情绪推断的评测资源。

Method: 构建CHIARO：1000条人工标注的句子基准，基于评估理论（appraisal theory），每场景含一个因果触发事件，使一人产生积极情绪、另一人产生消极情绪，采用十类情绪分类体系；随后用七个前沿LLM和四个现成情绪分类器进行基准测试，并验证其作为训练数据的效果。

Result: 最强LLM达到67.3宏F1，明显低于人类一致性；现成情绪分类器几乎接近随机水平。将CHIARO与现有情绪语料结合训练后，下游分类器在CHIARO自身和十个外部情绪基准中的六个上取得提升。

Conclusion: CHIARO是一个具有挑战性的对比情绪推断基准，揭示了现有模型在理解同一事件引发相反情绪上的不足，同时可作为互补训练信号提升通用情绪识别性能。

Abstract: Emotion recognition benchmarks often predict one emotion per text, missing many real-world scenarios where two people arrive at opposing emotions from a single shared event. For example, a child kicks the seat in front of her in excitement while the passenger ahead grows angry. We introduce CHIARO, a 1,000 human-annotated sentence benchmark for contrastive emotion inference grounded in appraisal theory. Each scene describes one causal trigger eliciting a positive emotion in one person and a negative emotion in the other, drawn from a ten-class taxonomy. We benchmark seven frontier LLMs and four off-the-shelf emotion classifiers. The strongest LLM reaches 67.3 macro-F1, well below human agreement, while existing emotion classifiers score near chance. Beyond evaluation, CHIARO also serves as a training signal. When combined with an existing emotion corpus, the resulting downstream classifier improves on CHIARO itself and on six of ten external emotion benchmarks, which positions our dataset as a complementary signal for emotion recognition.

</details>


### [139] [TabScope: Question-Adaptive Scope Selection for Table Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03395)
*Yuxiang Wang,Junhao Gan,Jianzhong Qi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现LLM在表格问答中的性能随表格变大而下降，且这种下降在不同问题类型上并不均匀：对定位敏感的问题受无关内容影响大，而需更广证据的问题反而受益于全表推理。据此提出问题自适应框架，通过操作感知的表格分解构造问题相关子表，并用预测的问题类型动态选择定位推理或全表推理模式，同时引入银标准参考子表用于评测证据选择，并构建了基于真实长表格的SLQA基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM在长表格问答中性能下降显著，但鲜有工作注意到这种退化在不同问题类型间并不一致：定位敏感型问题易受无关内容干扰，而需要广泛证据的问题仍可能从全表推理中获益。因此长表问答不仅需要解决"如何定位"的问题，还须回答"何时应定位"的问题，现有方法缺乏对这种差异化的考量。

Method: 提出一个问题自适应的问答框架：通过操作感知的表格分解（operation-aware table decomposition）构造仅含相关证据的问题特定子表；利用预测的问题类型决定采用定位推理（localized reasoning）还是全表推理（full-table reasoning）；此外引入银标准参考子表（silver reference sub-tables）作为证据选择的评测参照，并构建基于真实长表格的SLQA基准。

Result: 在WikiTQ和SLQA上的实验表明：定位策略对查找类（lookup）和局部推理类问题尤为有效；而自适应地在定位推理与全表推理之间进行选择可取得最佳整体性能，说明不同问题类型需要匹配不同的推理模式。

Conclusion: 长表问答的关键在于不仅要决定如何对表格进行定位，还要决定何时该进行定位。问题类型自适应的证据选择与推理模式切换能显著提升长表格场景下的问答准确率，研究代码与数据集将在论文发表后公开。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown strong performance on table question answering, yet their accuracy often degrades as table size increases. We find that this degradation is not uniform across question types. Localization-sensitive questions are particularly affected by irrelevant table content, while questions requiring broader evidence may still benefit from full-table reasoning. Based on this observation, we propose a question-adaptive framework that dynamically selects between localized and full-table reasoning. The framework constructs question-specific sub-tables through operation-aware table decomposition and uses the predicted question type to determine the appropriate reasoning mode. We further introduce silver reference sub-tables for evaluating evidence selection and construct SLQA, a benchmark based on real-world long tables. Experiments on WikiTQ and SLQA show that localization is particularly effective for lookup and local reasoning questions, while adaptive selection between localized and full-table reasoning achieves the best overall performance. These results highlight that long-table QA requires deciding not only how to localize, but also when to localize. Our code and datasets will be made available upon publication of the paper.

</details>


### [140] [To What Extent Do Large Language Models Understand Bangla Idioms?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03410)
*Mousumi Akter,Md. Faiyaz Abdullah Sayeedi,Nurul Labib Sayeedi,Swakkhar Shatabda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文首次构建了孟加拉语习语的大规模基准数据集，并配套合成多选题数据集用于习语意义识别。系统评估了多种最新LLM在释义、习语跨度检测和意义识别三个任务上的零样本与少样本表现，发现模型性能差异显著且无单一模型全面领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 习语是自然语言的重要组成部分，反映文化细微差别，尤其对低资源语言中的计算模型构成独特挑战。现有研究缺乏针对孟加拉语习语的系统评估资源，因此需要构建基准数据集并比较不同LLM的理解能力。

Method: 创建孟加拉语习语的首个大规模基准数据集和合成多选题数据集，设计三个任务（释义、习语跨度检测、意义识别），采用零样本和少样本提示策略对多个最新LLM进行全面评估。

Result: 模型性能差异显著，没有单一LLM在所有任务上一致最优。Phi-4-mini-instruct在释义任务上最佳，Kimi-K2-32b-instruct在跨度检测上最佳，Gemini-2.5-flash在意义识别上最佳。

Conclusion: 该数据集和分析为提升LLM对习语的理解提供宝贵资源，尤其有助于推动孟加拉语及其他低资源语言的习语处理研究。

Abstract: Idiomatic expressions are an integral part of natural language, reflecting cultural nuances and posing unique challenges for computational models, particularly in low-resource languages. In this paper, we present the first large-scale benchmark dataset of Bangla idioms, complemented by a synthetic multiple-choice question (MCQ) dataset for idiom meaning identification. We conduct a comprehensive evaluation of recent large language models (LLMs) across three idiom-related tasks: paraphrasing, idiom span detection, and meaning identification, leveraging zero-shot and few-shot prompting strategies. Our results reveal substantial variability in model performance, with no single LLM consistently outperforming others across all tasks. Notably, Phi-4-mini-instruct excels in paraphrasing, Kimi-K2-32b-instruct in span detection, and Gemini-2.5-flash in meaning identification. We believe that our datasets and analyses will provide valuable resources to guide future research in improving LLM comprehension of idiomatic expressions, particularly in Bangla and other low-resource languages.

</details>


### [141] [Lngram v2: Latent N-Gram Memory with Interpretable Discrete Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03426)
*Yunao Zheng,Bin Wen,Xiaojie Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Lngram v2 是一种改进的、可扩展的潜在条件记忆机制，通过解耦路由数、记忆维度和骨干宽度，并引入上下文感知的分组查询注意力读出（context-aware grouped-query attention readout）来独立扩展记忆容量，同时利用零值Sink和反事实代理梯度改善读出选择性与路由可训练性。实验表明其在视觉-语言模型（含30B规模）上持续改进性能，并大幅减少记忆参数，其离散ID还能保留连续隐藏状态的语义结构，为分析内部表示提供结构化接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformers 缺乏原生的查找（lookup）机制，需要反复的密集计算来识别和复用局部的静态模式。Lngram v1 通过离散潜在 n-gram 寻址引入了与分词器无关的条件记忆，但其记忆容量与骨干网络宽度耦合，导致参数量和激活成本高、难以扩展。本文旨在解耦记忆容量与骨干宽度，以更低的成本实现可独立扩展的潜在条件记忆。

Method: Lngram v2 提出三个核心设计：(1) 解耦路由数量、记忆维度与骨干宽度，使记忆容量可独立扩展；(2) 引入上下文感知的分组查询注意力读出机制（context-aware grouped-query attention readout）以高效读取记忆；(3) 采用零值Sink（zero-value Sink）和反事实代理梯度（counterfactual surrogate gradients）来提升读出选择性和路由可训练性，同时保持硬离散寻址的离散性。

Result: 在多个不同规模的视觉-语言模型（VLM）上取得一致的性能提升，成功扩展到30B参数模型。与 Lngram v1 相比，Lngram v2 大幅减少了总记忆参数和激活记忆参数，同时保持或提升了语言建模性能。进一步分析表明，其离散ID保留了连续隐藏状态的显著语义结构，仅凭ID即可进行语义恢复，并在不同数据集间保持稳定的ID-语义关联。

Conclusion: Lngram v2 被确立为一种高效且可扩展的潜在条件记忆机制，能够在不牺牲性能的前提下实现记忆容量的独立扩展，其离散地址还提供了一种结构化接口，可用于分析模型的内部表示。

Abstract: Transformers lack a native lookup mechanism, requiring repeated dense computation to recognize and reuse local static patterns. Lngram v1 introduces tokenizer-independent conditional memory through discrete latent n-gram addressing, but its memory capacity is coupled with the backbone width, limiting scalability due to high parameter and activation costs. We propose Lngram v2, which decouples the number of routes, memory dimension, and backbone width, and introduces a context-aware grouped-query attention readout to scale memory capacity independently. A zero-value Sink and counterfactual surrogate gradients further improve readout selectivity and routing trainability while preserving hard discrete addressing. Experiments across vision--language models (VLMs) of different scales show consistent improvements, including successful scaling to a 30B-parameter model. Compared with Lngram v1, Lngram v2 substantially reduces both total and activated memory parameters while maintaining or improving language modeling performance. Further analysis shows that its discrete IDs preserve substantial semantic structure of continuous hidden states, enabling semantic recovery from IDs alone and stable ID--semantic associations across datasets. These results establish Lngram v2 as an efficient and scalable latent conditional memory mechanism whose discrete addresses also provide a structured interface for analyzing internal model representations.

</details>


### [142] [Random Attention: Rethinking KV Cache Eviction for Efficient Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03430)
*Heng Wang,Jielin Qiu,Wenting Zhao,Cheng Qian,Liangwei Yang,Jiawei Han,Heng Ji,Silvio Savarese,Shelby Heinecke,Huan Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现现有KV缓存压缩方法中“评分选择”信号几乎无用。随机注意力（Random Attention）保留提示词并在每个注意力头内均匀随机驱逐token，不计算任何分数；在四个模型和六个推理任务上，其性能与最强先验驱逐器相当，且在vLLM部署中吞吐量高出32-43%。对照实验表明：1）提示词是缓存中最脆弱的部分，选择器之间的差距主要源于是否恰好保留了提示词；2）推理轨迹通过文本层和注意力头层两个层面的冗余自我保护，因此提示词安全后随机抽取足以保留所需副本，无需评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在需要长推理的任务上表现优异，但长思维链使KV缓存成为严重的内存瓶颈。现有KV缓存压缩方法都遵循同一范式：为每个缓存token打分并保留高分token。作者质疑这种评分信号的必要性，希望验证能否在不计算任何分数的情况下实现同等或更好的压缩效果。

Method: 提出Random Attention方法：保留提示词部分，在每个注意力头内均匀随机驱逐token，完全不计算重要性分数。在四个模型和六个推理任务上对比最强先验驱逐器，并在vLLM部署中测量吞吐量。同时设计受控实验，分别验证提示词的脆弱性以及推理轨迹在两个层面（文本层面的重述冗余、跨注意力头的副本冗余）的自我保护机制。

Result: Random Attention在性能上与最强先验驱逐器相当，但vLLM部署中吞吐量高出32-43%。受控实验证明评分选择信号几乎无贡献：选择器之间的性能差距主要源于是否保留了提示词；一旦提示词安全，随机抽取足以保留推理所需的冗余副本。

Conclusion: KV缓存压缩无需复杂的评分选择机制，随机驱逐即可达到同等效果。关键在于保护提示词，而推理轨迹自身的冗余保证了随机保留的充分性。该发现简化了缓存压缩设计，并公开了代码。

Abstract: Large language models achieve superior performance on tasks that require extended reasoning, but long chains of thought make the KV cache a severe memory bottleneck. Existing KV cache compression methods share one paradigm: score each cached token by some estimate of how much it will matter later, and keep the top-scoring ones. We show that the selection signal contributes almost nothing. Random Attention keeps the prompt and evicts uniformly at random within each attention head, computing no score at all; across four models and six reasoning tasks it matches the strongest prior evictor while serving 32-43% higher throughput than it in vLLM deployment. Controlled experiments explain this by showing that 1) the prompt is the fragile part of the cache, and most of the gap between selectors is just whether their selection signal happened to keep it; 2) the reasoning trace protects itself against eviction with redundancy at two levels, in the text (the model restates what it still needs as it works) and across attention heads (each keeps its own copy of the trace), so once the prompt is safe, a random draw retains enough copies of what the model still needs, and no score is required to pick them. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/SalesforceAIResearch/Random-Attention.

</details>


### [143] [Decoupled Analysis-Judging: An Automated Creativity Evaluator Using LLMs in Complex Multi-step Creativity Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03432)
*Xiangyu Wang,Jin Wu,Xiaoyu Li,Chanjin Zheng,Yifeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出CreaEval，一种用于复杂多步创造力任务（CGPST）的自动化评估器，通过将判断过程解耦为记忆增强分析和基于证据的判断两个阶段，缓解LLM作为评判者的偏差，平均性能提升22.74%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-Judge在创造力任务评估中易受冗长偏差和宽松偏差影响，尤其在上下文 grounded、程序化结构的复杂多步任务（CGPST）中更不稳定且不公正。现有方法（任务特定训练或直接应用LLM判断）难以保证可靠评估。

Method: CreaEval将典型LLM-as-a-Judge解耦为分析阶段和判断阶段。第一阶段（记忆增强分析）使用SoT-LLM将多步响应转换为结构化评估证据，并纳入跨步记忆；第二阶段（基于证据的判断）使用Judge-LLM仅依据提取的证据进行判断，不访问原始响应。

Result: 在CGPST和两个经典简单创造力任务上，CreaEval平均性能比第二好的基线提升22.74%，展示了泛化能力。

Conclusion: CreaEval通过解耦分析与判断，有效缓解LLM评判偏差，为复杂创造力任务提供更可靠的自动化评估方案，代码已开源。

Abstract: Automated evaluation of creativity tasks remains challenging for LLM-as-a-Judge, as LLM is susceptible to biases such as verbosity bias and leniency bias. Such limitations are particularly evident in Contextually-Grounded and Procedurally-Structured Tasks (CGPST), a complex multi-step creativity task where inter-step dependencies, highly subjectivity, and wide scoring ranges lead to more unstable and biased judgments. Existing approaches either rely on task-specific training or directly apply LLM-as-a-Judge, both of which struggle to ensure reliable evaluation under such complexity. To bridge these gaps, we propose CreaEval, an automated creativity evaluator for CGPST that decouples typical LLM-as-a-Judge into analysis and judging. Correspondingly, CreaEval involves two critical phases: Memory-augmented Analysis, a SoT-LLM converts multi-step responses into structured evaluation evidence, incorporating cross-step memory; and Evidence-based Judging, a Judge-LLM uses the extracted evidence for judging without accessing raw responses. Comprehensive experiments show that CreaEval achieves an average performance improvement of 22.74% over the second-best baselines across CGPST and two classic simple creativity tasks, demonstrating its generalizability. The code is available at https://github.com/Jaong/CreaEval.

</details>


### [144] [When Retrieval Helps: Selective Retrieval for Single-Turn Mental-Health QA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03454)
*Hyunseo Oh,Chong-Kwon Kim,Yoonhyuk Choi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究检索增强生成（RAG）在单轮心理健康问答中的适用性，发现检索并非总是有益的：无条件检索虽提升特异性却降低整体质量并引入安全敏感失败；基于草稿条件的轻量级选择性检索策略，通过心理教育需求、应对需求和响应特异性三个维度及规则式安全触发器，可在低需求场景保留闭卷行为、避免无条件检索带来的额外退化，表明检索激活本质上是安全敏感的控制决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG能提升大语言模型响应的特异性和接地性，但在单轮心理健康问答中效果并非均匀有益——用户查询常混合情绪困扰、治疗关切和安全敏感需求。现有研究缺乏对"何时检索有益、何时有害"的系统分析，也缺少轻量级策略来平衡这一权衡，这正是本文的出发点。

Method: 作者将检索需求操作化为三个基于草稿条件的效用维度（心理教育需求、应对需求、响应特异性），辅以基于规则的触发机制确保安全。遵循心理治疗接地RAG系统（如coTherapist），构建了包含应对策略、心理教育和安全资源三类内容的紧凑可控指南语料库。使用QLoRA在MentalChat16K上微调指令式生成器，并在CounselBench-Eval和CounselBench-Adv上对比闭卷、始终检索与选择性检索三种设置。

Result: 实验表明检索在该领域并非均匀有益：始终检索能提升特异性但降低整体质量，并引入额外的安全敏感失败；选择性检索在低需求场景保留闭卷行为，同时避免无条件检索带来的额外退化。

Conclusion: 研究支持"检索激活是安全敏感的控制决策"这一观点，即选择性检索策略能更好地控制RAG在心理健康QA中的利弊权衡，为心理领域RAG系统的安全设计提供依据。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) can improve the specificity and grounding of large language model responses, but its effect is not uniformly beneficial in single-turn mental-health question answering, where user queries often combine emotional distress, treatment concerns, and safety-sensitive needs. We study when retrieval helps or hurts mental-health QA, and whether a lightweight selective retrieval policy can better control this trade-off. We operationalize retrieval need using three draft-conditioned utility dimensions: psychoeducational need, coping need, and response specificity, together with a rule-based safety trigger. Following psychotherapy-grounded RAG systems such as coTherapist, we construct a compact and controllable guideline corpus comprising coping-strategy, psychoeducational, and safety resources. We fine-tune an instruction-tuned generator on MentalChat16K using QLoRA and compare Closed-book, Always Retrieval, and Selective Retrieval settings on CounselBench-Eval and CounselBench-Adv. Experiments show that retrieval is not uniformly beneficial in this domain. Always Retrieval improves specificity but lowers overall quality and introduces additional safety-sensitive failures. Selective Retrieval preserves closed-book behavior for low-need cases while avoiding the additional degradation caused by unconditional retrieval, supporting the view that retrieval activation is a safety-sensitive control decision.

</details>


### [145] [When Users Don't Ask: Benchmarking Context-Driven Memory Retrieval in Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03467)
*Wen-Yu Chang,Yun-Nung Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LOCOMO-CONV，一个基于LoCoMo衍生的对话式记忆基准，包含四种查询风格（对话、隐式、反事实、组合）。在五个代表性记忆系统上评估检索召回和端到端回复质量，发现对话式框架暴露了QA基准忽略的检索差距，尤其是隐式和组合查询；多面查询改写能缩小原始轮次记忆的差距但对抽象记忆无效。强检索并不完全转化为回复质量，且隐式查询存在"静默接地"现象。研究表明基于推理的记忆扩展是有前景的方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM记忆基准主要通过QA式探测评估记忆，而非在真实对话场景中评估记忆的现场使用。这种评估方式忽略了对话式查询与QA查询之间的差异，无法真实反映记忆系统在长时程对话代理中的实际表现，因此需要一种对话式记忆基准来弥补这一空白。

Method: 从LoCoMo构建LOCOMO-CONV对话记忆基准，设计了四种查询风格（对话式、隐式、反事实、组合式）；在五个代表性记忆系统上评估检索召回与端到端回复质量；分析多面查询改写的作用，并引入辅助的supportive_memory标注以捕捉对话中有用的上下文信息。

Result: 实验发现：对话式框架暴露了QA基准未发现的检索差距，尤其在隐式和组合查询上；多面查询改写对原始轮次记忆有效但对抽象记忆无效；强检索不能完全转化为回复质量；隐式查询存在"静默接地"现象，即记忆改善上下文接地但未明确浮现黄金事实。

Conclusion: 对话式框架揭示了现有记忆评估的盲点，指出基于推理的记忆扩展（reasoning-based memory elaboration）是有前景的研究方向；同时发布supportive_memory辅助标注，为对话情境下超越原始黄金证据的上下文利用提供支持。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increas- ingly deployed as long-horizon conversational agents, motivating growing interest in mem- ory systems. However, existing benchmarks primarily evaluate memory through QA-style probing rather than in-situ conversational usage. We introduce LOCOMO-CONV, a conversa- tional memory benchmark derived from Lo- CoMo with four query styles: dialog, implicit, counterfactual, and composed. Across five rep- resentative memory systems, we evaluate both retrieval recall and end-to-end response qual- ity. Our experiments show that conversational framing exposes substantial retrieval gaps over- looked by QA benchmarks, especially on im- plicit and composed queries, which multi-facet query rewriting narrows for raw-turn mem- ory but not abstractive memory. We further find that strong retrieval does not fully trans- late into response quality, and that implicit queries exhibit silent grounding, where mem- ory improves contextual grounding without ex- plicitly surfacing the gold fact. These results point to reasoning-based memory elaboration as a promising direction, and we release aux- iliary supportive_memory annotations captur- ing conversationally useful context beyond the original gold evidence.

</details>


### [146] [Pattern Over-Generalization of Knowledge Graph Embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03487)
*Junsik Kim,Kangil Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出PogRE方法，通过密集线性变换和复合操作改进关系表示，解决知识图谱嵌入中模式过泛化问题，在链接预测上超越现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGE模型能建模对称/反对称、逆、组合等推理模式，但存在模式过泛化缺陷：仅从单个模式实例学习的嵌入会将该模式不恰当地推广到所有相关实例，导致预测偏差。

Method: PogRE使用密集线性变换和复合操作来构造关系表示。理论分析表明，随着模式中三元组数量增加，线性变换使模式逐步泛化；当观察到d+1个线性无关实体（d为实体嵌入维度）后，该变换保证模式在所有相关实例上实现普适泛化。

Result: 在三个标准基准数据集上的实验显示，PogRE在链接预测任务上优于现有最先进KGE模型；同时经验结果验证其能有效缓解过泛化的负面影响。

Conclusion: PogRE通过理论保证和实证验证，为KGE中的模式过泛化问题提供了简单有效的解决方案，提升了链接预测性能。

Abstract: Knowledge graph embedding (KGE) demonstrates its effectiveness for predicting missing links in knowledge graphs (KGs) by projecting entities and relations into a low-dimensional vector space. It is crucial for KGE models to effectively capture inference patterns (patterns) inherent in KGs, such as symmetry/antisymmetry, inversion and composition. Although recent KGE models exhibit strong capabilities in modeling such diverse patterns, they suffer from inherent limitations stemming from pattern over-generalization, where embeddings learned from only a single pattern instance inevitably generalize that pattern to all related instances, i.e., generalize the pattern universally. To address this issue, we propose PogRE (Pattern Over-Generalization Robust Embedding), a simple but effective method that utilizes dense linear transformations and compound operations for relation representation. Our theoretical analysis demonstrates that a dense linear transformation allows a pattern to become progressively universal as more triples are observed in the pattern. Furthermore, after observing d+1 linearly independent entities (d+1 denotes the dimension of entity), the linear transformation guarantees universal generalization of the pattern across all related instances. Experimental results on three standard benchmark datasets show that PogRE outperforms existing state-of-the-art KGE models in link prediction. Moreover, our empirical results indicate that PogRE effectively addresses the negative impact of over-generalization.

</details>


### [147] [Building and Evaluating Fixed-Voice Thai TTS from Synthetic Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03502)
*Kunat Pipatanakul,Potsawee Manakul,Warit Sirichotedumrong,Sittipong Sripaisarnmongkol,Pakorn Nathong,Phatrasek Jirabovonvisut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探索了第三条TTS路线：利用大型声音克隆模型作为可编程数据源，将15秒的简短语音参考转化为训练自合成语音的紧凑固定音色学生模型，从而在低资源环境下实现无需参考音频的端到端设备端泰语TTS。所提出的82M参数模型Wayu-Paxa-TTS-Edge在多个指标上优于其教师模型OmniVoice，并逼近Gemini 3.1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低资源环境下部署TTS需在推理成本高的大型声音克隆模型与需特定说话人语料的紧凑固定音色系统之间取舍；作者希望研究第三条路线，即用大型克隆模型作为数据生成器，将短参考语音转化成紧凑固定音色学生模型，同时系统性地分析管线设计对最终模型的影响。

Method: 使用大型声音克隆模型合成大量语音数据，训练一个紧凑的固定音色学生模型；系统研究文本预处理、合成生成、质量过滤、拒绝采样、前端选择等管线环节对学生模型的影响；在泰语场景下处理词边界不明、声调、人名与外来词、数字读法、泰英代码混合等挑战；评估CER、挑战集关键词准确率、韵律停顿准确率、说话人相似度及语速。

Result: 所提出的82M参数模型Wayu-Paxa-TTS-Edge在挑战集关键词准确率上达到68.2%（为Gemini 3.1的85.5%），停顿精确率91.4%（超过其教师OmniVoice的89.9%，达到Gemini 3.1的94.8%）；同时，在泰语和英语上的CER分别为3.7%和1.1%，并且实现最低的停顿位置错误和词内停顿率。

Conclusion: 通过从大型教师模型合成数据来训练紧凑学生模型是低资源TTS的可行路线；该方法可在无参考音频情况下实现设备端泰语TTS，性能优于教师模型并接近更强的云端模型；作者开源了模型和评估框架以促进泰语TTS发展。

Abstract: In low-resource settings, deploying TTS typically requires choosing between a large voice-cloning model with costly inference or a compact fixed-voice system that requires a speaker-specific corpus. We study a third route: using a large voice-cloning model as a programmable data source to turn a short voice reference (e.g., 15 seconds) into a compact fixed-voice student trained entirely on synthetic speech. This setting makes pipeline design consequential: teacher errors become training targets, while filtering failed generations can reduce coverage of difficult texts. Thai further introduces challenges from ambiguous word boundaries, lexical tone, names and loanwords, numeric verbalization, and Thai-English code-switching. We study how text preparation, synthetic generation, quality filtering, rejection sampling, and frontend choices affect the resulting student, and where teacher limitations remain. We evaluate CER, Challenge-Set Keyword Accuracy, Prosody Pause Accuracy, speaker similarity, and speaking rate. The resulting 82M-parameter model, Wayu-Paxa-TTS-Edge, enables on-device Thai TTS without reference audio. It achieves 68.2% Challenge-Set Keyword Accuracy (85.5% of Gemini 3.1) and 91.4% pause precision, outperforming its OmniVoice teacher (89.9%) and reaching 94.8% of Gemini 3.1. It also achieves the lowest pause-placement error and intra-word pause rates among the three systems, and 3.7% and 1.1% CER on Thai and English, respectively. We open-source the model and evaluation framework for Thai TTS development.

</details>


### [148] [Lost in Reordering: Structural Sensitivity of Multilingual LLMs under Semantics-Preserving Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03511)
*Karthika Nhayakkat,Rajat Verma,Maharaj Brahma,Vetcha Gnana Mahesh,Maunendra Sankar Desarkar,Ganesh Ramakrishnan,Rohit Saluja*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多语言大语言模型（LLM）在语义保持不变的结构扰动下的稳健性，基于印地语和马拉雅拉姆语设计了两种语言学扰动（受限成分重排和主动-被动语态转换），构建了基准数据集IndicReStruct，并在六个先进LLM和多种提示策略下观察到数学推理性能显著下降，进一步通过错误分析和机械可解释性实验揭示实体-数量对齐中断和中间层对推理恢复的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM的多语言推理能力，但对其在语义等价但句法结构变化下的稳健性关注不足，尤其对语序相对自由的语言（如印地语、马拉雅拉姆语）。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统评估结构敏感性。

Method: 基于GSM8K构建两个变体数据集GSM8K-Reordered和GSM8K-Voice，分别施加受限成分重排和主动-被动语态转换，保持语义不变。评估六个LLM和多种提示策略，并进行定性错误分析和基于残差流激活修补的机械可解释性实验。

Result: 所有模型在结构扰动输入下数学推理性能一致显著下降；错误多源于实体-数量对齐中断；中间层对推理恢复贡献最大。

Conclusion: 当前多语言LLM对表面句法实现高度敏感，缺乏对结构不同但语义等价的输入的组合不变性，说明其推理并非基于稳健的语义表示。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) demonstrate strong multilingual reasoning performance, yet their robustness to semantics-preserving structural variation remains underexplored, particularly for relatively free word-order languages. We investigate the structural sensitivity of multilingual LLMs using two linguistically grounded perturbation settings in Hindi and Malayalam: constrained constituent reordering and active-passive voice transformation. We introduce a benchmark dataset IndicReStruct, with two variants, GSM8K-Reordered and GSM8K-Voice, constructed from GSM8K while preserving semantic meaning. Across six state-of-the-art LLMs and multiple prompting strategies, we observe consistent and significant degradation in mathematical reasoning performance under structurally perturbed inputs. To further understand these failures, we perform qualitative error analysis and mechanistic interpretability experiments using residual-stream activation patching. Our analyses show that reasoning failures frequently arise from disruptions in entity-quantity alignment and that intermediate transformer layers contribute most strongly toward reasoning restoration. Overall, our findings suggest that current multilingual LLMs remain highly sensitive to surface syntactic realization and lack robust compositional invariance under structurally different but semantically equivalent inputs.

</details>


### [149] [Language, Language Models, and What We're Talking About](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03577)
*Malvina Nissim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文以意大利语语言模型为证据，探讨语言模型作为技术产物与作为语言研究工具之间的本质差异。作者指出这些模型是在翻译数据、合成数据及人工精选数据上训练和特化而成，并在同样不自然的测试数据上评估，因此质疑它们是否真正是“意大利语模型”或“语言模型”。作者主张必须先区分两类不同的语言模型设计目标，才能回答“我们希望语言模型产出什么语言”这一核心问题，并认为答案可能是多样的，前景未必悲观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型常被当作纯粹的技术产物看待，但实际它们深受训练数据所承载的语言世界塑造。作者注意到意大利语模型是在翻译数据、合成数据和人工策展数据上训练的，又在同样不自然的测试数据上评估，这种实践引发了对模型本质（是否真的是意大利语模型/语言模型）以及NLP学科是否仍关注语言本身的根本质疑。

Method: 本文属于概念性/立场性论证而非实证研究。作者以意大利语语言模型作为案例证据，通过一系列递进的批判性提问（它们还是意大利语模型吗？它们还是语言模型吗？NLP还关心语言吗？）引导出核心问题，并提出一个二元区分框架：语言模型作为技术产品（technical products）与语言模型作为研究语言本身的工具（tools for studying language），以此作为回答“我们究竟想要语言模型产出什么语言”的先决条件。

Result: 作者论证了若不先厘清“技术产品”与“语言研究工具”两类语言模型之间的区别，就无法回答“我们想要什么样的语言产出”这一问题。基于这一区分，作者得出：答案可能是多样的，涉及的语言也可能是多样的，整体图景未必如人们担心的那样悲观。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁在语言模型设计与评价中重新确立对“语言”本身的关注，通过区分技术产品与研究工具两种定位，为语言模型的语言产出问题打开多元化的可能性，避免因当前数据与评测的“不自然性”而陷入悲观主义。

Abstract: Language models are commonly discussed as technical artefacts, but they are obviously shaped by the linguistic worlds conveyed by data during their training. Using Italian language models as evidence, I want to bring attention to the nature of the systems which result from training and specialising models on translated and synthetic data, and further curating them, and to the meaning of testing them on equally unnatural data. Are these eventually models of Italian? Are they models of language? Does NLP still care about language? These questions yield another, more concrete question: what language do we actually want language models to produce? I argue that this question cannot be answered if we do not first consider a clearer distinction between language models designed as technical products and language models designed as tools for studying language itself. The answers then might be diverse, the languages we are talking about might be diverse, and the picture might not be as pessimistic as we fear.

</details>


### [150] [How Far Can Synthetic Data Take Thai OCR?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03595)
*Kunat Pipatanakul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文旨在厘清合成OCR训练数据中哪些因素能有效迁移到真实泰语文档，据此构建了Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero——一个无需真实泰语页面OCR标签即可完成适配的泰语OCR模型。通过受控文档重建管线解耦来源域、页面上下文、字体、空间结构和字形变化等因素，发现非文本上下文影响甚微，而字体多样性、二维结构及真实手写字形能提升迁移效果，且源域匹配的效果取决于训练粒度。基于这些结论，作者用45,723张合成页面将0.9B参数的PaddleOCR-VL-1.6适配为Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero，使其在印刷页上的中位字符错误率从6.64%降到1.24%，手写从74.87%降到20.55%，并在五个评测集上全面超越Typhoon OCR v1 7B，证明纯合成训练具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 合成OCR监督数据能提供大规模、精确的标签，但"真实性"这一概念混淆了来源域、页面上下文、排版、空间结构与字形变化等多个因素，导致人们不清楚究竟是哪些属性真正决定了合成数据向真实文档的迁移效果。作者希望通过受控实验解耦这些因素，找出提升迁移性能的关键要素，为无需真实标签的泰语OCR模型构建提供理论依据。

Method: 采用受控的文档重建管线解耦并分别操控来源域、页面上下文、排版、空间结构和字形变化等因子；在页面级与裁剪级两种训练粒度下分别对印刷与手写泰语文档进行评测。基于实验结果，作者将0.9B参数的PaddleOCR-VL-1.6作为基座，在45,723张合成页面上适配出Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero。

Result: 实验表明非文本上下文几乎没有一致影响，而字体多样性、二维结构以及真实手写字形均能改善迁移。源域匹配的效果依赖训练粒度：页面级训练下，域内重建接近真实印刷监督（中位CER 1.82%对1.31%），而裁剪级训练下域内重建明显不如域外重建（15.59%对5.52%）。Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero相对基座将印刷页面中位CER从6.64%降至1.24%，手写从74.87%降至20.55%，并在全部五个评测集上优于Typhoon OCR v1 7B。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了合成OCR数据中真正影响向真实泰语文档迁移的因素，并证明纯合成训练即可取得竞争力：Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero不仅大幅降低印刷与手写场景的字符错误率，还能以更小模型胜过更大规模的Typhoon OCR v1 7B，验证了系统化合成数据设计在低资源稀缺语言OCR中的有效性。

Abstract: We investigate what makes synthetic OCR supervision transfer to real Thai documents and use the resulting insights to build Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero, a Thai OCR model adapted without OCR labels from real Thai document pages. Synthetic data provide exact labels at scale, but "realism" conflates source domain, page context, typography, spatial structure, and glyph variation. We disentangle these factors with a controlled document-reconstruction pipeline and evaluate each variant under page- and crop-level training on printed and handwritten Thai documents. Non-text context has little consistent effect, whereas typeface diversity, two-dimensional structure, and real handwriting glyphs improve transfer; moreover, source-domain matching depends on training granularity, with in-domain reconstruction approaching real printed supervision under page-level training (1.82% versus 1.31% median character error rate) but underperforming out-of-domain reconstruction under crop-level training (15.59% versus 5.52%). Guided by these findings, we adapt the 0.9B-parameter PaddleOCR-VL-1.6 into Wayu-Paxa-OCR-Zero using 45,723 synthetic pages: relative to its base checkpoint, it reduces median character error rate from 6.64% to 1.24% on printed pages and from 74.87% to 20.55% on handwriting and outperforms Typhoon OCR v1 7B on all five evaluation sets, showing that synthetic-only training can be competitive.

</details>


### [151] [KhatianDoc: A Human-Verified Benchmark Diagnosing Multimodal LLM Failure on Bengali Legal Land Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03597)
*Tasmiad Hasan,Arafat Zaman Ratul,Sarker Sadman Saalim,S. M. Shah Nawaz Hossain,Khan Raiyan Ibne Reza,Sumaiya Tabassum Nimi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 KhatianDoc，一个基于孟加拉国 107 份真实 RS Khatian 土地权属记录构建的四任务基准（符号识别、base-16 分数转十进制、结构化字段抽取、法律文档问答，含 1634 个 QA 对）。在六个多模态大模型、固定零样本协议下评估发现：39.3% 的分层 QA 类别所有模型零正确；算术任务上所有输出数字的模型均劣于常数均值基线（精确匹配与近似匹配分数一致，表明去相关而非近似）；并借助元评估修正了拒答计分 bug、标记了元数据指标的膨胀上界。结论是文档记录的不是性能差距，而是能力缺失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 孟加拉国的土地权属以 Ana-Ganda-Kora-Kranti-Til 这种 base-16 位置分数系统记录，使用专用 Unicode 字形，但无主流字体、无 OCR 管线或分词器覆盖。承载这些分数的手写 RS Khatian 记录是数百万地块的权威所有权凭证、民事诉讼常见对象，但此前没有任何基准检验机器能否读取它。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建流程：从孟加拉国 Munshiganj 的 Vumi（土地）办公室收集 107 份真实 RS Khatian 记录；设计四项任务（符号识别、base-16 转十进制、结构化字段抽取、法律文档 QA 的 1634 对）；ground truth 由人工转录并经土地法从业者核验至完全一致；通过位置 token 匿名化以保留多跳问题依赖的指称区分。评估协议：六个多模态 LLM（8B 到 72B+，开放与闭源），固定零样本协议；并审校自身指标，修正拒答计分 bug，将元数据指标标记为上界。

Result: 五个 QA 类别（占分层集合 39.3%）中所有模型均得到零正确答案；算术任务上每个输出数字的模型都比常数均值基线更差，且精确匹配与近似匹配分数完全重合，表明模型与真值去相关而非近似。修正拒答计分 bug 后重报了对照分数，并指出一个元数据指标因膨胀仅作为上界。

Conclusion: KhatianDoc 记录的不是模型性能差距，而是能力缺失——当前系统完全无法读取这类记录。基准配备经核验的 ground truth，供未来系统使用；代码、数据（含打码图像版本）已公开。该工作揭示了被主流 OCR/NLP 管线忽略的领域并设定了可复现的评测标准。

Abstract: Land ownership in Bangladesh is recorded in Ana-Ganda-Kora-Kranti-Til, a base-16 positional fraction system with dedicated Unicode glyphs, no mainstream font, and no coverage in any OCR pipeline or tokenizer. The handwritten records that carry these fractions, RS Khatians, are the authoritative title record for millions of parcels and a frequent subject of civil litigation, yet no benchmark has asked whether a machine can read one. We introduce KhatianDoc, a four-task benchmark built from 107 real RS Khatian records from the Vumi (land) Office of Munshiganj, Bangladesh: symbol recognition, base-16-to-decimal conversion, structured field extraction, and legal document question answering over 1,634 QA pairs. Ground truth was transcribed by hand, verified by a land-law practitioner to full agreement, and anonymized through positional tokens that keep the referential distinctions multi-hop questions depend on. We evaluate six multimodal LLMs (8B to 72B+, open and closed) under a fixed zero-shot protocol. Five QA categories, 39.3% of our stratified set, return zero correct answers from every model; on the arithmetic task, every model that emits a number does worse than a constant-mean baseline, with exact- and near-match scores coinciding: decorrelation, not approximation. Auditing our own metrics surfaced two artifacts in opposite directions: we correct a refusal-scoring bug and report the fixed scores beside the originals, and flag an inflated metadata metric as an upper bound. KhatianDoc documents not a performance gap but the absence of a capability, with verified ground truth for future systems. Code and data, with a redacted image release, are publicly available.

</details>


### [152] [Remember and Reweight: Enhancing Multi-Agent Debate with Experience Memory and Confidence Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03619)
*Xuanfa Jin,Zhijian Ma,Yongcheng Zeng,Xinyu Cui,Haifeng Zhang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对多智能体辩论（MAD）中的"共享误解"漏洞（即多数智能体初始陷于同一错误答案时，辩论反而放大错误），提出R²-MAD框架，通过为智能体配备历史辩论经验记忆，采用辩论状态感知的检索策略动态校准概念先验，并基于检索经验估算各智能体可靠性以分配置信权重来调节同伴影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MAD方法存在关键缺陷：当大多数智能体初始收敛于错误答案时，辩论会放大而非纠正错误（即共享误解）。已有方法主要处理同伴偏差（peer skew），却未解决智能体内在的有偏概念先验（biased concept priors），因此需要针对两类失败模式进行系统性干预。

Method: 提出R²-MAD（Remember and Reweight for Multi-Agent Debate）框架，包含两个互补机制：（1）辩论状态感知的检索策略——根据当前共识水平动态检索相关历史证据，以校准概念先验；（2）基于检索经验估算各智能体可靠性，生成置信权重以调节同伴影响。二者分别针对共享误解的两个失败模式进行干预。

Result: 在多种基准上，R²-MAD相较现有的单智能体与MAD基线取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 为多智能体辩论引入"记忆与再加权"机制，可有效缓解共享误解这一系统性弱点，从而提升大语言模型的推理能力。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate (MAD) improves the reasoning capabilities of large language models by having multiple agents iteratively refine their responses through discussion. However, MAD suffers from a critical vulnerability known as shared misconception: when a majority of agents initially converge on an incorrect answer, the debate process tends to amplify rather than correct the error. Existing methods primarily address peer skew but leave the agents' inherently biased concept priors unaddressed. To mitigate this systematic weakness, we propose R$^2$-MAD (Remember and Reweight for Multi-Agent Debate), a framework that equips agents with an experience memory accumulated from past debates. R$^2$-MAD intervenes on both failure modes through two complementary mechanisms: A debate-state-aware retrieval policy dynamically calibrates the concept prior by retrieving relevant historical evidence based on the current consensus level. Then these retrieved experiences provide a basis for estimating per-agent reliability, yielding confidence weights to modulate peer influence. Experiments on various benchmarks show that R$^2$-MAD achieves consistent improvements over existing single-agent and MAD baselines.

</details>


### [153] [</think> Doesn't Stop Reasoning: Analysis of Spurious CoT Termination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03633)
*Seunghee Koh,Sungjae Choi,Minchan Kwon,Sunghyun Baek,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究训练免早期的早退出方法在大型推理模型（LRM）中注入端思维令牌（EoT）时发现的“虚假CoT终止”现象：注入的EoT并不总能触发干净的从推理到回答的转换，模型可能在回答阶段前继续生成推理类内容并再次生成EoT。作者提出退出令牌注意力偏置（EAB）方法，通过增强对注入EoT的注意力来抑制此现象，实验表明该方法在四个LRM、五个基准和两种早退出方法上均有效减少虚假终止与回答阶段长度，揭示了仅靠外部匹配显式思维块格式来控制LRM的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT推理虽能提升LRM在复杂任务上的表现，但常产生冗长冗余的推理轨迹。训练免早期的早退出方法通过在中间点注入EoT令牌来提前结束推理，但作者发现注入的EoT并不总能干净地触发推理到回答的转换，存在推理类内容延续进入回答阶段的“虚假CoT终止”现象，需要深入理解其成因并加以改进。

Method: 作者系统分析注入EoT后的生成行为，发现模型可能在再次生成EoT前已开始回答阶段生成，且该跨度与早退出节省的推理令牌数成比例，呈现持续的推理行为。据此提出假设：模型对注入EoT的注意力不足导致了虚假CoT终止。为验证该假设，提出退出令牌注意力偏置（EAB）方法，通过增加模型对注入EoT的注意力来纠正推理-回答转换。

Result: 在四个LRM、五个基准和两种早退出方法上的实验表明，增加对注入EoT的注意力可减少虚假CoT终止现象，并缩短回答阶段长度，验证了注意力不足假设的正确性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了通过外部匹配LRM显式思维块格式来控制其行为的局限性：插入EoT令牌虽符合格式要求，但仅靠这一操作并不能保证模型按预期完成推理到回答的转换。这为理解和改进基于令牌注入的早退出控制策略提供了重要启示。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning improves large reasoning models (LRMs) on complex tasks but often produces long, redundant traces. Recent training-free early-exit methods shorten these traces by choosing an intermediate point to stop reasoning. We study one such strategy that injects an end-of-think token (EoT, </think>) at this point to trigger the reasoning-to-answering transition, and find that the injected EoT does not always induce a clean answering phase. Answering-phase generation can continue before the model regenerates another EoT, with the span preceding this regenerated EoT scaling with the reasoning tokens saved by early exit and exhibiting continued reasoning behavior. We call this spurious CoT termination, where reasoning-like generation continues into the answering phase. We hypothesize that insufficient attention to the injected EoT contributes to spurious CoT termination and probe this hypothesis with Exit-token Attention Biasing (EAB). Across four LRMs, five benchmarks, and two early-exit methods, increasing attention to the injected EoT reduces spurious CoT termination and answering-phase length. These results reveal a limitation of controlling LRMs by externally matching their explicit think-block format. Inserting the EoT token conforms to this format but does not by itself guarantee the intended reasoning-to-answering transition. Our code is available at https://github.com/Seunghee-Koh/Spurious-CoT-Termination.

</details>


### [154] [The Impact of Synthetic Data Augmentation on Discourse-Pragmatic Function Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03652)
*Sara Sorahi,Kevin Tang,Reza Kazemian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究合成数据增强中，生成样本在表示空间中的几何位置（相对真实训练数据）对话语语用功能分类性能的影响，发现核心邻近样本带来最大宏F提升，而距离平衡混合样本获得最高准确率，增强仅移动决策边界而不改善概率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据增强多关注生成样本的数量和多样性，忽视了其在表示空间中与真实训练数据的几何关系。针对语用语用功能分类这类数据稀疏为结构性特征的任务，需要探究合成样本的空间位置对分类性能的影响。

Method: 使用410条来自BNC的英语单词look人工标注样本，涵盖四种功能（注意力信号、指令、话语标记、感叹词）。用Llama 3.1生成合成训练样本，并按其在RoBERTa嵌入空间中与真实训练数据的余弦距离进行划分。在保持增强数量恒定的条件下，设计六种训练条件，对比合成样本相对于经验决策边界的不同放置位置。

Result: 所有增强条件在宏F和准确率上均优于仅使用真实数据的基线，但核心邻近样本（NEAR）带来最大宏F提升（0.113），距离平衡混合样本获得最高准确率（0.748）。没有任何条件提升AUC，表明增强仅移动了决策边界而非改善底层概率估计。

Conclusion: 合成样本在表示空间中的位置与其数量同样重要，这一发现对低资源语用分类具有更广泛的启示。

Abstract: Synthetic data augmentation has become a common strategy for addressing class imbalance in NLP, but most approaches focus on the quantity and diversity of generated examples rather than their geometric relationship to real training data. We investigate this question in the context of discourse pragmatic function classification, a task where data sparsity is a structural feature rather than a collection artefact. Using 410 manually annotated instances of the English word look drawn from the British National Corpus, spanning four functions: Attention Signal, Directive, Discourse Marker, and Interjection. We generate synthetic training examples with Llama 3.1 and partition them by their cosine distance from real training data in RoBERTa embedding space. We compare six training conditions that differ in the placement of synthetic examples relative to the empirical decision boundary, while holding augmentation quantity constant across conditions. All augmented conditions improve macro F and accuracy over the real only baseline, but core proximal examples (NEAR) yield the largest gains in macro F (0.113), while a distance balanced mix achieves the highest accuracy (0.748). No condition improves AUC, indicating that augmentation shifts the decision boundary rather than improving the model's underlying probability estimates. These findings suggest that where synthetic examples land in representation space matters as much as how many are generated, with implications for low resource pragmatic classification more broadly.

</details>


### [155] [Enhancing Financial Question Answering: A Novel Benchmark Dataset of Banks' financial statements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03654)
*Arianna Miola,Bruno Spaccavento,Lorenzo Silotto,Marco Bianchetti,Luca Cagliero*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出FinRAG-QA基准数据集，包含999个专家问题，覆盖24家欧美银行2019-2023年报与Pillar3报告，文档平均19.8万字，远超现有金融QA资源。评测多阶段RAG管道：上下文分块增强+检索优化嵌入将NDCG@10从0.322提升至0.710；在正确检索条件下，推理优化生成器将答案准确率从44.6%提升至79.0%（+34.4个百分点），但延迟增至约20倍。还发现交叉编码器重排在首阶段检索已强时反而降低性能，单块上下文优于更大上下文。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行财务报告复杂、冗长、术语密集且跨机构格式不一致，现有金融QA基准多聚焦美国单一机构，缺少跨机构、长文档检索评测，难以反映实际分析场景。

Method: 构建FinRAG-QA数据集（999个问题，10个标准化指标，209份报告，跨24家欧美银行）；设计多阶段RAG管道并逐步隔离各组件贡献；对比上下文分块、嵌入模型、重排序、生成器及上下文大小的影响。

Result: 上下文分块与检索优化嵌入使NDCG@10从0.322升至0.710；在已检索到正确答案的条件下，推理优化生成器将准确率从44.6%提升至79.0%（+34.4个百分点），但延迟约增20倍；交叉编码器重排在强首段检索时有害；单块上下文优于大上下文。

Conclusion: 跨机构长文档金融QA的检索质量受分块与嵌入影响最大，生成阶段平衡准确率与延迟。建议避免在强检索后使用重排，并采用紧凑上下文以提升生成效果。

Abstract: The comparative analysis of banks' financial statements poses significant challenges for automated question answering systems due to their complexity, substantial length, technical language, and inhomogeneity of both textual and numerical content across different jurisdictions and institutions. We introduce FinRAG-QA, a novel benchmark dataset for financial question answering, which comprises 999 practitioner-curated questions on 10 standardised indicators, grounded in 209 annual and Pillar 3 reports from 24 major European and U.S. banks spanning 2019-2023. Unlike prior financial QA benchmarks, which centre on U.S. filings and single-institution analysis, FinRAG-QA targets cross-institutional retrieval over documents averaging 198k words, longer than any existing financial QA resource. On this benchmark we evaluate a multi-stage RAG pipeline and isolate the contribution of each component. Contextual chunk enrichment combined with a retrieval-optimised embedding model raises NDCG@10 from 0.322 to 0.710; conditional on the ground truth being retrieved, a reasoning-optimised generator raises answer accuracy from 44.6% to 79.0% (+34.4 percentage points), at roughly 20x the generation latency. We further show that cross-encoder reranking degrades retrieval when the first-stage ranking is already strong, and that a single top-ranked chunk outperforms larger contexts at generation time. Experiments were run in late 2024-early 2025 with the models available at that time.

</details>


### [156] [A Circuit for Plural Reference: How LLMs Represent and Retrieve Singular and Plural Entities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03687)
*Anh Danh,Rick Nouwen,Massimo Poesio*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过机械可解释性和因果干预，揭示了LLM在复数指代中表示和检索实体的注意力头机制，并发现其与人类偏好相似。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心参照解析是上下文推理的关键任务，但LLM如何处理复数指代的内部机制尚不明确，需要深入理解以改进模型性能。

Method: 结合机械可解释性和注意力模式分析，对LLM预测复数代词的过程进行因果干预，识别参与表示、识别和传递核心参照信息的注意力头。

Result: 发现特定注意力头分别负责表示输入中的核心参照信息、识别形成复数参照的实体，以及将信息传递给选择先行词和预测代词的组件；此外，LLM在语义相似且由"and"连接的实体上更倾向使用复数代词。

Conclusion: LLM的复数指代机制在结构上与人类认知偏好一致，为提高上下文推理的可解释性和模型设计提供了新见解。

Abstract: Coreference resolution is an important task in contextual reasoning. In this paper, we investigate the mechanism for representing and retrieving singular and plural entities for plural reference. We use a combination of mechanistic interpretability and attention pattern analysis to study the process in which LLMs predict a pronoun to refer back to previously mentioned entities. Using a range of causal intervention techniques, we find a set of attention heads that are responsible for (1) representing coreference information in the input, (2) identifying entities that form a plural reference, (3) transferring the information to the component that is responsible for selecting the antecedents and predicting the pronoun. We also find that LLMs align with humans in preference for plural pronoun. Specifically, entities in a plural construction are more likely to be referred to as a plural entity if they are ontologically similar and are linked by the conjunction "and".

</details>


### [157] [Opening mind by opening architecture: analysis strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03719)
*Francesco Vitucci,Giuseppe Silvi,Daniele Giuseppe Annese,Francesco Scagliola,Anthony Di Furia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了电子音乐作曲中数字信号处理环境的发展困境：商业数字工具的普及导致封闭架构音频处理器模型占据主导，虽然功能强大，但其内部处理过程和交互系统如同"黑箱"般不可窥探，造成研究与发展环境的逐步丧失。作者主张通过文献视角重新审视这些环境，以重建科学方法和研究态度，并以Manfred Schroeder的历史混响器为案例，阐述了构建分析评估工具和实际实现的过程，为有意识的研究路径奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出，随着数字市场工具日益普及，特定开发和研究环境逐渐流失，封闭架构音频处理器模型（"黑箱"）日益主导电子音乐作曲的数值信号处理领域。该模型虽然强大，能够描述输出数据的感知特征，却牺牲了对内部过程及交互系统的理解。作者认为这种损失必须正视——任何数字信号处理技术都蕴含着一部关于实现、历史与美学后果的"故事"，需要被重新发现和理解，以恢复科学研究的方法与态度。

Method: 本文采用文献视角（"通过文学之镜"）作为方法论切入点，主张通过文献重新建立对信号处理环境的感知与科学化认知。具体而言，以Manfred Schroeder的历史混响器为案例研究，系统展示了构建分析性评估工具的过程，以及相应的实际（实践）实现手段，旨在形成一条有意识的研究路径，将数字信号处理技术从其封闭的"黑箱"状态中解放出来，还原其包含的社会、历史与技术多义层次。

Result: 文中以Schroeder历史混响器为例，论证了如何通过文献研究重新接触并解读封闭的信号处理环境，成功构建了分析评估工具并完成实际实现。这一过程表明，即便面对"黑箱"式的强势工具，通过文献视角重建科学方法和研究态度，依然能够还原技术实现的历史脉络及其美学后果，使研究者在认知层面重新获得对这些环境的主导权。

Conclusion: 作者得出核心结论：数字信号处理器不应被视为不可窥探的黑箱，而应被理解为承载着实现历史、社会背景与美学后果的叙事文本。只有通过文献视角加以审视，重建科学方法，才能恢复对封闭架构模型内部过程的意识，从而在电子音乐作曲的数值信号处理研究中建立一条自觉、可持续的研究路径。Schroeder混响器案例展示了这条路径的具体可行性。

Abstract: In numerical signal processing for electroacoustic composition, the progressive loss of specific development and research environments caused by the increasing use of digital market tools has favoured the dominance of the closed-architecture audio processor model. This model, while powerful, envisions the possibility of describing output data about its perceived characteristics, but at the cost of ignoring its internal process and interacting systems, which become complex, powerful environments but closed in an inscrutable black box, a loss we must consider. Any digital signal processing technique tells a story. Just as the words of a language incorporate social, historical and technical polysemic layers, a signal processor has its own story of implementation, a gradual technological achievement with its inevitable aesthetic consequences. Through the looking-glass of literature, one can access those environments with renewed awareness by reestablishing a scientific method and an attitude to research. In this specific case, starting from the case study of Manfred Schroeder's historical reverbs, we illustrate the process of building analytical evaluation tools, as well as practical implementation, at the basis of a conscious study path.

</details>


### [158] [Beyond BLEU: A Case for Redefining Sign Language Translation Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03734)
*Oline Ranum,Edward Fish,Simon Hadfield,Richard Bowden*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出口语语言翻译的标准指标BLEU-4并不适用于手语翻译评估，因为多模态、低资源的SLT环境使模型可依赖虚假相关和口语先验而非学习真正的符号表示。作者在Phoenix-2014T和CSL-Daily上对六个SLT模型验证了时空理解与BLEU-4之间的关系，证明BLEU-4增益不必然代表手语理解提升。文章提出基于开放权重LLM的问答协议作为替代指标，该协议更能对齐人工排序，且对释义的鲁棒性比BLEU-4高六到七倍。应用该协议后，五个无词汇标注系统在Phoenix-2014T上性能接近，而有词汇监督系统高出9.3分，这一差异在BLEU-4下不可见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BLEU-4作为手语翻译(SLT)的标准评估指标存在根本缺陷：口语语言指标无法充分反映手语能力，且SLT的多模态、低资源特性使模型倾向于利用虚假相关和口语先验而非学习更强的符号表示，导致评估结果失真。

Method: 在Phoenix-2014T和CSL-Daily上对六个SLT模型评估时空理解与BLEU-4的关系；引入源于语言学习评估的开放权重LLM问答(QA)协议，测量显著内容保留(salient content preservation)，并将其与BLEU-4在人工排序对齐、释义不变性和训练-测试重叠鲁棒性等方面进行对比。

Result: BLEU-4的提升不能单独作为手语理解增强的证据；所提QA协议与人类评分更为一致，释义不变性比BLEU-4高出六至七倍；在Phoenix-2014T上，五个无词汇(gloss-free)系统性能彼此在噪声范围内，而有词汇监督(gloss-supervised)系统高出9.3分，此差距在BLEU-4下被掩盖。

Conclusion: BLEU-4会掩盖SLT模型间真实能力差异；所提出的内容保留QA协议更准确、更稳健地反映手语内容传递能力，并揭示出词汇监督确实带来显著优势，这一结论为SLT评估提供了更可靠的替代方案。

Abstract: BLEU-4 is the standard metric for evaluating sign language translation (SLT), but spoken-language metrics may not adequately reflect sign language proficiency. The multimodal, low-resource context of SLT allows models to exploit spurious correlations and spoken-language priors, rather than learning stronger sign representations. In this paper, we evaluate the relationship between spatio-temporal understanding and BLEU-4 across six SLT models on Phoenix-2014T and CSL-Daily, showing that gains in BLEU-4 are not on their own evidence of better sign language understanding. This work introduces an alternative inspired by language-learning assessment, using an open-weight-LLM QA protocol that measures salient content preservation. It aligns more closely with human rankings and is six to seven times more paraphrase-invariant than BLEU-4. Applied to SLT, this protocol targets content transfer, is more robust to train-test overlap, and gives a different picture of the field: the five gloss-free systems are largely within noise of one another on Phoenix-2014T, while the gloss-supervised system stands 9.3 points higher, a gap invisible to BLEU-4.

</details>


### [159] [Typological Feature Prediction with Large Language Models: An In-Context Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03775)
*Qianwen Wang,York Hay Ng,Aditya Khan,En-Shiun Annie Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究利用大语言模型（LLMs）通过上下文学习预测语言类型学特征。研究发现零样本提示效果不足，但当提供来自URIEL+和Glottolog的谱系与地理邻居证据时，LLMs显著优于所有基线方法，且不会对低资源语言造成不利影响；同时大多数LLM推理与所提供证据一致，向可解释的类型学特征预测迈出了一步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 类型学特征广泛用于多语言NLP，预测缺失值具有下游效用。然而现有预测方法缺乏可解释性的预测依据，且其在不同资源水平和特征类型下的性能仍未被充分探索。这促使研究者利用LLMs的元语言学推理能力和提供理性依据的能力来改进类型学特征预测。

Method: 采用基于URIEL+和Glottolog语言数据的上下文学习（in-context learning）方法，向LLM提供谱系和地理邻居证据进行类型学特征预测，并与零样本提示及其他基线方法进行对比。

Result: 零样本提示效果不足；但在提供谱系和地理邻居证据后，LLMs大幅超越所有基线方法，且未对低资源语言造成不利影响；大多数LLM给出的推理与所提供证据一致。

Conclusion: 基于邻居证据的上下文学习能显著提升LLMs的类型学特征预测性能，兼具公平性（不损害低资源语言）和可解释性（理性与证据一致），为可解释类型学特征预测提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Typological features are widely used in multilingual NLP, and the prediction of such features holds downstream utility. However, existing methods to predict missing values lack interpretable justifications for predictions, while their performance across resource levels and feature types remains underexplored. Given LLMs' abilities in meta-linguistic reasoning and in providing rationales, we investigate LLMs' performance in typological feature prediction via an in-context learning approach with linguistic data from URIEL+ and Glottolog. We find that zero-shot prompting is insufficient, but when given phylogenetic and geographic neighbour evidence, LLMs substantially outperform all baselines without disadvantaging low-resource languages. We further find that most LLM rationales are consistent with the provided evidence, offering a step toward explainable typological feature prediction.

</details>


### [160] [IndicSafeEval: Safety Robustness of Large Language Models under Multilingual Persuasive Jailbreak Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03781)
*Saikat Mondal,Mamta,Deeksha Varshney,Oana Cocarascu,Asif Ekbal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IndicSafeEval，一个针对印度语言的基于劝说的越狱评估框架。该基准结合了十个安全关键内容类别、六种人类劝说策略和四种印度语言（印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语和旁遮普语），共生成7,200个对抗性提示。通过对多个开源LLM进行系统性黑盒评估，研究发现模型的安全性行为在不同语言和提示风格间存在显著差异，且不同风险类别的脆弱程度不同，揭示了当前以英语为中心的安全评估的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型越来越多地应用于多语言场景，但其安全性评估仍主要基于英语。这限制了对对齐失败在低资源和文化多样语言中如何表现的理解，从而需要多语言且感知劝说的安全性基准框架。

Method: 构建IndicSafeEval基准：将10个安全关键内容类别与6种人类劝说策略相结合，覆盖印地语、孟加拉语、马拉地语和旁遮普语四种语言，生成7,200个对抗性提示。随后对多个开源LLM进行系统性黑盒评估，分析安全行为在不同语言、劝说策略和风险类别间如何变化。

Result: 模型在不同语言和提示风格下表现出的安全性并不一致；安全性能强烈依赖于所使用的语言以及请求中劝说线索的表述方式。此外，不同风险类别表现出不同的脆弱程度，某些类型的有害内容对基于劝说的越狱攻击显著更易受影响。

Conclusion: 当前以英语为中心的安全评估存在重要局限，需要多语言且感知劝说的基准测试框架，以更准确地评估真实场景下的LLM安全性。实现代码已在GitHub上公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in multilingual settings, yet their safety is still evaluated primarily in English. This limits our understanding of how alignment failures manifest in low-resource and culturally diverse languages. We introduce IndicSafeEval, a persuasion-based jailbreak evaluation framework for Indian languages. Our benchmark combines ten safety critical content categories with six human-like persuasive strategies across four different Indian languages, such as Hindi, Bengali, Marathi and Punjabi, resulting in 7,200 adversarial prompts. We conduct a systematic black-box evaluation of several open-source LLMs to examine how their safety behaviour varies across languages, persuasion strategies, and risk categories. Our analysis shows that the model does not behave equally safely across all languages and prompt styles. Instead, safety performance depends strongly on both the languages used and the way a request is phrased using persuasive cues. We further observe that different risk categories exhibit different levels of vulnerability, with some types of harmful content being significantly more susceptible to persuasion-based jailbreaks than others. These findings reveal important limitations of current safety evaluations, which are largely English-centric, and underscore the need for multilingual and persuasion-aware benchmarking frameworks to more accurately assess real-world LLM safety. Our implementation is available at https://github.com/MonSaikat/IndicSafeEval. Warning: this paper contains example data that may be offensive or harmful.

</details>


### [161] [Evaluating Criterion-Conditioned Behaviour of Large Language Models in Content Moderation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03814)
*Danting Zhang,Bei Peng,Robert Loftin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 大型语言模型（LLM）在标准内容审核基准上表现出色，但这些基准常将多个审核标准聚合为单一标签，掩盖了模型是否真正具备按标准解耦并可靠应用各标准的能力。本文提出DECO（内容诊断性评估）——一种与标准无关的内容分解方法，实现可控的、标准级别的评估，并引入成对评估来比较同一输入在不同标准下的模型输出。在四个审核数据集和四个LLM上，研究发现：较强的整体基准表现可能掩盖标准层面上的显著失败，当正确决策取决于具体内容方面而非整体有害性时，模型最为吃力。这揭示了当前内容审核基准的关键局限，呼吁开发显式测量标准条件行为的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有内容审核基准将多个审核标准聚合为单一标签，无法判断模型是否能解耦不同标准并可靠地逐一应用。作者旨在探究LLM是否展现标准条件化（criterion-conditioned）行为，即能否依据不同审核标准对同一内容作出差异化的正确判断。

Method: 提出DECO（Diagnostic Evaluation of COntent），一种内容的标准无关因子分解方法，用于受控的、标准级别的评估；引入成对评估方法，比较同一输入在不同标准下的模型输出。在四个内容审核数据集和四个LLM上开展实验。

Result: 较强的聚合基准性能可能在标准级别掩盖显著失败。模型在正确决策依赖于评估内容的具体特定方面（而非整体有害性）时表现最差，即标准条件化能力不足。

Conclusion: 当前内容审核基准存在关键局限：聚合标签上的优异表现不足以证明LLM能依据单个审核标准可靠评估内容。呼吁开发显式衡量标准条件性行为的评估方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate strong performance on standard content moderation benchmarks. However, these benchmarks often aggregate multiple moderation criteria into a single label, making it unclear whether models can disentangle them and reliably apply each criterion when making decisions. To study whether LLMs exhibit criterion-conditioned behaviour, we introduce Diagnostic Evaluation of COntent (DECO), a criterion-independent factorisation of content that enables controlled, criterion-level evaluation. We also introduce pairwise evaluation to compare model outputs across different criteria for the same input. Across four moderation datasets and four LLMs, we find that strong benchmark performance can hide substantial failures at the criterion level. Models struggle most when correct decisions depend not on overall harmfulness, but on the specific aspect of the content that the criterion requires them to assess. Our results highlight a key limitation of current content moderation benchmarks: strong performance on aggregated labels does not provide sufficient evidence that LLMs can reliably evaluate content with respect to individual moderation criteria. These findings call for the development of evaluation methods that explicitly measure criterion-conditioned behaviour.

</details>


### [162] [Beyond Shallow Alignment: How Post-Training Methods Determine Refusal Circuits And Steering Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03887)
*Hoang Cuong Nguyen,Mark Dras,Usman Naseem*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对比了三种后训练方法（监督微调、推理增强微调、偏好优化ORPO）在三类不同架构模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Gemma-2-9B、Qwen3-8B）上如何影响模型内部拒绝机制的构建。研究发现训练方法（而非仅数据）会重塑拒绝计算方式：推理增强训练在所有模型上产生独特的拒绝计算模式，而架构则独立影响内部结构和可引导性。最重要的是，没有任何一种方法能同时满足理想安全对齐的三个属性：拒绝不集中于脆弱组件、安全增益不损害通用能力、以及可通过小型定向编辑纠正安全行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前对齐方法（如安全微调）被视为应对有害请求的可靠防御，但人们对训练方法如何从机制层面塑造模型内部的拒绝计算知之甚少。作者希望超越浅层对齐的表象，深入探究不同后训练方法在模型内部如何真正实现拒绝，以警示当前后训练方法并非已解决的、可靠的防御手段。

Method: 作者跨三个架构不同的模型（Llama-3.1-8B、Gemma-2-9B、Qwen3-8B）系统对比三种后训练方法：监督微调（SFT）、推理增强微调（在推理链上训练以证明安全决策）以及偏好优化（ORPO）。通过可解释性分析在模型内部层面考察拒绝机制如何被计算，并评估安全增益的鲁棒性、对通用能力的代价以及通过小规模定向编辑进行纠错的可能性。

Result: 训练方法（而非仅仅数据）重塑了拒绝的内部计算方式：推理增强训练在全部三个模型中一致地产生一种独特的拒绝计算类型；同时架构独立影响内部结构和拒绝可被可靠引导的程度。关键发现是：没有任何一种方法能同时满足理想安全的三个属性——拒绝不集中脆弱组件、安全增益不损失通用能力、以及行为可通过小型定向编辑被纠正。这些属性之间存在权衡或取舍。

Conclusion: 作者警告不应将当前后训练方法视为已解决的、可靠的安全防御，尤其在安全关键应用中。研究揭示了训练方法与模型架构在塑造拒绝机制上的交互作用，并指出理想安全对齐属性难以兼得，因此需要进一步的机制层面理解和更稳健的安全训练方法。

Abstract: How do the methods used to train language models to refuse harmful requests shape how that refusal actually works inside the model? We compare three post-training methods - supervised fine-tuning, reasoning-augmented fine-tuning (training on reasoning chains that justify a safety decision), and preference optimization (ORPO) - across three architecturally distinct models (Llama-3.1-8B, Gemma-2-9B, Qwen3-8B). We find that training method, not just data, reshapes how refusal is computed internally: reasoning-augmented training consistently produces a distinct kind of refusal computation, visible across all three models, while architecture independently shapes internal structure and how reliably refusal can be steered. Most importantly, no method we study achieves all three properties we would want from safe alignment at once: refusal that isn't concentrated in a few fragile components, safety gains that don't cost general capability, and safety behavior correctable through small, targeted edits. We caution against treating current post-training methods as a solved, reliable defense, especially for security-critical use. Code and models are available in https://github.com/hoangcuongnguyen2001/Beyond-Shallow-Alignment.

</details>


### [163] [CROCODIL: Cross-Model Code Editing with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03894)
*Linghan Zhong,Aditya Thimmaiah,Jayanth Srinivasa,Milos Gligoric,Junyi Jessy Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究了不同大语言模型（LLMs）在编辑由其他模型生成的"外来代码"时的行为差异，发现模型倾向于做更多甚至过度的修改。为此提出了CROCODIL（跨模型代码编辑框架），这是一个后训练框架，通过相似性奖励惩罚过大改动、执行奖励评估构建和测试成功，用二者的乘积来引导策略在保持任务成功率的同时减小编辑规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发团队常使用多个LLM助手，不同开发者偏爱不同模型，同一开发者也可能在不同会话间切换模型，导致一个模型生成的代码常被另一个模型编辑。由于各LLM训练数据不同、风格偏好各异，需要探究模型编辑另一种风格的外来代码时是否表现出不同行为，以及如何减少过度编辑。

Method: 提出CROCODIL后训练框架，由两个奖励组成：相似性奖励用于惩罚过大改动，执行奖励基于构建和测试成功来评分。通过这两个奖励的乘积引导策略在保持编辑任务成功率的同时减小编辑规模。

Result: 研究发现模型在编辑外来代码时往往做更多甚至过度的编辑；CROCODIL能够在保持功能正确性的前提下减少过度编辑。

Conclusion: CROCODIL有效解决了跨模型代码编辑中过度修改的问题，在降低编辑规模的同时不牺牲任务成功率，且已开源供社区使用。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have become ubiquitous tools for code generation and editing. However, development teams often use multiple LLM assistants. Different developers may prefer different models, and individual developers may switch between models across different coding sessions. Because of this, the edits any one model makes are frequently applied to foreign code originally generated by another model. These LLMs are often trained on different datasets, and as a result have different stylistic preferences. Do LLMs behave differently when they edit foreign code originally written by a different LLM with a different coding style? We find that models tend to make more, and often excessive, edits on foreign code. We introduce CROCODIL (Cross-model Code Editing with LLMs), a post-training framework for reducing excessive edits while preserving functional correctness. CROCODIL's similarity reward penalizes large changes, while its execution reward scores build and test success. We use the product of these two rewards to encourage the policy to decrease the edit size without decreasing the edit task success rate. CROCODIL is available at https://github.com/EngineeringSoftware/Crocodil.

</details>


### [164] [RuleMem: Active Rule Memory for Long-Term Conversational Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03915)
*Xingyuan Zeng,Zuohan Wu,Quanming Yao,Yue Wang,Wei Liu,Libin Zheng,Jiuke Wang,Jian Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 RuleMem，一种基于规则的记忆框架，通过从历史对话中归纳可复用的逻辑规则，以主动方式引导证据检索和推理，解决了现有记忆机制被动存储导致语义缺口与推理不可靠的问题。在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval_s* 两个长期对话基准上，RuleMem 在 LoCoMo 上与 14 个基线对比取得了最高准确率，超过基线平均值 27.47 分（相对提升 54.3%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期对话中的问答智能体需要在海量且时间分散的对话历史之上进行推理。现有记忆机制主要把过去信息当作被动存储的事实，导致语义缺口和不可靠的推理，难以检索语义距离较远的证据。因此需要一种能够主动利用历史信息进行推理的记忆机制。

Method: RuleMem 从对话中构建自然语言的 Horn 子句（Horn clauses），并通过规则困惑度一致性（Rule Perplexity Consistency, RPC）机制进行验证。这些归纳出的规则既能用于检索语义上相距较远的证据，又能为答案生成提供显式的逻辑结构。整体框架包含规则归纳、规则验证和规则驱动的证据检索与推理三个环节。

Result: RuleMem 在 LoCoMo 基准上与 14 个基线系统进行严格对比，取得了最高准确率，超过基线平均值 27.47 分，相对提升 54.3%；同时在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval_s* 两个长期对话基准上进行了全面评估。

Conclusion: RuleMem 通过将历史互动归纳为可复用的逻辑规则，主动引导证据检索与推理，有效弥补了被动记忆机制的语义缺口，在长期对话问答任务上取得了显著的性能优势，验证了基于规则的记忆框架的可行性。

Abstract: Question answering agents in long-term conversations must reason over massive, temporally dispersed dialogue histories. However, existing memory mechanisms primarily treat past information as \textit{passively} stored facts, leading to semantic gaps and unreliable reasoning. To address this limitation, we propose RuleMem, a rule-based memory framework that induces reusable logical rules from historical interactions to \textit{actively} guide both evidence retrieval and reasoning. Specifically, RuleMem constructs natural-language Horn clauses from conversations and validates them via a Rule Perplexity Consistency (RPC) mechanism. These induced rules enable the retrieval of semantically distant evidence while providing an explicit logical structure for answer generation. We conducted a comprehensive evaluation of RuleMem on two long-term conversational benchmarks, LoCoMo and LongMemEval_s*. In a rigorous comparison against 14 baselines on LoCoMo, RuleMem achieved the highest accuracy, exceeding the baseline average by 27.47 points (a 54.3% relative improvement).

</details>


### [165] [Fixed Suffix Dependency Ratio: Quantifying the Dual-Track Mechanism of Gender Assignment in Latvian Loanwords](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03930)
*Yelingyun Zhang,Atis Kapenieks,Marina Platonova*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"固定后缀依赖比"(FSDR)这一量化指标，用以区分借词性别指派中的形态锚定与自由选择。通过对1,832个拉脱维亚语名词词目类型的分析，发现阴性借词显著更依赖固定派生后缀，而阳性借词更集中于自由选择区域，表明阳性默认性别在语言接触中被激活并强化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究反复观察到英语借词在不同接受语中倾向于聚集于阳性性别，但这一模式来源难以确定，因为固定的形态规则与默认指派经常被混合分析。作者旨在解开这一混杂因素，将形态锚定效应与自由选择过程区分开来，从而揭示借词性别分布的深层机制。

Method: 作者提出固定后缀依赖比(FSDR)来量化不同性别对固定派生后缀的依赖程度。通过考察1,832个拉脱维亚语名词词目类型，区分形态锚定与自由选择两类分配机制，并比较不同性别间的FSDR不对称性，同时考察其借词特异性和历时变化。

Result: 结果揭示了借词系统内部显著的FSDR不对称性：阴性借词对固定派生后缀的依赖显著更高，而阳性借词更集中在自由选择区域。该模式具有借词特异性（非借词不具备），且在当代用法中更为显著。

Conclusion: FSDR为检验默认性别提供了量化框架，表明阳性默认性别在语言接触情境下能够被激活并得到强化，从而解释了英语借词在多种接受语中聚集于阳性性别的普遍倾向。
