Abstract: Existing research has repeatedly observed the tendency for English loanwords to cluster in the masculine gender across different recipient languages, yet the origin of this pattern remains difficult to determine, as fixed morphological rules and default assignments are frequently analysed together. This study proposes the Fixed Suffix Dependency Ratio (FSDR) to quantify the degree of reliance on fixed derivational suffixes across different genders, and to distinguish between morphological anchoring and free-choice in distribution. By examining 1,832 Latvian noun lemma types, the results reveal a significant FSDR asymmetry within the loanword system: feminine loanwords rely significantly more on fixed derivational suffixes, while masculine loanwords are more concentrated in the free-choice zone. This pattern exhibits loanword specificity and has become more pronounced in contemporary usage. FSDR therefore provides a quantitative framework for testing default gender and shows how masculine default can be activated and reinforced under language contact.

</details>


### [166] [Beyond Majority Vote: Multi-Perspective Adjudication for Medical Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03953)
*Joe Cecil,Marjorie Freedman*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对长文本聊天机器人回复中的事实性错误检测问题，提出了一种多视角标注研究方法，通过首轮标注、LLM作为裁判的候选发现以及医学专家和基于证据的事实核查两种裁决方式，发现单次标注会漏检错误，多轮裁决能提升覆盖率但结果仍受判断、专业知识和证据的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聊天机器人的安全性高度依赖于对生成文本中事实性错误频率的准确评估以及错误检测系统的性能。然而，现有的事实性错误检测通常被简化为单轮、单一标注者的标注问题。在长文本聊天机器人回复中，事实性错误往往微妙且嵌入在大部分正确的文本中，单次标注容易遗漏，因此需要更严谨的多视角标注方法来评估和构建更可靠的幻觉检测基准。

Method: 作者开发了一项针对医学相关聊天机器人回复的多视角标注研究，具体包括：(1) 首轮人工标注；(2) LLM作为裁判（LaJ）的候选发现；(3) 两种形式的裁决——医学专家裁决与基于证据的事实核查。通过比较不同阶段发现的错误以及裁决者之间的分歧，来评估各方法的覆盖率和可靠性，并将该技术应用于现有基准以验证其普遍性。

Result: 研究发现：首轮标注者经常遗漏后续被裁决者验证的事实性错误；LaJ能改进候选发现，但单独使用不够充分，也会遗漏标注者能发现的错误；裁决者之间存在分歧，表明多来源候选的裁决能提升基准完整性但无法消除对判断和专业知识的依赖。将该技术应用于现有基准，也发现了类似的漏标模式。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在所考察的场景中，单次通过的幻觉基准可能以漏计事实性错误为代价来换取规模。多轮裁决可以改善覆盖范围，但从中得出的推断仍然对用于判定错误存在与否的判断、专业知识和证据敏感。这提示构建幻觉基准时需要更审慎的多阶段标注流程。

Abstract: Understanding the frequency of factual errors in chatbot-generated text and evaluating systems that detect these errors is critical for determining chatbot safety. Yet factual-error detection is often treated as a single-pass, single-annotator labeling problem. In long-form chatbot responses, factual errors can be subtle and embedded within mostly correct text.
  We develop a multi-perspective annotation study of medically relevant chatbot responses, combining first-pass annotation, LLM-as-a-Judge (LaJ) candidate discovery, and two forms of adjudication: medical-expert and evidence-based fact-checking. First-pass annotators frequently miss factual errors later validated by adjudicators. LaJ improves candidate discovery, but is insufficient on its own: It misses factual errors that annotators catch. We also find disagreement among adjudicators, suggesting that adjudication over multiple candidate sources can improve benchmark completeness, but does not eliminate the need to apply judgment and expertise. Applied to an existing benchmark, this technique reveals a similar pattern of missing annotations. Together, these results suggest that in the settings examined here, single-pass hallucination benchmarks may achieve scale at the cost of undercounting factual errors. Multi-pass adjudication can improve coverage, but inferences drawn from the benchmarks are still sensitive to the judgment, expertise, and evidence used to determine error presence.

</details>


### [167] [Two-Stage Reinforcement Learning for Sound and Adversarial Test Generation in Code LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03955)
*Jiacheng Xu,Wentao Zhang,Zhiyi Lyu,Fuxiang Zhang,Chaojie Wang,Yang Liu,Bo An*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Test Cases Scaling (TCS)，一个两阶段强化学习框架，用于自动生成高质量的测试用例，以提升大语言模型（LLM）的代码生成能力。TCS 利用滚动策略对齐的缓冲区训练测试生成器：第一阶段生成与参考解决方案一致的测试，第二阶段将缓冲区限制在当前失效模式并学习反例测试。在 TACO 和 LiveCodeBench 基准上，TCS 显著提升了 pass@1 和基于生成测试的推理时答案选择性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习通过可执行反馈显著提升了LLM的代码生成能力，但编码问题的反馈主要来自具体的测试用例，而高质量测试用例（同时满足健全性sound和区分性discriminative）往往稀缺。现有方法受限于人工提供的测试用例质量，无法充分暴露求解器的失效模式，因此需要研究如何利用学习到的模型自动生成测试用例。

Method: 本文将测试用例自动生成形式化为一个对抗性强化学习问题：模型需根据求解器当前的失效模式生成有效的反例测试用例。具体提出TCS两阶段强化学习框架，两个阶段均从滚动策略对齐的缓冲区训练测试生成器——阶段1生成与参考解决方案一致的测试（保证测试的健全性），阶段2将缓冲区限制在当前失效模式上，学习能够击败求解器的反例测试。

Result: 在 TACO 和 LiveCodeBench 两个基准上，TCS 同时提升了 pass@1 指标以及基于生成测试的推理时答案选择准确率。此外，学习到的测试生成器还能有效实现其他 LLM 输出之间的选择。

Conclusion: TCS 证明通过对抗性强化学习自动生成测试用例是缓解高质量测试稀缺问题的有效途径，生成的反例测试不仅能改善求解器的训练过程，还能作为有效的推理时挑选工具，在多个 LLM 输出之间做出更优选择。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has substantially advanced code generation with large language models (LLMs) through executable feedback. The feedback for coding problems mainly comes from specific test cases, where high-quality test cases are often scarce since they should be both sound and discriminative. We thus turn to study the auto-generation of test cases using the learned model. We find this is naturally an adversarial RL problem: the model is expected to generate effective test cases as counterexamples, depending on the solver's current failure modes. We propose Test Cases Scaling (TCS), a two-stage RL framework for effective test generation. Both stages train a test generator from a rolling policy-aligned buffer: Stage 1 generates tests consistent with the reference solution, and Stage 2 restricts the buffer to current failure modes and learns counterexample tests. Across TACO and LiveCodeBench, TCS improves both pass@1 and inference-time answer selection according to generated tests. We find the learned test generator also enables effective selection among other LLM outputs.

</details>


### [168] [Investigating the Ability of Large Language Models to Analyze Recipes for Diabetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03967)
*Revathy Venkataramanan,Aditya Luthra,Venkatesan Nadimuthu,Amit Sheth*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）判断食谱是否适合糖尿病患者的能力，通过三种不同级别的饮食指南提示方式，构建了包含7607个食谱的基准数据集。结果显示多数LLM在预测时偏保守，能利用饮食指南进行推理的模型表现更好，其中Mistral-7B和Llama 70B表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究证明LLM在膳食规划方面具有能力，但尚不清楚它们能否分析给定食谱对糖尿病患者的适宜性。核心挑战在于检索相关饮食指南、分解食谱成分与烹饪方法，并应用指南进行判断。本文旨在系统研究这一能力。

Method: 采用三种提示策略：（i）直接查询提示，（ii）上下文引导提示，（iii）示例上下文提示，分别融入不同级别的糖尿病饮食指南（来自医学来源）。构建了包含7607个食谱的基准数据集（3807个适合糖尿病，3800个不适合），用于评估LLM的分类性能。

Result: 大多数LLM在预测食谱适合糖尿病时表现谨慎，以避免有害后果。能够利用饮食指南进行推理的模型在预测适宜性上表现更好。总体而言，Mistral-7B和Llama 70B的性能优于其他模型。

Conclusion: LLM具备分析糖尿病食谱适宜性的潜力，但需结合外部饮食指南进行推理。提示方式对性能有显著影响，且模型存在保守倾向。未来可进一步优化指南融合机制与评估基准。

Abstract: Several studies have evaluated the ability of Large Language Models (LLMs) for meal planning, yielding positive outcomes. These models can process natural language inputs and leverage learned knowledge from their pretraining to generate meal plans. In this work, we investigate the ability of LLMs to analyze the suitability of given recipes for diabetes. The primary challenge for LLMs is to retrieve relevant dietary guidelines for diabetes, decompose recipes into ingredients and cooking methods, and apply these guidelines to determine the recipe's suitability. To study these challenges, we employ three kinds of prompts namely, (i) Direct Query Prompt (ii) Context-Guided Prompt, and (iii) Exemplary Context Prompt that incorporate different levels of diabetes dietary guidelines from medical sources. We introduce a benchmark dataset curated for this investigation consisting of 7607 recipes that include 3807 recipes suitable for diabetes and 3800 recipes not suitable for diabetes. Our results demonstrate that most LLMs are cautious in predicting recipes as suitable to prevent detrimental outcomes. Further, the models that can reason using the dietary guidelines performed better in predicting the suitability of recipes for diabetes. Overall, Mistral-7B and Llama 70B showed superior performance to their counterparts.

</details>


### [169] [Alignment-Free Text-Audiobox for Voice Dubbing and Full-Duplex Dialogue Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03992)
*Sanyuan Chen,Min-Jae Hwang,Sho Inoue,Anna Sun,Bokai Yu,David Kant,Dongmin Hyun,Dorian Desblancs,Gregory Antonovsky,Oleg Repin,Peng-Jen Chen,Xutai Ma,Zehai Tu,Juan Pino,Wei-Ning Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Alignment-Free Text-Audiobox（Text-AB），一个基于Diffusion Transformer和流匹配目标训练的统一直播框架，用于高质量语音配音和全双工对话合成。相比Audiobox，它在潜在扩散、对齐机制和模型规模上做了三方面改进，支持跨语言配音、全双工对话和情感对话合成，并在真实配音基准上显著超越现有内部系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有配音和对话合成系统（如Audiobox）依赖强制对齐和显式时长预测，且音频压缩效率有限，模型规模和数据量不足，难以同时高质量地完成配音、全双工对话和情感表达等任务。作者旨在通过统一框架消除对齐依赖、提升压缩比并扩大规模，实现更自然、更富有表现力的语音生成。

Method: Text-AB基于Diffusion Transformer，使用流匹配（flow-matching）目标进行训练。核心方法包括：(1) 在潜在扩散框架中使用DAC-VAE特征，将48kHz波形编码为25Hz潜在序列，压缩率比EnCodec高10倍以上；(2) 采用对齐无关（alignment-free）设计，直接使用现成文本编码器并通过交叉注意力学习文本-语音对齐，避免强制对齐和时长预测；(3) 扩大规模：预训练3B参数模型于480k小时单语语音，然后在跨语言配音、全双工对话和情感对话三个下游任务上进行监督微调。推理时支持约1分钟的一次生成和通过多扩散方案的长篇生成，并采用多阶段重排序策略提升质量。

Result: 在真实配音基准上，Text-AB相比最新内部配音系统在韵律相似度、语音相似度、自然度和可分享性上带来显著提升。在全双工对话合成中，短对话接近人类录音水平，长对话在人性化和表现力上大幅优于内部最新模型，原生建模了轮流说话、反馈词和情感动态。情感对话合成中，情感条件显著改善了情感对齐和交互质量。

Conclusion: Text-AB通过对齐无关设计、高压缩潜在表示和大规模训练，在配音和全双工对话合成上取得了突破性性能，且能原生支持情感表达，展示了统一框架在复杂语音生成任务中的潜力。

Abstract: We present Alignment-Free Text-Audiobox (Text-AB), a unified framework for high-quality voice dubbing and full-duplex dialogue synthesis. Building on a Diffusion Transformer trained with a flow-matching objective, Text-AB departs from the Audiobox system along three dimensions. First, it operates in a latent diffusion framework using DAC-VAE features that encode 48 kHz waveforms into a 25 Hz latent sequence, giving over 10x higher compression than previous EnCodec representations while improving resynthesis quality. Second, Text-AB is alignment-free: it consumes raw text via an off-the-shelf text encoder and learns text-speech alignment through cross-attention, removing the need for forced alignment and explicit duration prediction. Third, we scale model and data substantially, pretraining a 3B-parameter model on 480k hours of monolingual speech, followed by supervised fine-tuning on three downstream tasks: cross-lingual voice dubbing, full-duplex dialogue synthesis, and emotional full-duplex dialogue synthesis. At inference, Text-AB supports one-shot generation for up to ~1 min of speech and arbitrarily long-form generation via a multi-diffusion scheme, plus a multi-stage reranking strategy that enhances quality based on automated metrics. On a real-world dubbing benchmark, Text-AB delivers a step-change improvement over the latest internal dubbing system, with large gains in prosody similarity, voice similarity, naturalness, and shareability. For full-duplex dialogue synthesis, it approaches human recordings on short-form conversations and substantially outperforms the latest internal model on long-form human-likeness and expressivity, while natively modeling turn-taking, back-channeling, and emotional dynamics. For emotional dialogue synthesis, emotion conditioning significantly improves emotion alignment and emotional interaction quality over the unconditioned baseline.

</details>


### [170] [Representational alignment yields generalizable safety in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04022)
*Lingyu Li,Yan Teng,Yingchun Wang,Xia Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现当前大型语言模型对道德概念的“原型式分类”能力较弱，在23个模型中普遍难以区分对立的道德类别或保持类别内的典型性梯度，且该缺陷不随参数规模与对齐阶段而改善。作者提出“表征相似性优化”方法，直接对齐模型潜在表征与人类道德判断的分类结构（而非监督生成回答）。在相同的251,334条道德标注上，标准行为对齐虽在响应层面学会判断，却基本未改变分类结构并加剧了对抗脆弱性；而重组道德分类虽在显式判断上增益有限，却在多种基准与攻击策略下持续提升对抗鲁棒性。研究为原型分类促进行为适应性提供了功能证据，并表明将该表征原理迁移到LLM可获得对抗条件下可泛化的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM对齐方法主要优化可观察的响应，但当相同的恶意意图以人类易于识别的不熟悉或对抗性形式出现时，模型仍然脆弱。作者希望借鉴认知科学的原型理论解释人类的适应性，并探究LLM是否具备类似的基于原型的概念分类能力，以及如何将该原理迁移到模型以实现可泛化的安全对齐。

Method: 首先对23个LLM进行道德概念原型分类能力的系统评测，考察其对对立道德类别的区分能力和类别内部典型性梯度的保持；随后提出“表征相似性优化”，直接对LLM的潜在表征进行对齐，使其符合人类道德判断所表达的分类结构，而不对生成响应施加监督。在匹配实验中，使用相同的251,334条道德标注，将新方法与标准行为对齐（响应层级监督）进行对比。

Result: 23个LLM普遍无法区分对立道德类别或保持类别内精细的典型性梯度，且该缺陷跨越参数规模和不同对齐阶段持续存在。与标准行为对齐相比，后者在响应层面学会了预期道德判断，但分类结构基本保持不变，并增加了对抗评估下的脆弱性；而表征相似性优化在显式判断上仅带来较温和的提升，但在不同模型规模、多个基准和多种攻击策略下持续改善了对抗鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究为“基于原型的分类有助于行为适应性”提供了功能性支持，并表明将这一表征原理迁移到LLM，能够在对抗条件下实现可泛化的安全。这提示对齐不应只停留在响应监督层面，更应关注模型内部表征的分类结构。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) is essential for their safe deployment. Current alignment methods mainly optimize observable responses, yet models remain vulnerable when the same harmful intent is recast in unfamiliar or adversarial forms that humans can easily recognize. Prototype theory offers an account of this adaptability. Human concepts are represented around central cases, and new instances are categorized according to their graded typicality relative to these prototypes. Here we show that such categorization of moral concepts is weakly preserved in current LLMs. Across 23 LLMs, models often failed to distinguish opposed moral categories or preserve fine-grained typicality within each category. These deficits persist across parameter sizes and alignment stages. We developed representational similarity optimization, which directly aligns the latent representations in LLMs with the categorization expressed in human moral judgements, without supervising generated responses. In matched experiments using the same 251,334 moral annotations, standard behavioral alignment learned the intended moral judgements at the response level while leaving the categorization structure largely unchanged and increasing vulnerability across adversarial evaluations. Reorganizing moral categorization produced more modest gains in explicit judgements but consistently improved adversarial robustness across model scales on diverse benchmarks and attack strategies. Our findings provide functional support for the view that prototype-based categorization contributes to behavioral adaptability. They also show that transferring this representational principle to LLMs yields generalizable safety under adversarial conditions.

</details>


### [171] [Translation as a Decision Space: A Multi-Agent Perspective on Low-Resource Dialect Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04048)
*Hasan Alkhder,Mohammad Abboush,Igor Tchappi,Ahmet Zengin,Amro Najjar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将神经机器翻译重新定义为由自主翻译代理探索的结构化决策空间，而非单一输出分析。在土耳其语-叙利亚阿拉伯语低资源方言翻译中，通过三种代理策略（零样本直接翻译、方言稳定化微调、英语枢轴翻译）进行实证研究，量化了各代理间的行为差异。结果显示轻量级稳定化使方言标记使用率从0.2266增至0.4988，显著降低结构不稳定性，而枢轴翻译引入标准化压力，零样本翻译具有最高决策方差。论文论证了跨代理的翻译分歧揭示了多语言模型中的潜在决策灵活性，为低资源方言生成提供了可解释性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统NMT系统为每个输入仅生成单一输出，掩盖了多语言解码中隐含的替代决策轨迹。在低资源方言场景中，多种语言上有效的实现可能在词汇真实性、语域和结构稳定性上有所不同，这种不透明性尤为突出。作者旨在通过将翻译建模为结构化决策空间，将代理间的分歧视为可解释的行为信号，而非误差，从而提供低资源方言生成的可解释性框架。

Method: 论文采用三种翻译代理进行实证研究：(1)零样本直接翻译；(2)通过轻量级微调实现的方言稳定化翻译（基于5,000对对话语料和MADAR-Turk资源训练）；(3)通过英语的枢轴翻译。在5,000句对话上评估，采用三个行为位移指标：方言标记频率、与标准阿拉伯语的词汇接近度、结构方差，而非传统性能指标。

Result: 轻量级稳定化使方言标记使用率近乎翻倍（0.2266→0.4988），同时显著降低结构不稳定性；枢轴翻译引入标准化压力并产生可测量的压缩效应；零样本翻译展示最高决策方差。三种代理在方言标记频率、词汇接近度和结构方差上表现出可量化的行为差异。

Conclusion: 跨代理的翻译分歧揭示了多语言模型中潜在的语言决策灵活性，而非简单错误。论文提供了一个基于原则的可解释性框架，用于低资源方言生成，主张结构化行为分析优于传统性能指标优化。

Abstract: Neural machine translation (NMT) systems typically produce a single output per input, obscuring the alternative decision trajectories implicitly available within multilingual decoding. This opacity becomes particularly problematic in low-resource dialect settings, where multiple linguistically valid realizations may differ in lexical authenticity, register, and structural stability. We propose reframing translation as a structured decision space explored by autonomous translation agents. Instead of analyzing a single output, we model distinct translation pathways as agents operating over a shared multilingual backbone. Inter-agent divergence is treated not as error but as an interpretable behavioral signal. We conduct an empirical study on Turkish--Syrian Arabic translation using three agents: (1) zero-shot direct translation, (2) dialect-stabilized translation via lightweight fine-tuning, and (3) pivot translation through English. Evaluation is performed on 5,000 dialogue sentences, while stabilization is trained on 5,000 additional Turkish--Syrian sentence pairs drawn from television dialogue and MADAR-Turk resources. Rather than optimizing for conventional performance metrics, we quantify structured behavioral displacement using dialect marker frequency, lexical proximity to standardized Arabic, and structural variance. Lightweight stabilization nearly doubles dialect marker usage, increasing it from 0.2266 to 0.4988, while significantly reducing structural instability. Pivot mediation introduces normalization pressure and measurable compression effects, whereas zero-shot translation exhibits the highest decision variance. We argue that translation divergence across agents reveals latent decision flexibility within multilingual models and we provide a principled interpretability framework for low-resource dialect generation.

</details>


### [172] [Sequential Beats Joint: On the Interplay between On-Policy Distillation and RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04108)
*Boyan Li,Bingsen Chen,Chenghao Yang,Ping Nie,Chen Zhao,Xi Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个简单的两阶段训练方案——先进行在线策略蒸馏（OPD），再进行带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）——在逻辑和数学推理基准上，该方案始终优于纯OPD、纯RLVR以及所有联合训练基线，并从pass@k、学习动态和参数更新三个角度系统解释了其优势：OPD扩展学生对教师支持解的覆盖，而RL在该支持范围内进一步锐化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作试图在同一训练步骤中融合OPD的稠密token级监督与RLVR的稀疏奖励信号，采用加权相加或教师调制重缩放等方式。作者认为这两种信号被"纠缠"在一起会相互干扰，因此需要探讨是否可以通过分阶段使用这两种信号来获得更好的效果。

Method: 将训练过程分解为两个明确的阶段：第一阶段采用OPD（在线策略蒸馏）利用教师模型的稠密token级监督来训练学生模型；第二阶段切换为RLVR（带可验证奖励的强化学习）以奖励信号进行强化。通过实验对比纯OPD、纯RLVR、加权相加融合和教师调制重缩放等多种基线，并结合pass@k行为、学习动态和参数更新三个维度进行系统性分析。

Result: 两阶段方案（OPD-then-RL）在逻辑推理和数学推理基准上一致优于所有对比方法。实验发现：OPD扩大了学生对教师支持解的覆盖范围，RL则在已覆盖的支持范围内进一步锐化策略；联合优化两种信号会导致两者相互干扰，效果反而变差。此外，OPD验证分数是切换阶段的关键信号，且OPD比SFT作为RL的冷启动效果更好。

Conclusion: OPD-then-RL是一种简单且强力的两阶段组合训练方案，将原本纠缠的两种训练信号转化为互补的训练阶段，建立了OPD-then-RL作为推理大语言模型后期训练的一种系统性、可解释的强基线方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and on-policy distillation (OPD) have emerged as two dominant methods for post-training reasoning LLMs. Prior work uses OPD's dense token-level supervision to complement the sparse RL reward, fusing the two signals within a single step: either as a \emph{weighted-additive combination} or a \emph{teacher-modulated rescaling} of the RL advantage. In this paper, we show that a simple two-stage scheme, OPD-then-RL, consistently outperforms pure OPD, pure RLVR, and all such joint baselines across logic and math reasoning benchmarks. Beyond the empirical results, we further provide a systematic understanding of this through pass@$k$ behavior, learning dynamics, and parameter updates, yielding a consistent explanation: OPD expands the student's coverage of teacher-supported solutions and RL sharpens within that support, while jointly optimizing the two signals causes them to interfere.To provide a practical recipe, we find that the OPD validation score is the key signal for when to switch to RL, and that OPD is a better cold start for RL than SFT. Together, our results establish OPD-then-RL as a simple yet strong way to combine the two methods, turning two entangled signals into complementary stages.

</details>


### [173] [Last Translation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04173)
*Vilém Zouhar,Niyati Bafna,Mukund Choudhary,Maike Züfle,Sara Rajaee,Pinzhen Chen,Jannis Vamvas,Sara Papi,Ona de Gibert,Bhavitvya Malik,Eliya Habba,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Patrícia Schmidtová,Michelle Wastl,Sheriff Issaka,Leshem Choshen,Stella Biderman,Antonis Anastasopoulos,Jan Niehues,Rico Sennrich,Mrinmaya Sachan,Ondřej Bojar,Kenton Murray,Jörg Tiedemann,Alham Fikri Aji,Philipp Koehn,Christof Monz,Alexandra Birch,Sowmya Vajjala,Chalamalasetti Kranti,Cristina España-Bonet,Nobin Sarwar,David Kaczér,Shunta Asano,Malik Marmonier,Daban Q. Jaff,Vaisakhi Mishra,Hend Al- Khalifa,Gabriele Sarti,Sourajit Saha,Nils Rehlinger,Juan Daniel Cuervo Villa,Jonathan Tonglet,Saugata Purkayastha,Dominik Macháček,Jagannathan Ramanujam,Heejin Do,Zuzana Nadova,Fred Philippy,Fabian Retkowski,Maria Lymperaiou,Silvia Casola,Hanna Yukhymenko,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Sangwon Ryu,Andrés Jerez,Ron Keinan,Shuaib Shuaib Yusuf,Avantica Vempati,Maria Carmen Staiano,Sukannya Purkayastha,Adrian Cosma,Vitalii Babenko,Erivan Inan,Aviral Nigam,Wafa Aissa,Fatima Haouari,Venkata Prasanth Kumar Gummadi,Mehdi Jafarzadeh,Valentin Scourneau,Lukas Edman,Kaiser Sun,Shaomu Tan,Mohammad Sadegh Gholizadeh,Johannes-Rudolf David,Dipankar Srirag,Javier García Gilabert,Ruta Binkyte,Manar Ali,Ana-Maria Bucur,Sabry E. Farrag,Youssef Saber,Yihong Liu,Jean Maillard,Cojocaru Nicoleta,Xiaochuang Yuan,Sina Ahmadi,Philipp Mondorf,Kaustubh Dhole,Roman Wixinger,Shenbin Qian,Manuel Tuor,Sergey Troshin,Jonathan Yahav,Fida Mohammad Thoker,Amir Arsalan Rezapour,Lance Calvin Lim Gamboa,Manon Reusens,Kätriin Kukk,Koel Dutta Chowdhury,Giuseppe Gallipoli,Christian Hoang,Shaswati Saha,Seth Aycock,Jan Kocoń,Bo Chen,Linh Vu,Vatsal Venkatkrishna,Arafat Ahsan,Luan Thanh Nguyen,Hassan Soliman,Daryna Dementieva,Theresia Veronika Rampisela,Ngoc Quynh Tram Do,Marius Huber,Kazuki Egashira,Azmine Toushik Wasi,Vladislav Poritski,Mike Zhang,Deep Shah,Paul Gavrikov,Luis Frentzen Salim,David Africa,R. Damanhuri,Bello Umar Bello,Anumit Garg,Gengyu Rao,Pawan Sasanka Ammanamanchi,Kamile Dementaviciute,Andrianos Michail,L D M S Sai Teja,Dawei Zhu,Yi Fan,Wei Liu,Farhan Farsi,Elias Herranen,Sankalan Pal Chowdhury,Karen Sanchez,Farzad Shami,Ashok Urlana,Zimu Wang,Tomasz Limisiewicz,Priyaranjan Pattnayak,Marii Ojastu,Hongbin Na,Emilian Radoi,Chenyi Zhao,Carlos Hinojosa,Andrea Gregor de Varda,Zaid Alyafeai,Reem Alzahrani,Nehal Kathrotia,Alex Flückiger,Ulysses Sekai Tully Carr,Jimson Paulo Layacan,Guy Kaplan,Ritwik Tiwari,Rishit Dagli,Oksana Volchek,Isaac R Caswell,Bowen Yi,Blanka Kövér,Amir Hossein Yari,Aicha Chorana,Zhengxiang Wang,Selja Keränen,Samuel Simko,Joy Olusanya,Jenny Chim,Enzo Doyen,Vivek Harsha Lakkamaneni,Sophia Conrad,Pouya Sadeghi,Panayiotis Panayiotou,Luis Lara,Jannatul Nayem,Eran Yahav,Debanshu Das,Antonia Karamolegkou,Anmol Goel,Aishik Mandal,Tommaso Cerruti,Raoyuan Zhao,Mykola Haltiuk,Thura Aung,Naser Almousa,Amir Hossein Kargaran,Rachel Bawden,Qiaoyuan Zheng,Mateusz Lango,Beni Egressy,Fidel Rodríguez Velásquez,Natchapon Jongwiriyanurak,Minh Ngoc Do,Marco Gaido,Lena Libon,Dzmitry Kuzmin,Badal Nyalang,Antoine Taroni,Andrei Niculae,Abdulaziz Nura Kani,Rushikesh Zawar,Marek Šuppa,Beatrice Savoldi,Andreas Simons,Rayyan Merchant,Ilai Yaron Levy,Francesco Pinto,Ziyi Yang,Yolanda Xavier,Samuel Frontull,Muhammad Ravi Shulthan Habibi,Kenneth Enevoldsen,Harris Abdul Majid,Francesca Padovani,Tim Graf,Tatiana Bielakova,Sharifa Djurabaeva,Shaoxiong Ji,Raia Abu Ahmad,Pavel Stepachev,Jirui Qi,Ayush Sunil Munot,Alireza Pakniat,Ayla Rigouts Terryn,Yuxing Lu,Yurii Paniv,Xiyan Fu,Tosin Adewumi,Sunisth Kumar,Stéphane J. P. S. Thunus,Shree Harsha Bokkahalli Satish,Shayan Bali,Prakhar Gupta,Papa Abdou Karim Karou Diallo,Matija Akrap,Marko Culjak,Kristýna Onderková,Joseph Attieh,Esrael Teferi Tensay,Elisabeth Fittschen,Benoît Sagot,Jingwei Ni,Yu Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“Last Translation Benchmark”（LTB，最后一英里翻译基准），一个由人工撰写并经同行评审的对抗性示例集（含文本、图像、音频、视频），用以打破现有领先机器翻译模型；同时提出一种新的评估方法：每个示例配有手工制作的验证规则，描述具体失败案例，从而实现可靠且可行动的评估。LTB 是持续更新的活数据集（当前版本 LTBv1）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型能力增强，标准机器翻译基准趋于饱和；自动翻译指标不可靠、易受奖励黑客攻击且缺乏可行动性；即使人工黄金评估也存在可复现性、客观性和可扩展性不足的问题，阻碍了客观跟踪领域进展和识别改进路径。

Method: 构建一个包含人工撰写且经同行评审的多模态示例（文本、图像、音频、视频）的基准，这些示例专门用于打破领先的翻译模型；并引入一种新的评估范式，即每个示例附带手工制作的验证规则，对特定失败案例给出明确判定，支持可靠且可行动的未来评估。该基准为活数据集，持续接受贡献。

Result: 发布了最新版本 LTBv1，包含 2026 年 9 月 1 日之前接受的贡献，并计划随着持续数据收集推出未来版本。

Conclusion: LTB 通过对抗性多模态示例和基于验证规则的评估方法，为在模型能力增强背景下跟踪机器翻译客观进展、识别具体失败模式提供了可靠且可扩展的替代方案，并借助持续更新的机制保持长期有效性。

Abstract: For scientific progress, we need benchmarks that test the limits of state-of-the-art models, and evaluation methods that inform us about failure cases. As models get stronger, standard benchmarks for machine translation are approaching saturation. Further, automatic translation metrics are unreliable, vulnerable to reward-hacking, and provide unactionable assessments. Even gold human evaluation is not problem-free, because it often lacks reproducibility, objectivity, and scalability. Overall, this prevents us from tracking objective progress in the field and identifying pathways for improvement. We introduce the Last Translation Benchmark, a collection of human-authored and peer-reviewed examples (texts, images, audio, videos) that break leading machine translation models. We also present a new evaluation approach: each example comes with handcrafted verification rules describing concrete failure cases on that example, therefore allowing reliable and actionable future evaluation. The Last Translation Benchmark is a live dataset that accepts ongoing contributions. The latest version is LTBv1, containing accepted contributions prior to September 1st 2026, with future releases planned as new data is continuously collected.

</details>


### [174] [Knowledge Acquisition During Pre-training? Large Language Models Learn Better With Auxiliary Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04180)
*Joseph Lee,Yidi Huang,Dokyoon Kim,Shu Yang,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验研究了大型语言模型（LLM）预训练中知识获取的机制，提出辅助视图（知识的重述形式）对学习具有因果帮助作用。研究发现：重复是知识获取的必要条件且释义仅在较小批大小时有帮助；在固定token预算下将文档重复的token分配给辅助视图反而能改善学习（甚至包括事实回忆）；辅助视图的有效性不依赖于生成它的教师模型强度；识别出有助于弥补先前知识缺口的上下文性和基础性知识形式；并从逐层偏差和压缩角度揭示了其机制性表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对LLM在预训练过程中如何获取知识仍存在理解空白。本文旨在填补这一空白，探究辅助视图（即知识的多种表述形式）是否是预训练成功的关键因素，从而为数据多样性为何重要提供解释。

Method: 采用受控实验设计来隔离变量影响。具体包括：1）验证重复是知识获取的必要条件，并测试释义在不同批大小下的效果；2）在固定token预算下，比较将token从文档重复分配到辅助视图的效果；3）改变生成辅助视图的教师模型强度来测试其影响；4）识别不同类型的知识（上下文性和基础性）及其对学习的作用；5）通过逐层偏差和压缩分析揭示机制性的影响路径。

Result: 实验结果显示：重复对知识获取是必要的，而释义仅在较小批大小下有帮助；将token从重复分配至辅助视图可改善学习效果（即使对事实回忆也是如此），这与直觉相悖；辅助视图的有效性不受教师模型质量制约；上下文性和基础性知识形式能弥补先前知识缺口；机制上表现为逐层偏差和压缩的变化。

Conclusion: 研究结论表明，辅助视图作为知识的多种表述形式，在大型预训练语料库中自然出现，是预训练成功的关键因素之一。这一发现为数据多样性为何重要提供了合理解释，并深化了对LLM知识学习机制的理解。

Abstract: Gaps remain in our understanding of how large language models (LLMs) acquire knowledge during pre-training. We posit that auxiliary views, reformulations of knowledge, are causally helpful for learning. We design controlled experiments to isolate this. First, we confirm that repetition is necessary for acquisition and clarify that paraphrasing helps only at smaller batch sizes. Second, holding the token budget fixed, allocating tokens from document repetition to auxiliary views improves learning, counterintuitively, even for factual recall. Third, the effectiveness of auxiliary views is not contingent on the strength of the teacher model that generates them. Fourth, we identify forms of knowledge, contextual and foundational, that aid learning in the presence of prior knowledge gaps. Finally, we examine how these effects manifest mechanistically via layer-wise biases and compression. Together, our findings suggest that auxiliary representations of knowledge, which arise naturally in large pre-training corpora, are a key factor in the success of pre-training and offer a plausible explanation for why data diversity matters.

</details>


### [175] [Legibility is Not Interpretability: Comparing Judged and Actual Importance in Chain-Of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04194)
*Kevin Du,Alexander Hoyle,Laura Ruis,Acyr Locatelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛模拟估算推理步骤的优势值（advantage），将步骤重要性作为基准，评估LLM评判员能否从文本中识别出关键推理步骤。研究发现：能力足够强的LLM能超过流行率基线，但远低于噪声上限；微调步骤级评判员对错误回答有显著改善，但对正确回答仍与上限有差距，表明推理步骤的重要性只能从文本中部分恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 链式思维模型的推理轨迹被视为可理解的窗口，被广泛用于诊断错误、评估忠实性，以及通过过程奖励模型和生成式评判员提供步骤级监督。然而这些实践依赖于一个关键假设：推理步骤的文本承载了其功能角色的信息。本文旨在验证这一假设——文本是否真的编码了哪些推理步骤重要的信息？

Method: 本文将推理步骤的重要性操作化为其优势值（advantage）：即包含该步骤所带来的期望奖励（如产生正确答案）的变化，通过蒙特卡洛展开（Monte Carlo rollouts）估计。以这些估计为基准，评估LLM评判员能否识别高优势步骤；同时微调步骤级评判员模型，比较其在正确与错误回答上的表现差异。

Result: 研究发现：能力足够强大的LLM能够超过流行率基线，但远未达到噪声上限；微调后的步骤级评判员在错误回答上表现显著提升，但在正确回答上仍与上限存在明显差距，说明步骤重要性只能从推理轨迹文本中部分恢复。

Conclusion: 本文结论为链式思维忠实性研究增添了警示性证据：不应将推理轨迹的易读性（legibility）等同于可解释性（interpretability），这对过程奖励建模具有重要启示意义。

Abstract: Reasoning traces from chain-of-thought models appear to offer a legible window into how a model arrives at its answer. A growing body of work treats them as such, using LLM judges to diagnose errors, evaluate faithfulness, and provide step-level supervision via process reward models and generative critics. These practices rely on the text of a reasoning step carrying information about its functional role. But does the text actually encode information about which reasoning steps matter? We operationalize the importance of a reasoning step as its advantage: the change in expected reward, e.g., producing the correct final answer, from including that step, estimated via Monte Carlo rollouts. Basing ground truth on these estimates, we evaluate whether LLM judges can identify high-advantage steps and find that sufficiently capable LLMs can outperform a prevalence baseline but fall well short of a noise ceiling. Fine-tuning a model as a step-level critic yields strong improvement for incorrect responses but remains distant from ceiling for correct responses, suggesting that step importance is only partially recoverable from the text of the reasoning trace. Our findings contribute to a growing body of chain-of-thought faithfulness work that cautions against treating the legibility of reasoning traces as interpretability, especially with implications for process reward modeling.

</details>


### [176] [ESPO: Error-Structured Prompt Optimization via Diagnose, Diversify, and Stabilize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04197)
*Lihao Liu,Peng Tang,Kunwar Yashraj Singh,Shabnam Ghadar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ESPO（错误结构化提示优化），通过诊断-提出-选择三阶段解决进化提示优化器（如GEPA）的提示膨胀问题，在七个NLP基准上平均准确率提升3.76个百分点，提示长度缩短47%，且跨模型实验中均取得最佳平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化提示优化器如GEPA存在提示膨胀问题：每次迭代不断追加规则与告诫，使提示最长膨胀3倍但准确率不增。作者将其归因于三个缺陷：错误观察不完整、搜索多样性有限、选择不可靠。

Method: ESPO将提示优化分解为三个阶段：Diagnose（一轮内将所有训练错误聚类为结构化模式）、Propose（通过四种互补策略生成候选，每种策略具有独立偏差）、Select（应用bootstrap稳定性选择）。并给出泛化界，将每个阶段对应测试时差距的相应项。

Result: 在Tweet、MMLU、GSM8K、HotpotQA、ScoNe、HoVer和PUPA七个公开基准上，ESPO平均准确率74.67%对GEPA的70.91%，提升3.76个百分点；每个数据集均持平或超越GEPA；提示字符数1004对1878（缩短47%），推理更快。跨模型实验（Gemma 3 12B、Mistral 14B、Qwen3 32B、Claude Haiku 4.5）中ESPO在每类模型均取得最佳平均准确率，其中Qwen3 GSM8K从15.00%提升至91.40%。消融实验证实：增加多样性但不用bootstrap选择反而损害性能（-1.20%）。

Conclusion: ESPO通过错误结构化诊断、互补探索与bootstrap稳定性选择，系统性缓解提示膨胀并显著提高提示优化效果；泛化界与消融实验支持各阶段设计的合理性，证明bootstrap选择对多样性增益的必要性。

Abstract: Evolutionary prompt optimizers such as GEPA suffer from prompt bloat: each iteration appends rules and caveats, producing prompts up to 3$\times$ longer yet no more accurate. We trace this to three deficiencies - incomplete error observation, limited search diversity, and unreliable selection - and propose ESPO (Error-Structured Prompt Optimization), which decomposes prompt optimization into three phases: Diagnose clusters all training errors into structural patterns in one round; Propose generates candidates via four complementary strategies with independent biases; Select applies bootstrap stability selection. On seven public NLP benchmarks - Tweet, MMLU, GSM8K, HotpotQA, ScoNe, HoVer, and PUPA - ESPO improves average accuracy by $+$3.76 pp over the state-of-the-art (74.67% vs 70.91% for GEPA), matching or exceeding GEPA on every dataset while producing prompts 47% shorter (1,004 vs 1,878 chars) and faster at inference. Cross-model experiments across four additional student models (Gemma 3 12B, Mistral 14B, Qwen3 32B, Claude Haiku 4.5) show ESPO yields the best average accuracy on every model tested, with the largest gap on Qwen3 GSM8K (15.00% $\to$ 91.40%). A generalization bound (Appendix) grounds each phase in a corresponding term of the test-time gap, and the ablation confirms a key prediction: adding diversity without bootstrap selection actually hurts performance ($-$1.20%).

</details>


### [177] [Compile by Training: Turning Natural-Language Specifications into Local Neural Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04199)
*Yuntian Deng,Pengyu Nie,Stuart Shieber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"编译式训练"（compile by training）方法，将自然语言规范转化为可复用的神经函数。该方法在编译阶段利用教师模型生成任务特定示例，训练一个小型适配器用于紧凑的解释器，生成的函数无需教师模型即可运行，并可像普通软件一样存储、版本化和组合。在FuzzyBench-Hard上达到83.6%的语义准确率，显著优于Program-as-Weights快速编译器，但编译成本更高（约一分钟）。该编译器已部署为公开交互服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多重复性文本函数易于用自然语言描述，但难以用规则实现；而直接调用大型远程模型处理每个输入会带来重复的成本、延迟以及对供应商的依赖。作者旨在解决"描述容易、实现困难、调用大模型成本高"的矛盾，寻求一种将规范编译为可复用、低成本、本地可运行的函数的方法。

Method: 核心方法是"编译式训练"：在编译阶段，由教师（teacher）模型根据自然语言规范生成任务特定示例，用这些示例训练一个小型适配器（adapter）来适配一个紧凑的解释器（compact interpreter）。编译产物是一个无需教师模型即可运行的神经函数，具备软件工程的属性（可存储、版本化、可组合）。

Result: 在FuzzyBench-Hard子集上（该子集是Program-as-Weights快速编译器无法产生精确匹配的部分），编译式训练达到83.6%的语义准确率。相比快速编译器以秒计的编译时间，本方法编译成本更高，约为一分钟。作者将该编译器部署为公开交互服务，并在多站点网站辅助工具、语言控制3D虚拟角色和双向英-Claudish翻译器中展示了编译函数的实际应用。

Conclusion: 编译式训练在将自然语言规范转换为可复用神经函数方面取得了更高的语义准确率（83.6% vs 快速编译器的0%精确匹配），代价是更高的编译时间（约一分钟）。该方法在多种实际场景（网站辅助、3D角色控制、翻译器）中验证了可行性，展示了将描述性文本函数转化为普通软件组件的能力。

Abstract: Many recurring text functions are easy to describe but difficult to implement with rules, while calling a large remote model for every input introduces repeated cost, latency, and dependency on a provider. We present compile by training, which turns a natural-language specification into a reusable neural function. At compile time, teacher models generate task-specific examples that are used to train a small adapter for a compact interpreter. The resulting function runs without the teachers and can be stored, versioned, and composed like ordinary software. On FuzzyBench-Hard, a subset on which the Program-as-Weights fast compiler produced no exact matches, compile by training reaches 83.6% semantic accuracy. This higher accuracy comes with a higher compile-time cost: roughly a minute rather than seconds for the fast compiler. We deploy the compiler in a public interactive service and demonstrate compiled functions in a multi-site website helper, a language-controlled 3D avatar, and a bidirectional English-Claudish translator.

</details>


<div id='cs.MM'></div>

# cs.MM [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [CAPQ-FAST: Content-Adaptive Perceived Quality Assessment for Faster Audiovisual Playback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03498)
*Jiarun Song,Yuxin Song,Fuzheng Yang,Weisi Lin*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文针对在线音视频服务中倍速播放场景下用户感知质量问题，通过主观实验分析了视频、语音、音乐及音视频内容在不同播放速度下的感知质量差异，提取内容特有的时间动态特征（视频的TI、语音的WPM、音乐的BPM），分别构建预测模型，并整合为统一的内容自适应感知质量评估模型（CAPQ-FAST），实验验证该模型能有效预测不同播放速度下的感知质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 倍速播放已成为现代在线音视频服务的常见功能，但视频、音频（语音和音乐）和音视频等不同模态内容在倍速播放下对可理解性和信息完整性的要求各不相同，导致感知质量因内容类型而异。然而，不同播放速度下用户感知质量的研究仍不充分，缺乏针对性的评估模型。

Method: 本文开展了一系列主观实验，分析播放速度与视频、音频、音视频内容感知质量之间的关系。提取内容特有的内在特征以捕获时间动态，包括视频的时间信息（TI）、语音的每分钟字数（WPM）和音乐的每分钟节拍数（BPM）。分别针对视频、语音和音乐开发感知质量预测模型，并将其整合为统一的内容自适应倍速感知质量评估模型（CAPQ-FAST）。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提出的模型能有效预测不同播放速度下的感知质量，验证了结合内容特定时间特征与多模态整合建模方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该模型可帮助服务提供商更好地理解用户在倍速播放下的观看意图和感知体验，从而支持更自适应的个性化推荐和播放控制，增强用户体验的自适应性。

Abstract: Faster playback has become a common feature in modern online audiovisual services, allowing users to consume content in less time while still maintaining a coherent viewing experience. However, different modalities of media content, such as video, audio (including speech and music), and audiovisual, exhibit varying requirements for understandability and information integrity under faster playback. These differences lead to noticeable variations in perceived quality depending on the content type. Nevertheless, users' perceived quality at different playback speeds remains insufficiently investigated. To address this gap, this paper conducts a series of subjective experiments to analyze the relationship between playback speed and perceived quality for video, audio, and audiovisual content. Content-specific intrinsic features are extracted to capture temporal dynamics, including temporal information (TI) for video, words per minute (WPM) for speech, and beats per minute (BPM) for music. Predictive models of perceived quality under faster playback are then developed separately for video, speech, and music. By integrating these models, a unified content-adaptive perceived quality assessment model (CAPQ-FAST) is proposed for faster audiovisual playback. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed model effectively predicts perceived quality under different playback speeds. This model can help service providers better understand users' viewing intentions and perceptual experiences under faster playback, thereby enabling more adaptive personalized recommendations and playback control to enhance user experience adaptability.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [179] [The Geometry of Ignorance: LLMs Know When to Temper Bayesian Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02959)
*Toni J. B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Yizhou Liu,Gurbir Arora,Nicolas Boullé,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型的unembedding矩阵中存在一个编码训练语料unigram分布的单一方向，称之为"无知方向"（direction of ignorance），它充当模型在不确定时的贝叶斯先验。通过将最终预测状态投影到该方向，可得到每个token的"先验加载因子"λ，该因子随上下文信息量增加而稳定下降。该投影将预测状态分解为两个正交向量，恰好对应温度化贝叶斯更新的两个因素：以λ为指数的unigram先验和上下文驱动的似然。λ在不同模型大小和家族间可比，更大的模型在高上下文情境下对先验依赖更低。此外该方向具有因果活性，通过调整λ可控制预测在KL散度上靠近或远离unigram先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型在信息匮乏（线索很少）时的预测行为，探究其预测背后的先验机制：模型究竟如何利用什么作为默认分布/贝叶斯先验，以及该先验如何被几何地编码在unembedding矩阵中。

Method: 在四个模型家族（Llama、Qwen、Gemma、Pythia，规模0.4B~405B）中分析unembedding矩阵的几何结构，识别编码unigram分布的单一方向；将最终预测状态投影到此方向获取每token先验加载因子λ；将预测状态正交分解为两个向量，分别对应tempered贝叶斯更新中的unigram先验（指数λ）与上下文似然；进行因果干预实验，通过调整λ来在KL散度上控制预测偏离/趋近先验的程度。

Result: 发现"无知方向"在全部四类模型家族中出现；λ随上下文信息量增加而稳定下降；投影分解可校准λ使其跨模型大小和家族可比，更大模型在高上下文限制下对先验的依赖更低；λ在KL散度上因果地控制预测朝向或远离unigram先验。

Conclusion: 语言模型的预测不确定性由unembedding几何中的"无知方向"及其对应的先验加载因子λ所支配，这提供了一种兼具几何与概率论意义的统一解释框架。λ可将模型预测状态形式化为tempered贝叶斯更新，且具备因果可控性，为理解和操控模型在低信息语境下的预测行为提供了理论基础。

Abstract: What does a language model predict when it has few clues? The answer lurks in its unembedding geometry: a single direction of the unembedding matrix encodes the unigram distribution of the training corpus, which serves as the Bayesian prior the model falls back on when uncertain. This structure --- which we term the \emph{direction of ignorance} --- appears in all four model families examined (\texttt{Llama}, \texttt{Qwen}, \texttt{Gemma}, and \texttt{Pythia}), ranging from 0.4B to 405B parameters. Projecting the final prediction state onto this direction yields a per-token \emph{prior loading factor} $λ$, which, empirically, declines steadily as the context becomes more informative. Formally, the same projection decomposes the prediction state into two orthogonal vectors that correspond exactly to the two factors of a tempered Bayesian update: a unigram prior raised to the exponent $λ$ and a context-driven likelihood. This geometric-probabilistic interpretation calibrates $λ$, making it meaningfully comparable across model sizes and families, with larger models generally exhibiting lower prior reliance in the high-context limit. Finally, we show that the direction of ignorance is causally active: raising or lowering $λ$ at the final prediction state steers the prediction toward or away from the unigram prior in KL divergence.

</details>


### [180] [Equation Recast for Canonical Operator Learning Across Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02982)
*Qiyun Cheng,Valentin Duruisseaux,Cesar F. Clauser,Md Hossain Sahadath,Huihua Yang,Shaowu Pan,Nathaniel Ferraro,Anima Anandkumar,Wei Ji,Cristina Rea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"方程重铸"（equation recast）方法，将参数化算子学习重构为对单一规范算子的学习，参数诱导的算子变化从控制方程解析推导并吸收为有效源项，从而实现跨新参数区间的零样本预测。该方法在多元参数、非线性及奇异PDE场景中支持外推，能将稀疏异质数据集整合到共享规范表示中，并利用收敛失败作为重铸迭代失效的内部警示信号。在核聚变托卡马克高保真仿真中，该框架通过规范域映射统一了四种装置几何的电子温度数据，为方程引导迁移、数据效率与可监控推理的可复用神经PDE求解器提供了路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纯数据驱动的参数化模型在跨广泛参数范围学习解算子时，需要大量覆盖输入函数和物理参数；且模型在训练分布之外可能静默失效（fail silently），缺乏可靠性保障。这促使探索一种能够从控制方程解析地推导参数变化、无需额外数据即可泛化到新参数区间的方法。

Method: 提出"方程重铸"框架：将参数化算子学习重构为单一规范（canonical）算子学习。参数诱导的算子变化从控制方程中解析推导，并被吸收到有效源项（effective sources）中，使原始问题的参数多样性归约到统一规范表示。同时利用迭代收敛性损失作为重铸过程失效的内部预警信号。

Result: 在多元参数、非线性及奇异PDE场景中，方程重铸实现了跨新参数区间的零样本外推；可将稀疏异质数据集整合进共享规范表示进行联合训练；在核聚变托卡马克仿真中，将四种装置几何的电子温度数据在规范域映射下统一于单一联合训练算子中。

Conclusion: 方程重铸为构建可复用神经PDE求解器提供了可行路径，其结合了方程引导迁移（equation-guided transfer）、数据效率以及可监控推理（monitorable inference），兼具泛化能力与可靠性。

Abstract: Learning solution operators across broad parameter ranges can require substantial coverage of both input functions and physical parameters, particularly for purely data-driven parametric models. In addition, the resulting models may fail silently outside the training distribution. We introduce equation recast, which reformulates parametric operator learning as the learning of a single canonical operator. Parameter-induced operator variations are derived analytically from the governing equation and absorbed into effective sources, enabling zero-shot prediction across new parameter regimes. Across multi-parameter, nonlinear, and singular PDE settings, equation recast supports extrapolation, integrates sparse heterogeneous datasets in a shared canonical representation, and uses loss of convergence as an internal warning signal for failure of the recast iteration. In high-fidelity tokamak simulations for nuclear fusion, the framework unifies electron-temperature data across four device geometries through canonical-domain mapping within one jointly trained operator. Equation recast provides a route toward reusable neural PDE solvers combining equation-guided transfer, data efficiency, and monitorable inference.

</details>


### [181] [From Euclidean to Graph-Structured Data: A Survey of Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02984)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇针对协作学习（联邦学习与去中心化学习）从欧几里得数据扩展到图结构数据的综合性综述，围绕学习有效性、效率与隐私保护三个核心维度展开，提出了图分布场景的分类体系、统计异质性刻画以及标准化的问题表述与算法框架，并系统梳理了开放挑战与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习（在单一位置完成数据收集、模型训练与推理）在可扩展性和隐私保护方面存在根本性局限，制约了其实际应用。协作学习通过多个智能体在本地进行训练和推理并辅以有限协作来应对这些挑战，但现有研究多聚焦于欧几里得数据（如图像、文本），难以捕捉许多真实世界应用中由图数据结构所表征的关系模式。图的基于消息传递的学习机制在概念上与需要信息交换的协作环境天然契合，因此图结构数据的协作学习机遇与挑战值得深入探究。

Method: 本文首先回顾协作学习在欧几里得数据上的基本原理，并按学习有效性、效率与隐私保护三个核心维度进行组织；随后将讨论延伸到图结构数据，提出图分布场景的分类体系，刻画相关的统计异质性，并建立标准化的问题表述与算法框架。

Result: 系统整合了从欧几里得数据到图结构数据的协作学习这一新兴领域，形成了涵盖基础原理、三维修炼框架、图分布场景分类与算法框架的综合体系，并归纳了当前面临的主要开放挑战与有前景的研究方向。

Conclusion: 该综述旨在巩固这一新兴研究领域，为图结构数据上的协作学习提供统一的视角；同时明确指出该方向仍存在大量未解决的难题（如统计异质性、隐私与效率的权衡等），并指引了未来研究的重点方向。

Abstract: The conventional approach to machine learning, that is, collecting data, training models, and performing inference in a single location, faces fundamental limitations, including scalability and privacy, that restrict its applicability. To address these challenges, recent research has explored collaborative learning approaches, including federated learning and decentralized learning, where individual agents perform training and inference locally, with limited collaboration. Most collaborative learning research focuses on Euclidean data with regular, grid-like structure (e.g., images, text). However, these approaches fail to capture the relational patterns in many real-world applications, best represented by graphs. Learning on graphs relies on message-passing mechanisms to propagate information between connected nodes, making it conceptually well-suited for collaborative environments where agents must exchange information. Yet, the opportunities and challenges of learning on graph-structured data in collaborative settings remain largely underexplored. This survey provides a comprehensive investigation of collaborative learning from Euclidean to graph-structured data, aiming to consolidate this emerging field. We begin by reviewing its foundational principles for Euclidean data, organizing them along three core dimensions: learning effectiveness, efficiency, and privacy preservation. We then extend the discussion to graph-structured data, introducing a taxonomy of graph distribution scenarios, characterizing associated statistical heterogeneities, and developing standardized problem formulations and algorithmic frameworks. Finally, we systematically identify open challenges and promising research directions.

</details>


### [182] [Modern Transformers Are Implicit Hybrids: From Functional Differentiation to Principled Hybrid Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02986)
*Runlin Shi,Bojian Yin,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种干预指标（RFIS与RPD），基于RoPE Transformer的头部级功能组织，发现了检索头与位置头之间存在由全局位置带（GPBand）界定的完整分类学。据此提出两条设计原则，并实例化为头部级混合架构（HwH）：用无位置编码的FA做全局检索、LA做局部位置建模。在FA:LA比例低于1:3时，HwH在保持语言建模与常识推理的同时，增强了检索并大幅改善了零样本长上下文外推能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全注意力（FA）与线性注意力（LA）的混合架构日益流行，但两者的分配仍是启发式的。作者希望从RoPE Transformer中头部级的功能组织出发，为FA与LA的分配寻找基于证据的、而非启发式的依据。

Method: 提出两种干预性指标：RoPE频率重要性分数（RFIS）衡量每个频率对头部注意力分布的影响；RoPE位置依赖性（RPD）隔离对旋转位置调制的依赖。在Qwen3系列与Llama3.1上验证了由显著的中低频带分隔的检索头与位置头的完整分类学。通过受控Transformer实验证实该分界随训练长度位置尺度变化，命名为全局位置带（GPBand），并据此设计头部级混合架构（HwH）。

Result: RFIS与RPD验证了检索头与位置头的完整分类学及其由GPBand划定的边界。HwH在FA:LA比例低于1:3时，保留较强的语言建模与常识推理能力，同时改进检索并显著增强零样本长上下文外推，优于Transformer、LA及层级混合基线。消融实验验证了两条原则及各组件的作用。

Conclusion: 分析揭示了零样本长度外推失败的潜在原因，提出两条原则：位置建模应仅局部进行、全局访问通过位置无关检索实现；两种功能应按头部粒度分配且具有层特定布局。这证明了原则驱动的混合架构设计是通向未来基础模型的有希望路径。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining Full Attention (FA) and Linear Attention (LA) are increasingly prominent, yet their allocation remains heuristic. We seek an evidence-grounded basis in head-level functional organization learned by RoPE-based Transformers. Behavioral probes do not yield a complete taxonomy, so we propose two intervention metrics: RoPE Frequency Importance Score (RFIS), measuring how each frequency affects a head's attention distribution, and RoPE Positional Dependence (RPD), isolating dependence on rotary positional modulation. On Qwen3-series models and Llama3.1, RFIS suggests and RPD verifies a complete taxonomy of retrieval and positional heads separated by a salient mid-low-frequency band. Controlled Transformers show that this boundary follows the training-length positional scale; we term it the Global Positional Band (GPBand). The analysis suggests a potential cause of zero-shot length-extrapolation failure and yields two principles: positional modeling should operate only locally, with global access through position-independent retrieval; and both functions should be assigned at head granularity with layer-specific allocation. We instantiate them in Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH), using NoPE FA for global retrieval and LA for local positional modeling. With an FA-to-LA ratio below 1:3, HwH retains strong language modeling and commonsense reasoning while improving retrieval and substantially strengthening zero-shot long-context extrapolation over Transformer, LA, and a layer-wise hybrid baseline. Ablations validate both principles and component roles, highlighting principled hybrid architecture design as a promising route toward future foundation models.

</details>


### [183] [Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02987)
*Shrinivas Ramasubramanian,Daman Arora,Fahim Tajwar,Guanning Zeng,Qingyang Wu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng,Yuda Song,Aarti Singh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,J. Andrew Bagnell,Jeff Schneider,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TailRL算法，通过直接优化奖励分布的上尾部（即超过随机选定奖励阈值的对数概率），替代传统的平均奖励优化，从而保留对稀有高奖励轨迹的概率质量，提升训练与推理阶段的采样效益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准强化学习优化平均奖励，但对生成策略而言，平均奖励无法区分期望相同但产生稀有高奖励轨迹概率不同的策略。随着训练和推理阶段采样数量的增加，其收益取决于策略是否保留了高奖励结果的概率质量，因此需要直接优化这种覆盖度而非仅依赖期望值。

Method: 将连续奖励转化为一族二值成功事件（每个奖励阈值对应一个事件）。TailRL最大化超过随机选取奖励阈值的对数概率，其梯度会给予稀有高奖励轨迹更高的权重，在数学上可解释为Best-of-(k)梯度的混合。实现上仅需对优势函数进行简单修改，即可无缝兼容现有强化学习流水线。

Result: 在物体定位、迷宫导航、GUI接地和代码优化等任务中，TailRL能够利用稀有高奖励训练样本避免陷入次优解，并训练出在推理阶段从额外采样中获得更大收益的模型。

Conclusion: TailRL通过上尾似然优化，显著增强了策略在稀有高奖励区间上的概率质量，提高了样本利用率与推理时的采样增益，且实现方式简单、易于部署到既有系统中。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically optimizes average reward. For generative policies, the average can hide an important distinction: two policies can achieve the same mean reward while having very different chances of producing a rare but high-reward rollout. This matters as sampling increases during training and inference, since its benefit depends on retaining probability mass on high-reward outcomes. We propose to optimize this coverage directly. Rather than considering only expected reward, we consider all of its upper tails: for each reward threshold, how likely is the policy to exceed it? This turns a continuous reward into a family of binary success events. We introduce Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL), which maximizes the log-probability of exceeding a randomly chosen reward threshold. Its gradient gives more weight to rare, high-reward rollouts and can be interpreted as a mixture of Best-of-(k) gradients. TailRL requires only a simple modification to the advantage function, making it compatible with existing reinforcement learning pipelines. Across object localization, maze navigation, GUI grounding, and code optimization, TailRL leverages rare high-reward training samples to avoid suboptimal solutions and yields models that benefit more from additional samples at inference time.

</details>


### [184] [Mesh-Native Physics-Informed Graph Surrogates for TCAD-in-the-Loop Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02988)
*Leonid Popryho,Ayoub Sadeghi,Inna Partin-Vaisband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息图注意力网络（GAT）替代模型，直接在TCAD四面体网格上预测漂移-扩散系统的三个基本未知量（静电势和电子/空穴准费米能级）。训练结合数据损失与有限体积电流连续性残差，嵌入载流子输运物理。该代理模型具有尺寸泛化能力，可用少鳍网格训练的模型直接应用于更大的阵列。深度集成提供逐节点不确定性，驱动主动学习循环快速筛选候选设计。在以Sentaurus Device为基准的多鳍三栅FinFET上，每个场的RMSE低于伏特级，吞吐量比全仿真高数个数量级，且优势随器件尺寸增大而增强，可在数秒内完成对大阵列的推理，支持跨器件规模的Pareto前沿探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真TCAD漂移-扩散仿真虽是FinFET器件设计的核心工具，但计算开销大，尤其在3D结构中随网格复杂度急剧上升，严重限制了多目标设计空间探索。现有机器学习代理仅将固定设计参数映射到少数标量指标，丢失了底层物理，且无法跨器件几何和族类迁移。因此需要一个保留物理场信息、可跨尺寸泛化的高效代理模型。

Method: 提出物理信息图注意力网络（GAT）代理：直接在四面体TCAD网格上操作（将网格视为图），预测每个网格节点上的静电势及电子、空穴准费米能级（漂移-扩散系统的基本未知量）。训练目标由数据损失和有限体积电流连续性残差组成，将载流子输运物理嵌入目标函数。以图为输入的机制使模型获得尺寸泛化能力；深度集成提供逐节点不确定性，驱动主动学习循环从大规模候选中快速筛选最有信息量的设计进行完整仿真。

Result: 与Sentaurus Device基准对比，在多鳍三栅FinFET上，模型能以亚伏特级每场RMSE复现三个漂移-扩散场，每设计吞吐量比全仿真器高数个数量级。优势随器件尺寸增大而增强：对于直接仿真代价过高的多鳍大阵列，推理仍在每器件1秒内完成，从而支持跨器件规模、直接TCAD扫描不可行的Pareto前沿探索。

Conclusion: 物理信息GAT代理能够以远低于全仿真的计算成本高保真地复现漂移-扩散场，且具备跨尺寸泛化和主动学习的可扩展性，将设计空间探索范围扩展到直接TCAD仿真不可行的器件规模，显著提升多鳍FinFET等器件的多目标设计优化效率。

Abstract: High-fidelity TCAD simulation of drift-diffusion transport remains the workhorse of emerging FinFET device design, but it is computationally expensive, especially for 3D structures where runtime escalates steeply with mesh complexity. This sharply limits multi-objective design space exploration. Existing machine-learning surrogates map a fixed set of design parameters to a few scalar device metrics, discarding the underlying physics and losing transferability across device geometries and families. A physics-informed graph attention network (GAT) surrogate is proposed. It operates directly on the tetrahedral TCAD mesh and predicts, at every mesh node, the electrostatic potential together with the electron and hole quasi-Fermi levels, the fundamental unknowns of the drift-diffusion system. Training combines a data loss with finite-volume current-continuity residuals, embedding carrier-transport physics into the objective. Operating on the mesh as a graph, the surrogate inherits size generalization: a model trained on few-fin meshes applies unchanged to substantially larger arrays, bounded at inference only by GPU memory. Per-node uncertainty from a deep ensemble drives an active-learning loop that screens large candidate pools in seconds and forwards only the most informative designs for full simulation. Benchmarked against Sentaurus Device on multi-fin tri-gate FinFETs, the surrogate reproduces the three drift-diffusion fields with sub-volt per-field RMSE and reaches a per-design throughput orders of magnitude higher than the full simulator. The advantage grows with device size: on large multi-fin arrays that are prohibitively slow to simulate directly, inference still completes in under a second per device, enabling Pareto-front exploration across device scales infeasible for direct TCAD sweeps.

</details>


### [185] [TRACE: Spatiotemporal Contact Memory Graph Network Simulator for Granular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02991)
*Changjian Zhou,Negin Yousefpour,Jie Qi,Junfeng Fang,Guillermo A. Narsilio,Hans Petter Jostad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 TRACE，一种将接触历史直接存储在接触边上的图网络模拟器。每条边利用注意力消息传递和门控循环单元维护持久化记忆，并通过边身份字典在接触图变化时保留该记忆；物理结构化解码器预测颗粒间法向/切向接触力、强制执行库仑摩擦极限并施加等大反向内力。在 2D 与 3D 颗粒柱坍塌基准上，相比 GNS 和 NMGNS，长程滚动位置误差降低 31-62%、最终沉积误差降低 58-89%，参数更少、穿透近乎为零，且相比 MPM 分别获得 12.2 倍和 8.9 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颗粒运动强烈依赖于颗粒间接触历史，而颗粒接触的形成、断裂与重排使得该历史难以保留。现有图网络模拟器主要将时间信息存储在节点特征或节点级记忆中，无法充分刻画随接触图动态变化的粒间相互作用，导致长时间滚动的物理一致性不足。

Method: TRACE 将交互历史直接存储于接触边：每条边维护由注意力消息传递和门控循环单元（GRU）更新的持久化记忆，并使用边身份字典在接触图重排时保持记忆对应关系。物理结构化解码器分别预测法向与切向接触力，强制满足库仑摩擦极限，并施加等大反向的内力以保证动量守恒。训练采用单步预训练加自回归滚动微调的两阶段策略。

Result: 在 2D 和 3D 颗粒柱坍塌基准上，TRACE 产生稳定、物理一致的长时间滚动，精确再现最终沉积几何与坍塌释放的动能。与 GNS 和 NMGNS 相比，长滚动位置误差降低 31-62%，最终沉积误差降低 58-89%，同时使用更少参数并维持接近零的颗粒互穿；对 MPM 参考求解器分别获得 12.2 倍与 8.9 倍速度提升。

Conclusion: 在接触边上持久化存储交互历史是提升颗粒动力学图模拟器精度与物理一致性的有效途径；TRACE 在精度、参数效率、无穿透约束与计算速度方面均优于现有节点式记忆模拟器，可作为高保真求解器的高效替代方案。

Abstract: Learned graph simulators provide an efficient alternative to high-fidelity solvers for granular dynamics. However, granular motion depends strongly on inter-granular contact history, which is difficult to preserve when particle contacts form, break, and rearrange. Existing simulators mainly store temporal information in node features or node-level memory. Here we introduce TRACE, a graph-network simulator that stores interaction history directly on contact edges. Each edge maintains a persistent memory updated by attention-based message passing and a gated recurrent unit, while an edge-identity dictionary preserves this memory as the contact graph changes. A physics-structured decoder predicts inter-granular normal and tangential contact forces, enforces the Coulomb friction limit, and applies equal-and-opposite internal forces. The model is trained with single-step pretraining followed by autoregressive rollout fine-tuning. We evaluate TRACE on 2D and 3D granular column-collapse benchmarks. In both cases, TRACE produces stable, physically consistent long-horizon rollouts, closely reproducing the final deposit geometry and the kinetic energy released during collapse. Compared with graph network simulator (GNS) and node-memory graph neural simulator (NMGNS), TRACE reduces long-rollout position error by 31-62% and final-deposit error by 58-89% across the two benchmarks, while using fewer parameters and maintaining near-zero particle interpenetration. TRACE also achieves 12.2$\times$ and 8.9$\times$ speedups over the material point method (MPM) reference solver in 2D and 3D, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/Data-Driven-Computational-Geotechnics/TRACE.

</details>


### [186] [No-Regret Bayesian Optimization with Finite-Library Input-Warped Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02993)
*Edvin Ketabati Augustinsson,Robert A. Bridges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出有限库输入扭曲贝叶斯优化（FLIWBO），它从有限库中选择平滑的输入映射来适配输入几何，加速学习，同时在高概率收敛保证下保持性能。实验表明FLIWBO-UCB在错误指定的几何下优于原始坐标GP-UCB，能避开陷阱并恢复手动对数缩放的大部分增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高斯过程贝叶斯优化（GP-BO）在昂贵函数的黑盒优化（如超参数优化和多智能体系统设计）中表现出色，但其收敛性保证（如GP-UCB）要求内核固定不变。然而内核编码了输入邻近性如何影响目标值相似性，当原始坐标与这一几何不匹配时（如对数缩放超参数或局部峰值），输入扭曲可大幅提升采样效率，但已知的GP-UCB证明要求固定内核，限制了方法的适用性。

Method: 提出FLIWBO方法，它从有限库的平滑输入映射中按任意依赖于历史记录的规则选择扭曲映射，以适配输入几何并加速学习，同时在温和假设下保留高概率收敛保证，并给出显式的√(N_ε)库大小代价。

Result: 受控诊断显示有限库扭曲能修复植入的几何失配并识别FLIWBO的失败案例。在四个重复基准上（扭曲合成目标、置信围栏陷阱、Fashion-MNIST超参数优化），FLIWBO-UCB在错误指定几何下优于原始坐标GP-UCB，能避开甚至击败oracle扭曲期望改进的陷阱，并恢复手动对数缩放的大部分增益，同时领先那些允许匹配遗憾保证的测试方法。20维多智能体系统设计研究进一步验证了在昂贵带噪评估下的可行性。

Conclusion: FLIWBO通过有限库输入扭曲在保持收敛保证的同时显著提升样本效率，解决了GP-UCB固定内核限制下输入几何失配的问题，且在实际场景（含高维MAS设计）中可行；代码已开源。

Abstract: Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization (GP-BO) excels at black-box optimization of costly functions, e.g., hyperparameter optimization (HPO) and multi-agent system (MAS) design. Convergence-rate guarantees exist for select methods, notably GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB), but require a fixed kernel. Critically, the kernel encodes how input proximity affects objective value similarity. When raw coordinates poorly match this geometry - as with log-scaled hyperparameters or localized peaks - input warping can greatly improve sample efficiency, yet known GP-UCB proofs require a fixed kernel. We propose Finite-Library Input-Warped Bayesian Optimization (FLIWBO), which selects warps from a finite library of smooth input maps by any history-dependent rule. It adapts the input geometry to accelerate learning while retaining high-probability convergence guarantees under mild hypotheses, with an explicit $\sqrt(N_\varepsilon)$ library-size cost. Controlled diagnostics show that finite-library warping repairs planted geometry mismatches and identify FLIWBO failure cases. Across four repeated benchmarks - warped synthetic objectives, a confidence-fence trap, and Fashion-MNIST HPO - FLIWBO-UCB beats raw-coordinate GP-UCB under misspecified geometry, escapes traps that defeat even oracle-warp expected improvement, and recovers much of the gain from manual log scaling, while leading the tested methods that admit a matching regret guarantee. A 20-dimensional MAS design study further shows feasibility under costly noisy evaluations. Code for experiments is available: https://github.com/edvin-ketabati/bogp-paper-experiments.

</details>


### [187] [Evaluating Graph Neural Networks for Change-Criticality Classification in Maritime Navigation Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02996)
*Abhishek Potnis,Jacob Arndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将电子海图（ENC）空间矢量数据表示为图结构，其中空间对象作为节点、空间与语义关系作为边，将旧版与新版本ENC构建成图对，并将ENB更新分类任务框架化为图对分类问题，用于判断变更对航行安全是否构成关键风险。通过训练多种GNN架构，实验表明图表示方法能有效提升ENC更新分类的准确性，为自动化工件维护提供可扩展方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子海图（ENC）作为海洋导航所用的地理空间矢量数据，其维护是一个挑战，特别是在根据对航行安全的重要性对ENC对象变更进行分类方面尤为重要。然而，目前尚不清楚哪种消息传递操作、架构配置和图形表示最适合对ENC中对象变更进行分类，因此需要探索GNN在此任务上的应用。

Method: 将ENC矢量数据表示成图结构：空间对象作为节点，空间关系和语义关系构成边。将旧版和新版ENC各编码为一个图，构成图对（graph pair），并将任务表述为图对分类问题。在此表示基础上，训练和评估多种GNN架构及模型配置，对ENC更新是否构成关键或非关键的航行安全风险进行分类，数据集由海事专家审校过的ENC变更构成。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于图的表示方法能够提升ENC更新分类的准确性，提供了自动化或改进ENC维护工作流的可扩展方法。

Conclusion: 图结构与GNN相结合的方法在ENC变更分类任务上表现优于现有方法，为自动化ENC维护提供了可扩展途径，验证了将空间/语义关系编码为图所带来的分类性能提升。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are a class of neural networks suitable for learning on graph-structured data. Their application to spatial data is a natural extension, however its relatively unclear which message-passing operations, architectural configurations, and graph representation is best suited for classifying changes to objects in electronic navigational charts (ENCs)--geospatial vector datasets used for marine navigation. Maintaining these datasets is a challenge, and categorizing changes to objects in the ENC based on their significance to navigational safety is of particular importance. Here, we propose to represent these vector navigation datasets as a graph structure where the spatial objects serve as nodes and their spatial and semantic relationships form edges. We encode both the old ENC dataset and new ENC dataset into a pair of graphs and frame the task as a graph-pair classification problem. Building on this representation, we investigate the use of GNN architectures to classify whether the encoded graphs constitutes a critical or non-critical risk to navigational safety. We train and evaluate several GNN architectures and model configurations on ENC changes reviewed by maritime experts. Our results demonstrate that graph-based representations improve the classification of ENC updates, providing a scalable approach for automating or improving ENC maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [188] [Verify Before You Distill: Prompt-Level Teacher Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02998)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Zechen Sun,Fei Zhao,Kang Peng,Bin Liang,Huayu Deng,Yao Hu,Kam-Fai Wong,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD)，针对朴素OPD对所有提示统一施加密集教师监督而忽视教师可靠性的缺陷，在提示级验证教师可靠性：先用少量验证器打分的教师探针估计可靠性，通过检查的提示走密集OPD，未通过的则由验证器锚定的GRPO处理。在4B和35B规模、数学/代码/指令跟随任务上，TGOPD在全部六个单领域场景和两个规模的多领域训练中都优于朴素OPD，并将教师侧GPU利用率从9.8%提升至78.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 朴素On-Policy Distillation (OPD)对所有提示不加区分地施加来自冻结教师的密集token级监督，但未检查教师对每个提示是否可靠。由于反向KL具有模式寻求特性，一个自信但错误的教师会产生强大而误导性的更新。虽有熵或师生似然一致等分布性代理指标可度量不确定性或一致性，但它们并未直接验证结果正确性，因此需要一种在提示级直接验证教师可靠性的机制。

Method: TGOPD建立在"在准入密集监督之前应在提示级验证教师可靠性"的原则之上。它用一小部分由验证器打分的教师生成探针来估计提示级可靠性；当可靠性检查通过时，该提示被路由至密集OPD；否则路由至基于验证器grounding的GRPO。此外，TGOPD利用原本空闲的教师容量做可靠性估计，减少异步OPD中教师侧的计算浪费。

Result: 在数学、代码和指令跟随任务上，4B与35B两个规模的学生模型上，TGOPD在全部六个单领域设置中均优于朴素OPD，并在两种规模的多领域训练下取得更高的七个基准平均值。在实测的4B单领域运行中，教师节点GPU利用率从9.8%提升至78.9%。

Conclusion: 在提示级Gate教师可靠性再决定是否采用密集OPD监督，可避免自信错误的教师产生误导性更新，同时复用空闲教师计算以提升资源利用率。TGOPD在多种规模与任务领域上一致优于朴素OPD，验证了"先验证可靠性再准入密集监督"这一思路的有效性与可扩展性。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) accelerates post-training by providing dense token-level supervision from a frozen teacher on the student's own rollouts. Vanilla OPD applies this supervision uniformly across prompts, without checking whether the teacher is reliable for each prompt. Because reverse KL is mode-seeking, a confidently wrong teacher can induce a strong yet misleading update. Distributional proxies, such as entropy or teacher-student likelihood agreement, measure uncertainty or agreement but do not directly verify outcome correctness. We introduce Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD), built on the principle that teacher reliability should be verified at the prompt level before dense supervision is admitted. TGOPD estimates reliability from a small set of verifier-scored teacher probes and routes each prompt exclusively to dense OPD when the reliability check passes or to verifier-grounded GRPO otherwise. Across 4B and 35B students in mathematics, code, and instruction following, TGOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD in all six single-domain settings and achieves higher seven-benchmark averages at both scales under multi-domain training. By using otherwise-idle teacher capacity for reliability estimation, TGOPD also reduces teacher-side compute waste in asynchronous OPD, increasing teacher-node GPU utilization from 9.8% to 78.9% in the measured 4B single-domain run.

</details>


### [189] [Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03003)
*Christopher Stith,Hossein Rahmani,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了因果基础模型（CFMs）——一种将基础模型范式引入因果推断的新兴方法：通过预训练网络，在全新数据集上利用上下文学习估计因果量（如平均处理效应），无需微调或更新模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断需要为每个新问题定制流程（提出因果机制、选择估计器、训练模型），效率低下且难以跨场景泛化；而机器学习已广泛采用预训练基础模型范式，因果推断尚未充分受益于此，因此需要引入CFMs。

Method: 文章作为实践性导论，先总结因果推断和机器学习所需背景知识，然后系统阐述CFMs的概念、预训练方式及应用方式，并附带示例代码和Jupyter笔记本。

Result: 给出了CFMs的实用介绍，展示了如何利用预训练网络在无需模型更新的条件下，通过上下文学习对新数据集直接进行因果量估计。

Conclusion: CFMs将基础模型范式扩展到因果推断，有望简化因果分析流程，提升跨数据集的可迁移性与应用效率；本文为研究者提供了入门指导和实践资源。

Abstract: Causal inference is the practice of estimating the effect of a treatment or intervention from data. It traditionally requires a bespoke pipeline for every new problem: first proposing a causal mechanism, selecting a compatible estimator, and finally training it. Meanwhile, across diverse settings and modalities, much of machine learning has shifted to the paradigm of foundation models: networks pretrained once at scale and applied to new tasks without fine-tuning. Causal foundation models (CFMs) bring this paradigm to causal inference. CFMs are pretrained neural networks that estimate causal quantities, such as the average treatment effect, on entirely new datasets using in-context learning without requiring model updates. This work provides a practical introduction to this emerging area. We summarize the necessary background in causal inference and machine learning before discussing CFMs. Throughout, we include example code and Jupyter notebooks.

</details>


### [190] [ObserverBench: Testing Mechanistic Estimates for Intervention and Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03026)
*Vijay Erramilli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ObserverBench 是一个用于检验可解释性方法内部估计器（observer）是否足以支撑其下游干预、控制或安全任务的基准框架。该框架为每项任务固定模型、信息边界、允许动作、决策规则、留出样本与损失，并分别报告估计准确率与所选动作造成的损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性机制（mechanistic interpretability）日益被用于引导干预（activation steering、电路移除、安全监控），但一个平均意义上准确的内部估计仍可能选择糟糕的动作。因此需要一种机制来评估内部估计器是否真正适用于其所驱动的任务，而不仅仅是估计是否准确。

Method: 提出 ObserverBench 基准框架：为每个任务签订固定契约（模型、信息边界、允许动作、决策规则、留出案例、损失函数），将估计准确率与动作引起的损失分开报告。结合理论分析与实验：在闭环控制中分析误差在起点和干预可达方向上的影响；在 GPT-2-small 和 Qwen2.5-7B 上做电路干预任务；在 Qwen2.5-7B、Gemma-2-9B-it 及前瞻性冻结的 Qwen3.5-9B APPS 任务上做安全分诊比较。

Result: 理论与实验均表明估计准确率与动作损失需同时报告：成对观察者在预测未见效应上更准，但不总能选择更优动作，而用动作损失训练的观察者会选择更低损失的动作；在安全分诊中，能完美区分违规的分数在面对不同成本的违规时可能分配不佳；AUROC 对监控者的排序可能与部署损失不同，且最佳信息来源随模型变化；稀疏 SAE 读取在公开的 Qwen 面板上落后于层匹配的密集控制。

Conclusion: ObserverBench 通过固定的任务契约、可运行的基线和基于表格的提交机制，用可解释性方法实际启用的动作来评估其质量。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability is increasingly used to guide interventions such as activation steering, circuit removal, and safety monitoring. Yet an internal estimate that is accurate on average can still choose a poor action.
  We present ObserverBench, a benchmark framework for testing whether an internal estimator---an observer---is adequate for the intervention, control, or safety task it directs. Each task fixes the model, information boundary, allowed actions, decision rule, held-out cases, and loss. The benchmark reports estimation accuracy separately from the loss caused by the chosen action.
  Theory and experiments show why both are needed. In closed-loop control, observer errors matter at the starting point and along directions the allowed intervention can reach. On circuit-intervention tasks in GPT-2-small and Qwen2.5-7B, pairwise observers predict unseen effects more accurately without always choosing better actions; observers trained on action loss choose lower-loss actions. In safety triage, a score that perfectly separates violations can allocate a fixed intervention budget poorly when violations have different costs. Across Qwen2.5-7B, Gemma-2-9B-it, and prospectively frozen Qwen3.5-9B APPS tasks, AUROC can rank monitors differently from deployment loss, and the best information source changes across models. Sparse SAE readouts also trail their layer-matched dense controls on the reported Qwen panels, under disclosed activation-density or checkpoint mismatches.
  ObserverBench provides fixed task contracts, runnable baselines, and table-based submissions for evaluating interpretability methods through the actions they enable.

</details>


### [191] [Learnable composition for neural operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03069)
*Zituo Chen,Baiming Zhang,Sili Deng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LatentDDM通过先在小子域上预训练神经算子，再冻结该算子仅训练轻量级组合模块，实现跨几何/尺寸/工况的高效迁移，显著降低目标域适配成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经算子在物理仿真中作为可微代理，但当域几何、尺寸或工况变化时精度下降；有监督适配虽可恢复精度，但即使小目标集也需要昂贵的高保真仿真，因此需要协同设计预训练与迁移来降低部署成本。

Method: LatentDDM先在小子域上预训练神经算子预测场；面对新设定时冻结该算子，仅训练一个轻量级模块来组合局部预测。

Result: 在稳态Darcy流（大孔隙域）上，与容量匹配的全域模型相比，LatentDDM在16个目标仿真适配后误差降低36-56%；在快速俯仰翼型非定常不可压缩流中，20步场滚动预测在零样本和少样本校准下均有提升。

Conclusion: 局部预训练与组合级迁移的协同设计是物理基础模型的有前景原则。

Abstract: Neural operators are fast, differentiable surrogates for physical simulation, but their accuracy often degrades when domain geometry, size, or operating conditions differ from training. Supervised adaptation can recover accuracy, but even a small target set requires costly high-fidelity simulations. We therefore ask how pretraining and transfer can be designed together to reduce this deployment cost. LatentDDM first pretrains a neural operator to predict fields on small subdomains. For a new setting, it freezes this operator and trains only a lightweight module that composes the local predictions. We evaluate our method on two complementary problems: steady Darcy flow, where long-range pressure coupling must extend across increasingly large porous domains, and unsteady incompressible flow around a pitching airfoil, where rollout errors compound as target pitching frequencies exceed the training range. Compared with the capacity-matched models that process the full domain at once, LatentDDM's error is 36-56% lower on larger Darcy domains after adaptation with 16 target simulations. It also improves 20-step field rollouts in fast-pitching airfoil flow, both zero-shot and after few-shot calibration. These results identify the co-designed local pretraining and composition-level transfer as a promising design principle for physical foundation models.

</details>


### [192] [LeanStream: A Speculate-and-Refine Streaming Framework for Efficient on-Device LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03079)
*Renyuan Liu,Yuyang Leng,Kaiyan Liu,Yuzhou Zhong,Shaohan Hu,Chun-Fu,Chen,Peijun Zhao,Heechul Yun,Shuochao Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LeanStream是一种面向端侧LLM推理的流式推测与修正（speculate-and-refine）框架，通过利用部分GPU结果渐进式地细化计算、加载与缓存驻留优先级，实现了GPU计算与存储I/O的细粒度重叠，在移动和嵌入式平台上相比现有系统将内存占用降低4.8×至7.5×，同时将Token生成吞吐提升1.6×至2.1×。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端侧LLM推理具有隐私和响应速度优势，但模型权重远超可用DRAM。现有系统利用激活稀疏性并将权重卸载到SSD或闪存，却面临一个根本性系统权衡：准确的稀疏执行决策需要最新上下文，而高效的计算-I/O重叠需要提前预测。这导致现有设计要么串行化执行，要么产生冗余权重复取、额外计算和大量缓存开销。

Method: LeanStream采用流式推测与修正机制：利用部分GPU执行结果（partial GPU results）渐进式地细化计算决策、权重加载决策和缓存驻留优先级，从而在GPU计算和存储I/O之间实现细粒度重叠，避免对最新上下文的提前预测依赖，兼顾决策准确性与执行效率。

Result: 在移动和嵌入式两种平台上实现并评估。相比现有端侧LLM推理系统，在达到已有系统最佳吞吐时，LeanStream将内存使用量降低4.8×至7.5×；同时进一步将Token生成吞吐提升1.6×至2.1×，在内存效率与吞吐性能两端均取得显著提升。

Conclusion: LeanStream通过推测-修正架构有效化解了稀疏执行决策准确性与计算-I/O重叠效率之间的系统级矛盾，证明了在资源受限的移动/嵌入式设备上实现高效、低内存的端侧LLM推理是可行的，为该领域提供了新的系统设计范式。

Abstract: On-device LLM inference is attractive for privacy and responsiveness, but remains challenging on mobile and embedded devices because model weights far exceed available DRAM. Prior systems exploit activation sparsity and offload weights to SSD or flash storage, but face a fundamental systems trade-off: accurate sparse execution decisions require the latest context, whereas efficient computation-I/O overlap requires early prediction. As a result, existing designs either serialize execution or incur redundant weight fetches, extra computation, and large cache overheads. We present LeanStream, a streaming speculate-and-refine framework for efficient on-device LLM inference. LeanStream progressively refines computation, loading, and cache-retention priorities using partial GPU results, enabling fine-grained overlap between GPU execution and storage I/O. We implement LeanStream on both mobile and embedded platforms. Compared with prior on-device LLM inference systems, LeanStream reduces memory usage by 4.8$\times$ to 7.5$\times$ at the best throughput achieved by prior work, while further improving token generation throughput by 1.6$\times$ to 2.1$\times$.

</details>


### [193] [The Gradient Does Not See Rank: Rank-Indifference in Matrix-CODI on ProsQA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03090)
*Samuel Larson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究矩阵CODI模型中"秩"作为潜在矩阵Z的逐样本结构可观测量的作用。实验表明，在四种训练制度下，秩-k投影消融曲线近乎平坦（偏差在0.6个百分点内），且4种非线性读出器（双线性、双线性+GELU、SVD增强、二次型）的秩-k曲线也保持平坦。线性探针表现不及原始预训练隐藏状态。负对照实验（vanilla GPT-2 SFT）同样重现平坦曲线，证明秩-k消融本身将"秩盲"与"位置无关性"混为一谈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续思维链（CoT）模型将推理压缩为潜在token，矩阵变体通过d×d矩阵瓶颈路由每个潜在token，从而将"秩"引入为潜在矩阵Z的逐样本结构可观测量。研究者假设若矩阵潜在携带叠加的并行推理路径，秩应能表征它们，且将Z截断至低秩应会损害那些需要多组件解的任务的精度。该工作旨在检验这一假设。

Method: 在矩阵-CODI模型的四种训练制度（三种在ProsQA上，一种在GSM8K-Aug上低于学习阈值）下进行秩-k投影消融实验，并进行三种子重复（81.0±2.0个百分点精度，Z的有效秩跨{4,12,13}）。为检验秩盲是否源于"展平-投影"读出方式，训练了四种读出器：双线性重参数化、双线性+GELU非线性读出、SVD增强读出（将奇异值经MLP）、Z Z^T二次型读出，均绘制秩-k曲线并计算Spearman p值。另设线性探针与负对照（vanilla GPT-2 SFT）实验。

Result: 秩-k投影消融曲线在所有四种训练制度中平坦，偏差在0.6个百分点内。四种非线性读出器的秩-k曲线均保持平坦（Spearman p值分别为0.63、0.14、0.82、0.46）。线性探针在目标预测上表现不佳（AUC 0.673 vs. 0.846）。负对照在GPT-2 SFT上重现平坦秩-k曲线（池化均值范围0.20pp），随机-h敏感度下限落在相同精度上。

Conclusion: 矩阵潜在结构中的秩并不跟踪并行推理路径，损失函数不奖励任何特定秩。秩盲现象不仅源于展平-投影读出，即便读出器对Z非线性也依然存在。更关键的是，负对照实验表明秩-k消融方法本身无法区分"秩无关性"与"位置无关性"，该干预范式需要修正才能正确归因矩阵潜在结构的功能。

Abstract: Continuous chain-of-thought models compress reasoning into latent tokens. Matrix-valued variants, which route each latent token through a d x d matrix bottleneck, introduce rank as a single-sample structural observable on the latent matrix Z. If matrix latents carry parallel reasoning paths via superposition, rank should track them, and truncating Z to low rank should hurt accuracy on tasks whose solutions plausibly require multiple components. Across four training regimes of a matrix-CODI model (three on ProsQA, one on GSM8K-Aug below the learning threshold), the rank-k projection ablation curve is flat to within 0.6 percentage points. A three-seed replication yields 81.0 +/- 2.0 percentage points accuracy while the final effective rank of Z spans {4, 12, 13}; the loss does not reward any particular rank. To test whether rank-blindness arises from the flatten-then-project readout alone, we trained four readouts: a bilinear reparametrization, a bilinear-plus-GELU readout nonlinear in Z, an SVD-augmented readout feeding singular values through an MLP, and a quadratic readout in Z Z^T. All four rank-k curves remain flat (Spearman p-values 0.63, 0.14, 0.82, 0.46). The flat curves persist for readouts nonlinear in Z. A linear probe on Z underperforms a raw pretrained hidden state at target prediction (AUC 0.673 vs. 0.846). A negative control on vanilla GPT-2 SFT (no matrix bottleneck, no Z, three seeds, n=500) reproduces a flat rank-k curve under the same intervention paradigm with pooled-mean range 0.20pp, and a random-h sensitivity floor lands at the same accuracy: the rank-k ablation alone conflates rank-blindness with position-irrelevance.

</details>


### [194] [Distilling deep optical flow stereo methods to retrieve dense three-dimensional wind fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03100)
*Thomas J. Vandal,Dong L. Wu,James L. Carr,Derek J. Posselt,Elise Penn,Tristan Ballard,August Posch,Kate Duffy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究提出用深度光流替代传统窗口相关进行立体匹配，用于地球静止卫星大气运动矢量（AMV）反演，并通过知识蒸馏实现单卫星全盘风场反演，验证表明在水汽波段性能优于业务AMV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统AMV依赖窗口相关和红外亮温配对NWP背景估计高度，存在循环依赖、精度不高、计算量大、覆盖稀疏等问题；立体风虽能几何解算高度，但计算开销大且需多卫星重叠覆盖。

Method: 用深度光流替代窗口相关进行立体匹配，通过自监督几何残差损失与有监督无线电探空重建的联合微调平衡精度；将立体教师模型蒸馏为单卫星学生模型，消除多卫星重叠需求，并模拟教师的卡方和高度不确定性用于质量控制。

Result: 三重配置验证显示，立体风在水汽波段（6.2、6.9和7.3 μm）优于业务AMV，但在长波红外（11.2 μm）波段有所退化；单卫星学生模型可在全球全盘GEO影像上生成风场。

Conclusion: 深度光流与知识蒸馏结合可提升AMV反演效率与覆盖范围，同时保持或改善水汽波段精度，但长波红外波段仍需进一步优化。

Abstract: Geostationary atmospheric motion vectors (AMVs) provide the dense horizontal wind vectors (u,v) and heights ingested into data assimilation systems. Traditional AMVs track features using window-based cross-correlation and estimate heights via infrared brightness temperatures paired with numerical weather prediction (NWP) background states, creating a circular dependency that yields inaccurate heights, high computational cost, and sparse retrievals. Stereo winds from GEO-GEO and GEO-LEO geometrically resolve heights from parallax shifts across different poses, eliminating NWP dependence and improving accuracy, but they remain computationally heavy with limited coverage. In this work, we replace window-based tracking in stereo matching with deep optical flow for efficient, improved retrieval. Fine-tuning balances a self-supervised geometric residual loss with supervised radiosonde reconstruction. To eliminate multi-satellite overlap requirements, we distill the stereo teacher into a single-satellite student model. Chi-square and height uncertainties from the teacher are emulated by the student for quality assurance. The student generates winds across full-disk GEO imagery globally. Validation compares stereo and student models against radiosondes, operational AMVs, ERA5 reanalysis, and EarthCARE cloud profiles. Results through triple collocation show that stereo winds improve performance beyond operational AMVs for water vapor bands (6.2, 6.9, and 7.3 μm), wit degradation in the long-wave infrared (11.2 μm) band.

</details>


### [195] [Scaling Laws, Tabular Data and Actuarial Ratemaking Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03106)
*Ronald Richman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探究了深度学习的缩放定律（scaling laws）是否适用于精算费率厘定场景。作者使用真实机动车保险组合数据，在训练数据比例递增和多随机种子条件下训练多种模型家族，以泊松偏差（Poisson deviance）评估样本外表现。研究发现TabM表现出明显优于监督式表格Transformer和标准MLP基准的数据缩放能力；Transformer变体在参数缩放方面较弱，除非引入额外归纳偏置（TabM风格适配或自监督）。这为按数据规模选择模型提供了量化依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代深度学习存在启发性的缩放定律——随着模型容量、训练数据和算力增加，留出损失常以幂律趋势改善。但此类规律是否存在于精算费率厘定中尚不明确。精算数据具有表格化、异构、高噪声特点，且经典GLM模型仍是强基线。作者旨在检验该领域是否同样存在缩放规律性，从而为实务中的模型选择提供依据。

Method: 基于真实机动车保险组合数据，训练多个不同模型家族（TabM、监督式表格Transformer、标准MLP、Transformer变体等），在递增的训练数据比例和多个随机种子下进行评估。采用样本外泊松偏差（基于似然的损失，针对泊松计数预测，数值越低表示留出拟合越好）作为评价指标，比较各模型的数据缩放指数（scaling exponents）与参数缩放表现。

Result: 所有模型家族均随数据量增加而改善，但缩放指数差异显著：TabM的数据缩放明显强于监督式表格Transformer和标准MLP基准；Transformer变体参数缩放较弱，除非辅以额外归纳偏置（TabM式适配或自监督）。单纯增加Transformer规模带来的收益有限。

Conclusion: 在精算表格任务上，有效的缩放取决于架构和损失函数目标设计，而非简单扩大模型容量。研究为按数据规模进行模型选择提供了定量指导，指出架构层面的归纳偏置是提升缩放效率的关键。

Abstract: Scaling laws in modern deep learning describe how held-out loss improves as model capacity, training data, and compute increase, often following power-law trends. We investigate whether analogous scaling regularities arise in actuarial ratemaking, where data are tabular, heterogeneous, and noisy, and where classical models such as GLMs remain strong baselines. Using a real-world motor insurance portfolio, we train models from different families across increasing fractions of the training data and multiple random seeds, evaluating out-of-sample Poisson deviance, a likelihood-based loss for Poisson count predictions in which lower values indicate better held-out fit. We find that all model families improve with additional data, but scaling exponents differ substantially: TabM exhibits markedly stronger data scaling than purely supervised tabular Transformers and standard MLP baselines. Transformer variants show weak parameter scaling unless augmented with additional inductive biases (TabM-style adaptation or self-supervision). These results provide quantitative guidance on model selection by data regime and suggest that effective scaling on actuarial tabular tasks depends on architecture and loss function objective design, with simple increases in Transformer size providing limited gains.

</details>


### [196] [Kernel Reboot: Breaking the Boundaries of Neural Tangent Kernels for Neural Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03117)
*Amir Mallak,Alaa Maalouf,Lior Wolf,Daniela Rus,Dan Rosenbaum*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出三种算法（NTK-KIP、MetaQuill、MetaQuill-KIP），将神经正切核（NTK）回归与元学习结合，使神经场（NFs）能够从稀疏观测中快速高质量重建，实现非线性、可复用先验的少样本重建与修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经场将连续坐标映射到颜色或密度等信号，但稀疏观测下高质量重建困难；经典NTK回归可提供闭式拟合，但其本质为线性，无法积累可复用的任务先验，因此需要开发新算法弥补这些不足。

Method: 提出三种算法：NTK-KIP学习蒸馏支持集坐标（及可选标签），使有限NTK能从小观测数据修复大缺失区域，获得紧凑非线性表示；MetaQuill元学习隐式神经表示（INR）的共享初始化，新场景仅更新小任务特定权重偏移，实现特征学习与可复用先验；MetaQuill-KIP融合两者：以KIP风格非线性热启动作为任务种子，再围绕元学习初始化仅细化小偏移。

Result: MetaQuill-KIP在极稀疏观测下取得高PSNR重建和语义合理修复，仅需轻量级每实例适应；相比依赖大型预训练生成先验和昂贵逐图像调优的扩散式基线，该方法更具高效性。

Conclusion: NTK驱动的神经场可以同时实现非线性和元学习，缩小了解析核与实际少样本重建之间的差距，为稀疏观测下的快速高质量重建提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Neural fields (NFs) map continuous coordinates to signals such as color or density, but fast high-quality reconstruction from sparse observations remains difficult.
