Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) become increasingly capable, the long-term value of AI systems depends not only on solving individual requests, but also on transforming experience and accumulated knowledge into durable, reusable competence. We introduce SimSkill, a self-evolving agent built around the Simulation of Urban MObility (SUMO) traffic simulator. SimSkill identifies capability gaps, generates and solves environment-grounded tasks, verifies solutions through an action--critic loop, and consolidates experience into episodic, procedural, and semantic memory without updating the backbone model. Through autonomous exploration, it builds a reusable library spanning the traffic-simulation workflow. We evaluate SimSkill on two held-out benchmarks with three backbone LLMs and independent artifact-based verification. SimSkill improves verified completion by up to 25 percentage points, while ablations show complementary contributions from procedural and semantic memory. Its benefits remain backbone- and budget-dependent: memory does not improve every model or uniformly reduce inference cost. More broadly, SimSkill illustrates a design paradigm in which natural language preserves and composes computational capabilities, while executable tools and code provide precise and reproducible execution. All code and experimental data are publicly available at https://github.com/qiliuchn/SimSkill-V1.

</details>


### [294] [Rethinking World Models for Safety-Critical Embodied Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03774)
*Kailang Ma,Heye Huang,Inhi Kim,Kitae Jang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该观点文章指出现有世界模型存在三个结构性错配（似然vs风险、预测vs干预、有限时间预测vs累积后果），并提出以决策为中心、面向安全关键具身系统的"风险知情世界模型"（RIWM）研究方向，呼吁未来世界模型从预测可能未来转向识别哪些未来重要，并在经验中修正判断、识别何时行动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型已从紧凑潜动力学演变为具身环境的生成式、可控、交互式模拟器，但高预测似然与视觉保真度并不能保证模型保留安全决策所需的证据。作者指出当前世界建模在安全决策方面存在根本性缺陷，需要转向面向风险与后果的建模范式。

Method: 论文采取观点/展望式论证方法：首先识别三个结构性错配——似然与风险、预测与干预、有限时域预测与累积后果；随后提出RIWM框架，围绕后果、干预、认知不确定性、可恢复性四大支柱组织世界建模，并整合四项相互依赖的能力（决策相关表征、反事实推理、安全关键情景记忆、运行时安全保证）。RIWM区分物理、社会与操作三类后果，并利用认知不确定性限定支持行动的证据。

Result: 提出并阐述了Risk-Informed World Model（RIWM）这一决策中心的研究方向，明确了其四大支柱与四项相互依赖能力，并系统梳理了开放挑战：识别后果性未来、验证反事实推理、维护可修正的安全记忆、将学习到的后果转化为可执行约束，以及确定证据何时足以行动。

Conclusion: 未来世界模型应超越预测"可能的未来"，转向识别"哪些未来重要"，通过经验修订判断，并能识别行动、修订、感知、延迟或放弃的时机——即以风险知情的方式服务于安全关键的具身决策。

Abstract: World models have progressed from compact latent dynamics to generative, controllable, and interactive simulators of embodied environments. However, high predictive likelihood and visual fidelity do not necessarily ensure that a model preserves the evidence required for safe decision-making. This perspective identifies three structural mismatches in current world modeling: likelihood versus risk, prediction versus intervention, and finite-horizon prediction versus accumulated consequences. We propose the Risk-Informed World Model (RIWM) as a decision-centric research direction for safety-critical embodied systems. RIWM organizes world modeling around consequences, intervention, epistemic uncertainty, and recoverability, and integrates four interdependent capabilities: decision-relevant representation, counterfactual reasoning, safety-critical episodic memory, and runtime safety assurance. It distinguishes physical, social, and operational consequences while using epistemic uncertainty to qualify the evidence supporting action. We further discuss open challenges in identifying consequential futures, validating counterfactual reasoning, maintaining revisable safety memories, translating learned consequences into executable constraints, and determining when evidence is sufficient to act. This perspective argues that future world models should move beyond predicting likely futures toward identifying which futures matter, revising judgments through experience, and recognizing when to act, revise, sense, defer, or abstain.

</details>


### [295] [DNative-Twin: Decision Graphs and Digital Twins for Reconstructable Agentic Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03787)
*Junjie Pang,Zhenzhen Xie,Haoke Han,Ying He,Jing Wang,Gang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DNative-Twin，一个图原生数字孪生框架，它将代理驱动的人工智能决策记录为带类型的轨迹，并可在受控条件下重新执行其决策机制，从而将图结构、回放上下文和验证证据在决策审查中的角色分离开来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能代理越来越多地收集证据、调用工具、应用约束并产生最终决策，但单一的最终输出无法揭示其背后的依据（证据、工具状态、规则、授权或行动路径）。这导致对决策可信度与可审计性的担忧，亟需一种能够记录并复审决策机制的方法。

Method: 提出了DNative-Twin，一个图原生数字孪生：(1) 将已提交的代理决策记录为带类型的轨迹；(2) 将代理观测的状态、其路径以及导致行动的权限在图结构中关联起来；(3) 同步这些信息，在隔离环境中回放决策机制，并在受控变更下进行比较。在三组条件下（仅图结构、加入回放契约状态、再加入验证结果），对300个注入实例进行受控实验，并在BPI 2020流程日志上评估扩展性。

Result: 识别出具体失败模式：图结构能定位所表示的变化，但无法确定未观测工具状态带来的后果。在300个注入实例的三条件对照实验中，未解决分歧的召回率从0提升到0.667（加入回放契约状态）再提升至1.0（加入验证结果）；保留测试集中无关键类实例。在500-5000个BPI 2020案例上，中位端到端时间从0.794秒增加到8.889秒（在报告平台上）。

Conclusion: 实验结果清楚地将图结构、回放上下文和验证证据在决策机制审查中的角色相互区分开，确认仅靠图结构不足以推断未观测工具状态的后果，而增加回放契约与验证信息可显著增强对分歧的检出能力。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly gather evidence, invoke tools, apply constraints, and produce decisions that people or software may commit to action. A final output alone cannot show which evidence, tool state, rule, authorization, or action path produced it. We present DNative-Twin, a graph-native digital twin that records a committed agentic decision as a typed trajectory and re-executes its decision mechanism under declared conditions. The graph links the state observed by the agent, the path it followed, and the authority behind the resulting action. The twin synchronizes this information, replays the mechanism in isolation, and compares it under controlled changes. We instantiate the framework in enterprise decision processes using three public process logs and controlled replay suites. The experiments identify a specific failure: graph structure localizes represented changes but cannot determine the consequence of an unobserved tool state. In a three-condition controlled experiment with 300 injected instances, unresolved-divergence recall increased from 0 to 0.667 when replay-contract state was added and to 1.0 when verification results were also available; the held-out set contained no critical-class instance. Across 500--5,000 BPI 2020 cases, median end-to-end time increased from 0.794 to 8.889 seconds on the reported platform. These results separate the roles of graph structure, replay context, and verification evidence in reviewing a decision mechanism.

</details>


### [296] [Transfiver: Human-AI Co-Inference through a Shared Editable State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03797)
*Minji Park,Seunghyun Yoon,Hyuk Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Transfiver是一种用于人机协同推理的架构，通过一个持久共享状态(S_t)让模型和人类都能直接更新，从而解决长期交互中推理信息不可见、不可控制的问题。它将状态演化分为隐式流更新和显式定向编辑两种模式，二者作用于同一底层状态，使人类修正能直接影响后续计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期人机交互困难，因为引导推理的信息由模型隐式更新，用户无法直接检查或控制。这种不透明性限制了用户的参与和信任，需要一种可检查、可验证、可编辑的共享表示机制。

Method: 提出Transfiver架构：维护一个持久状态S_t，模型和人类都能更新。隐式流更新中模型解读交互并决定修订或新建状态项；显式定向编辑中人类检查并修改指定项。架构将预训练共享参数θ与部署期演化的持久状态S_t分离，无需参数重训练。

Result: 该架构使得人类修正直接改变后续计算所读取的状态，而非添加额外指令或独立记录。论文指出将其扩展到丰富自然语言、关系型和大规模共享状态仍是开放问题。

Conclusion: Transfiver为可交互、可验证、可编辑的人机共同推理提供了基础框架，但尚需进一步研究以适应更复杂和更大规模的共享状态。

Abstract: Long-term human-AI interaction is difficult because the information that guides inference is updated implicitly by the model and is not directly inspectable or controllable by the user. We introduce the TRANSparent Framework for Interactive, Verifiable, Editable Representation (Transfiver), an architecture for human-AI co-inference through a shared editable state. Its central idea is that interaction-specific information is maintained in a single persistent state $(S_t)$ that both the model and the human update.
  Transfiver distinguishes two modes of state evolution. In an implicit stream update, the model interprets ongoing interaction and decides whether new information revises an existing state item or creates a new one. In an explicit directed edit, a human inspects and modifies an addressed item. Both act on the same underlying state, so a human correction changes the state that subsequent computation reads, rather than adding another instruction or separate record.
  The architecture separates shared parameters $(θ)$, learned before ordinary use, from the persistent state $(S_t)$, which evolves during deployment without parameter retraining. Extending Transfiver to rich natural-language, relational, and large-scale shared states remains open.

</details>


### [297] [Govern the Model, Not Only the Data: Storage, Circulation, and Learning in Creative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03800)
*Phoenix Perry,George Simms,Elizabeth Wilson,Yasmine Boudiaf,Nick Bryan-Kinns,Tega Brain,R. Luke DuBois,Alix Rule,Rachel Meade Smith,Kelani Nichole,Atharva Pravin Pawar,Rebecca Fiebrink*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文挑战了"联邦学习天然具备隐私保护优势"的普遍认知，指出联邦只是分布式计算，并非提取式AI的解药。文章提出创意社群可在存储、流通、学习三个层面掌握其作品，并指出现有治理止步于学习层——贡献者能同意训练，却难以掌控由此产生的模型及其联邦。作者提出四项设计原则，倡导构建一个不仅治理数据集、更治理模型及其联邦的"创意数据公地"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习被广泛宣传为隐私保护的进步，但其借用了"联邦"这一源自社会协作的词汇却颠倒了其逻辑：计算被分布化，而最终模型却留在召集训练的一方手中。作者认为，联邦本身并不能补救提取式AI（extractive AI），因为结果取决于谁治理数据与模型、谁对塑造它们的实践拥有主动权。创意社群（如艺术家）的自主权在存储和流通层面已有先例，但在学习层面缺失，这构成了研究的出发点。

Method: 作者分析并比较了艺术家主导的信托、合作社与同意基础设施（consent infrastructures），展示了创作者治理在存储与流通层已能实现，但在学习层面中断。基于此，作者将技术开放问题与人类学问题配对，映射出研究空间，进而提出四项设计原则：治理模型而非仅语料库；在贡献时刻使条款可读；将拒绝设计为一等状态；公开决定管理权并加以说明。

Result: 本文通过梳理现有创意社群治理实践，揭示了"存储-流通-学习"三层中学习层治理的空白，并确立了四项设计原则作为未来"创意数据公地"的治理框架，该公地不仅治理数据集，更治理模型及其联邦化过程。

Conclusion: 联邦学习不足以保障创作者权益；真正需要的是把治理对象从数据集扩展到模型本身及其联邦形态。为此须在设计上落实四项原则，赋予贡献者对模型的实质话语权，使拒绝成为合法且受支持的状态，并在开放、可问责的程序中决定数据与模型的最终管理权。

Abstract: Federated learning is increasingly presented as a privacy-preserving advance: personal data remain on the device, and only model updates are shared. It borrows the vocabulary of the federated social web, yet inverts its logic, distributing computation while the resulting model stays with whoever convened the training. We argue that federation is not in itself a remedy for extractive AI, because outcomes depend on who governs the data and the model and who has agency over the practices that shape them. We describe three layers at which a creative community can hold its work: storage, circulation, and learning. Examining artist-governed trusts, cooperatives, and consent infrastructures, we show that creator governance is established at storage and circulation but stops at learning: contributors can consent to training, yet have little say over the resulting model or its federation. We map the research space this opens, pairing technical open problems with the human questions from which they unfold. We propose four design principles for a creative data commons that governs models and their federation, not only datasets: govern the model, not only the corpus; make the terms legible at the moment of contribution; design for refusal as a first-class state; and decide stewardship in the open and account for it.

</details>


### [298] [SVG-Score: Human-Aligned Evaluation of Text-to-SVG Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03806)
*Marco Cipriano,Leonardo Zini,Alexandra Schild,Valentin Teutschbein,Afsana Mimi,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi,Gerard de Melo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SVG-Bench，一个面向文本到SVG生成任务的人类对齐评估框架，包含语义对齐数据集、适配CLIP评分器和经强化学习训练的VLM评判器，并基于独立描述集对主流生成器进行基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SVG生成模型日益强大，但缺乏领域专用评估协议；现有方法使用面向自然图像的指标（如CLIPScore），其未针对矢量图形训练，且与人类判断契合度有限。通过可控扰动实验发现CLIP评分对颜色、数量、空间关系等SVG典型错误几乎无反应，而现成VLM评判器响应不均，因此需要更可靠、更对齐人类的评估框架。

Method: 首先通过设计受控的标题和图像扰动，系统分析CLIP评分与VLM评判器对各类错误及SVG风格的敏感度。然后构建带人工标注的语义对齐数据集。在此基础上开发两个互补评估器：一是针对矢量图形适配并对齐人类偏好的CLIP评分器（用于大规模快速评估）；二是经监督微调和基于奖励的强化学习训练的VLM评判器（用于更具表达力和可解释性的评估）。最后用独立描述集对多个开源、商业和优化型SVG生成器进行基准测试。

Result: 实验表明CLIP类评分对SVG生成器的典型错误（颜色、数量、空间关系等）几乎不敏感；现成VLM评判器虽更敏感但在不同错误类型和SVG风格上响应不均匀。所提出的两个评估器在语义对齐上更能反映人类偏好，并成功应用于对主流SVG生成器的统一基准评估。

Conclusion: SVG-Bench提供了首个针对文本到SVG生成的人类对齐评估协议，既支持大规模快速筛选，又支持细粒度可解释评判，为SVG生成模型的性能衡量和迭代优化提供了更可靠的依据。

Abstract: Scalable Vector Graphics (SVG) generation is attracting increasing attention as generative models improve in expressiveness and controllability. Progress, however, is held back by the lack of domain-specific evaluation protocols: current practice relies on metrics designed for natural images, most notably CLIPScore, which was never trained on vector graphics and aligns only partially with human judgment. We introduce \textbf{\ours}, a human-aligned evaluation framework for text-to-SVG generation. Through controlled caption and image perturbations, we first show that CLIP-based scores barely react to the errors SVG generators actually make, such as wrong colors, counts, and spatial relations, and that off-the-shelf Vision-Language Model (VLM) judges, while more sensitive, respond unevenly across error types and SVG styles. We then introduce a human-annotated dataset for \textit{Semantic Alignment}, measuring how faithfully a generated SVG reflects its caption. Building on it, we develop two complementary evaluators: CLIP scorers adapted to vector graphics and then aligned to human preferences, for fast large-scale evaluation, and a VLM judge trained with supervised fine-tuning and reward-shaped reinforcement learning, for more expressive and interpretable assessment. Using both, we benchmark major open-source, commercial, and optimization-based SVG generators on an independent caption set.

</details>


### [299] [CauseCollab: Causal Unified and Modality-Agnostic Network for Heterogeneous Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03818)
*Weize Li,Yang Li,Quan Yuan,Xiaoyuan Fu,Guiyang Luo,Jinglin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出CauseCollab，一个因果统一且模态无关的协同感知网络，通过因果度量学习解耦语义与模态混杂因素，并采用上下文引导的统一转换器保证跨模态语义一致性，在OPV2V和DAIR-V2X上达到SOTA，尤其在大模态差异场景提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于协议的两阶段方法将异构特征映射到共享协议空间，但独立训练的模态特定转换器会产生模态特定的伪协议分布，导致语义不一致和误差累积，尤其在模态差异大的场景中更明显。

Method: 从因果视角构建协议空间中的表征学习，通过因果度量学习显式解耦语义因素与模态特定统计混杂因素；同时采用上下文引导的统一转换器处理异构模态，保证跨模态语义一致性；新增模态仅需训练少量参数的适配器。

Result: 在OPV2V和DAIR-V2X数据集上达到最先进性能，在模态差距大的场景中获得更显著的性能提升。

Conclusion: CauseCollab有效解决了协议空间中模态特定转换导致的语义不一致问题，提升了协同感知的泛化性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Collaborative perception enhances environment understanding through multi-agent information sharing, but its performance in real-world scenarios is constrained by heterogeneous sensor modalities and model architectures. Recent protocol-based two-stage methods alleviate this problem by mapping heterogeneous features into a shared protocol space; however, independently trained modality-specific converters often generate modality-specific pseudo-protocol distributions, leading to semantic inconsistency and error accumulation, which is particularly pronounced in scenarios with large modality discrepancies. To address this issue, we propose CauseCollab, a causal unified and modality-agnostic network. CauseCollab formulates representation learning in the protocol space from a causal perspective, explicitly disentangling semantic factors from modality-specific statistical confounders via causal metric learning. Meanwhile, CauseCollab adopts context-guided Unified Converter for heterogeneous modalities to ensure cross-modal semantic consistency. In addition, integrating new modalities only requires training adapters with minimal parameters. Extensive experiments on the OPV2V and DAIR-V2X datasets demonstrate that CauseCollab achieves state-of-the-art performance, with more significant gains in scenarios involving large modality gaps.

</details>


### [300] [Semantic Bayesian World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03834)
*Tommaso Soru*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出语义贝叶斯世界模型（SBWM），将知识图谱视为共享且演化的信念网络，通过贝叶斯条件化进行更新，以弥补知识图谱的确定性断言与概率推理系统（如基础模型）之间的不匹配，并列出实现该愿景所需的技术组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱以确定性的断言描述世界，而当代消费这些图谱的系统（基础模型、自主智能体）本质上是概率推理。这种不匹配导致语言模型与知识图谱的集成仅停留在数据喂养流水线，而非统一的推理架构。

Method: 构想语义贝叶斯世界模型（SBWM）：将网络描述为覆盖知识图谱的共享、演化的信念织物；本体论公理约束先验，观测通过贝叶斯条件化更新信念，动作干预世界。通过多个场景（门卫安全代理、精算估计、规划任务、未陈述量估计）演示其增益，并提出所需构建：RDF 1.2上的信念标注、概率蕴含机制、语义校准层、以及智能体间交换和分歧的协议。

Result: 通过示例展示SBWM如何使智能体获得超越传统知识图谱和语言模型的能力：例如区分快递员或盗贼、通过蕴含而非字符串频率进行精算估计、完成语言模型常失败的规划任务、估计文档中从未陈述的量。

Conclusion: 作者认为构建SBWM需要社区开展多项基础工作，包括信念注释标准、概率推理范式、语义校准以及智能体间的信念交换协议，从而将知识图谱从事实数据库转变为共享演化的信念网络。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs describe reality in crisp assertions, while the systems now consuming them, foundation models and autonomous agents, reason natively in probabilities. We argue that this mismatch is why the integration of language models and knowledge graphs remains a data-feeding pipeline rather than a unified reasoning architecture. We envision Semantic Bayesian World Models (SBWMs): a Web that describes the world not as a database of facts but as a shared, evolving fabric of beliefs over knowledge graphs, where ontological axioms constrain priors, observations update beliefs by Bayesian conditioning, and actions intervene upon the world. We work through what an agent gains from such a model: a home-security agent deciding whether the figure at the gate is a courier or a burglar, an actuarial estimate aggregated by entailment rather than by string frequency, a planning task that language models reliably fail, and the estimation of quantities that no document has ever stated. We then set out what the community must build to make them possible: belief annotation over RDF~1.2, probabilistic entailment regimes, semantic calibration layers, and protocols by which agents that have never met can exchange, and disagree over, calibrated beliefs.

</details>


### [301] [Adapting to Evolving Requirements: Agentic AI for Retail Supply Chain Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03860)
*Lei Zheng,Liping Yang,Zihao Li,Guodong Lyu,Chaik Ming Koh,Chung-Piaw Teo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对零售供应链中异构决策管线（由多个耦合优化模块组成）的需求驱动自适应问题，提出一种图约束的智能体框架：领域智能体暴露可接受的改型接口，中央处理器在受限干预路径上搜索，联合选择干预路径与模块级改动，并用下游KPI验证候选方案。与三个基础LLM（GPT、Qwen、DeepSeek）结合，在100个真实零售仓库需求上，相比直接LLM改型，正确性和端到端成功率均提升（端到端成功率从72–76%提升到79–83%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的需求驱动自适应方法主要聚焦于单个优化模型，难以扩展到异构决策管线；因为一个需求可能对应多条干预路径，且各路径下游影响不同，需要联合考虑干预路径选择和模块级改动。

Method: 提出图约束智能体框架：领域智能体暴露可接受的重构接口，中央处理器在受限干预路径上搜索；将需求驱动自适应建模为联合选择干预路径和可接受的模块级改动；通过下游KPI验证并比较候选方案。

Result: 与大型零售伙伴合作，用GPT、Qwen和DeepSeek作为基础LLM评估100个仓库需求；相对直接LLM重构，框架在三个模型上均提升正确性和端到端成功率，端到端成功率从72–76%提高到79–83%。

Conclusion: 图约束智能体框架能更有效地将LLM应用于零售供应链异构决策管线的需求驱动自适应，显著优于直接LLM重构方法，并具备跨模型泛化性。

Abstract: Retail supply chain operations rely on coupled decision modules that must adapt as requirements evolve. LLMs offer a natural-language interface for this task, but existing methods primarily focus on individual optimization models. Extending them to heterogeneous decision pipelines is challenging because a requirement may admit multiple intervention paths with different downstream effects. We formulate requirement-driven adaptation as the joint selection of an intervention route and an admissible module-level change, and propose a graph-constrained agentic framework in which domain agents expose admissible reformulation interfaces and a central processor searches over bounded intervention paths. Candidates are validated and compared using downstream KPIs. In collaboration with a large retail partner, we evaluate 100 warehouse requirements elicited from practitioner interviews, with GPT, Qwen, and DeepSeek as base LLMs. Relative to direct LLM reformulation, our framework improves correctness and end-to-end success across all three models, raising end-to-end success from 72--76% to 79--83%.

</details>


### [302] [Bioinfoysis Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03871)
*Qingyang Shao,Xin Zhang,Zhouyang Yuan,Xianying Chen,Yujia Xiang,Zihao Yang,Tong Ye,Yangqi Zhang,Jiakang Xu,Xiaoqing Yan,Xuan Luo,Keyi Li,Enci Fan,Kai Kang,Zhuohan Liu,Xingyu Jin,Chunran Teng,Tao Li,Xinyu Lv,Minghui Wang,Wenfeng Li,Yidan Gao,Siyu Liu,Mingrui Luo,Zhu Liang,Guanren Qiao,Zhiping Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Bioinfoysis，一个多智能体框架，将每个请求表示为持久化、基于工件（artifact）的分析运行，通过全局规划与基于证据的逐步重规划相结合，并采用结构化交接机制防止陈旧证据复用，显著提升长时程生物信息学任务的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型智能体在生物信息学中主要聚焦于生成最终答案，将规划、工具使用和代码执行视为瞬时交互，不适合需要将结论与数据、计算和中间证据保持连接的长时程任务。

Method: Bioinfoysis采用多智能体框架，包含全局规划器维护可执行检查清单，并根据每次工作节点执行后的结构化交接信息进行证据驱动的逐步重规划；交接信息将中间结果绑定到责任智能体、检查清单步骤和规划代次，防止重规划后复用陈旧证据；同时通过受控运行时验证生成的脚本、表格和图形，并配备角色特定上下文、持久记忆和受管控的生物信息学技能。

Result: 在BixBench上达到82.4%的准确率，达到当前最优；在Lab-Bench 2的两个问答轨上，Bioinfoysis将四种底层语言模型的平均准确率从27.81%提升至64.13%（SeqQA2），从3.13%提升至31.25%（DbQA2）。

Conclusion: 可靠的生物信息学自动化不仅依赖于模型能力，还依赖于管理规划、执行、记忆和证据流的框架。Bioinfoysis的提出有望推动生物信息学社区的发展。

Abstract: Large language model agents have shown promise in bioinformatics, but most existing systems focus primarily on producing final answers, treating planning, tool use, and code execution as transient interactions. This design is poorly suited to long-horizon bioinformatics tasks, where conclusions must remain connected to the data, computations, and intermediate evidence that support them. We introduce \textbf{Bioinfoysis}, a multi-agent harness that represents each request as a persistent, artifact-grounded analysis run. Bioinfoysis combines global planning with step-wise, evidence-driven replanning: the planner maintains an executable checklist and revises pending steps using structured handoffs returned after each worker execution. These handoffs bind intermediate results to their responsible agent, checklist step, and plan generation, preventing stale evidence from being silently reused after replanning. A controlled runtime validates generated scripts, tables, and figures before they are used in downstream analysis or reporting, while role-specific context, persistent memory, and governed bioinformatics skills support reliable execution over long analysis trajectories. We evaluate Bioinfoysis on BixBench and two question-answering tracks of LAB-Bench 2. On BixBench, Bioinfoysis achieves state-of-the-art accuracy of 82.4\%. Across four underlying language models, Bioinfoysis increases average accuracy from 27.81\% to 64.13\% on SeqQA2 and from 3.13\% to 31.25\% on DbQA2. These results demonstrate that reliable bioinformatics automation depends not only on model capability, but also on the harness that governs planning, execution, memory, and evidence flow. We hope that the emergence of Bioinfoysis will play a driving and leading role in the development of the bioinformatics community. Our demo website can be seen in https://report.bioinfoysis.com/.

</details>


### [303] [STAIR (STructure Aware Information Retriever): A novel dataset and LLM based retriever for document structure augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03874)
*Vineet Kumar,Meghanadh Pulivarthi,vishwajeet kumar,Jaydeep Sen,Riyaz Ahmad Bhat,Sachindra Joshi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新型检索系统STAIR，利用语料库的全局结构（如目录ToC）来增强大语言模型的检索能力，有效解决RAG系统中因长文本分块导致的"lost in the middle"问题。在发布的新基准SearchTome上，STAIR实现了82.6%的Recall@1，显著优于DSI（76.9%）、BM25（59.5%）、DPR（68.7%）等基线，且幻觉率低于0.05%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG是大语言模型生成准确、无幻觉回答的关键组件。尽管LLM处理长上下文的能力在提升，但仍存在"lost in the middle"问题，因此精确检索至关重要。现有检索器将长文本切分为基于长度的块，这一过程丢失了语料库中丰富且信息量大的语义全局结构（如目录）。作者希望利用这种全局结构来构建更精确、低幻觉的检索系统。

Method: 提出STAIR检索系统，使LLM能够利用语料库的全局结构（如表征全书层次结构的目录ToC），在模型参数中高效地存储和检索信息。系统基于微调的可微分搜索索引（DSI）构建，通过目录结构辅助生成式信息检索。此外，作者构建了SearchTome基准，包含来自6个不同领域的18本书籍，用于评估目录驱动的检索方向。

Result: 通过细致、谨慎的消融研究，目录（ToC）帮助构建了低幻觉（低于0.05%）的生成式信息检索系统，且能推广到训练样本极少的场景。在SearchTome基准上，STAIR的Recall@1达到82.6%，显著优于DSI（76.9%），并大幅超越BM25（59.5%）、DPR（68.7%）和现成的Mistral（13.8%），差异具有统计学显著性。

Conclusion: 利用语料库全局结构（目录）的STAIR系统在准确性和抗幻觉能力上均优于传统长度分块的检索方法，同时具有少样本泛化能力。作者通过发布SearchTome基准，为目录驱动的检索这一新方向提供了研究基础，展示了利用语义全局结构的RAG检索系统的潜力。

Abstract: Retrieval Augmented Generation (RAG) is a key component for generating accurate and hallucination free answers using Large Language Models (LLMs). LLMs are improving at handling long context, but still suffer from "lost in the middle" problem. Thus, precise and accurate retrieval is important. Current retrievers chunk long context into length-based manageable chunks - in the process throwing away rich and informative semantic global structure in the corpus. We introduce a novel retrieval system STAIR that empowers an LLM to exploit global structure in a corpus such as a Table of Contents (ToC) to efficiently store and retrieve information from its model parameters. Our thorough and careful ablation studies with a finetuned Differentiable Search Index (DSI) system show that ToC helps build a low hallucination (less than 0.05%) generative Information Retrieval (IR) system and can generalize to examples where very few training samples are available. To further research in this novel direction of ToC based retrieval we release SearchTome - a diverse benchmark created from 18 books across 6 diverse domains to further research in this novel direction. STAIR achieves a high Recall@1 score of 82.6% on SearchTome as compared to DSI (76.9%), where the difference is found to be statistically significant. STAIR easily beats other strong baselines such as BM25 (59.5%), DPR (68.7%) and out-of-the-box Mistral (13.8%).

</details>


### [304] [Xiaomi-TabLDM: A Tabular Foundation Model Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03880)
*Xiaomi-TabLDM Team,:,Penghui Wang,Wei Liu,Hong Wang,Chengyue Huang,Yuxi Sun,Zirui Wang,Hongming Huang,Quan Wang,Chunxiao Liu,Erli Meng,Bin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了 Xiaomi-TabLDM，一个面向表格数据的分类与回归任务的上下文学习（in-context learning）大型基础模型，仅使用结构因果模型（SCMs）生成的合成数据进行预训练，在不需任务特定微调的情况下取得优越预测精度。模型在 OpenML-CTR23 上排名第一，在多个回归基准上表现优异，同时大幅降低计算成本（训练时间减少82%，预测时间减少68%）。模型采用三阶段训练、双流特征分组、轻量注意力残差和稀疏专家混合等技术，并支持测试时计算扩展（test-time scaling），在推理时分配更多计算持续提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表格数据基础模型在回归任务上表现欠佳，且通常需要任务特定的微调，缺乏灵活上下文利用和高效容量扩展能力。作者旨在构建一个仅在合成数据上预训练、无需微调即可在分类与回归任务上取得强预测性能的表格基础模型，并同时解决性能与计算效率之间的权衡问题。

Method: Xiaomi-TabLDM 采用仅基于结构因果模型（SCMs）生成的合成数据的大规模预训练，扩展了合成表格数据的覆盖范围与多样性。训练采用三阶段训练策略，结合双流特征分组（dual-stream feature grouping）、轻量注意力残差（lightweight Attention Residual）和稀疏专家混合（sparse Mixture-of-Experts），以学习更丰富的特征交互和专家专业化。此外，模型支持测试时计算扩展（test-time scaling），通过在推理阶段分配额外计算来提升预测性能。

Result: Xiaomi-TabLDM 在 OpenML-CTR23 上排名第一，在 TALENT、TabArena 和 BCCO 回归基准上排名第二，在四个互补基准套件上展现出持续强劲的回归性能。在效率方面，在 TabArena 回归任务上取得第二高 Elo，同时比排名第一的 TabFM 减少 82% 训练时间和 68% 预测时间。测试时计算扩展在推理阶段持续提升预测性能。

Conclusion: Xiaomi-TabLDM 展示了仅用合成数据预训练即可实现无需微调的强泛化能力，在预测精度与计算效率之间取得了优异平衡，同时通过测试时计算扩展进一步提升了性能，为表格基础模型的发展提供了新的性能标准和高效的可扩展方案。

Abstract: We introduce Xiaomi-TabLDM, a tabular large data foundation model for classification and regression via in-context learning, which delivers superior prediction accuracy without requiring task-specific fine-tuning. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data generated from structural causal models (SCMs), our model enables more flexible context utilization and more efficient capacity scaling.
  i) A new performance standard. Strong regression performance across benchmarks: Xiaomi-TabLDM ranks 1st on OpenML-CTR23 and 2nd on regression across TALENT, TabArena, and BCCO, demonstrating consistently strong regression performance across four complementary benchmark suites. Favorable performance--efficiency trade-off: Xiaomi-TabLDM combines strong predictive performance with substantially lower computational cost. For example, on TabArena regression, it achieves the second-highest Elo while using 82% less training time and 68% less prediction time than the top-ranked TabFM.
  ii) Large-scale synthetic pretraining. Xiaomi-TabLDM expands the coverage and diversity of synthetic tabular data used for pretraining. We also adopt a three-stage training strategy together with dual-stream feature grouping, lightweight Attention Residual, and sparse Mixture-of-Experts, enabling Xiaomi-TabLDM to learn richer feature interactions and expert specialization across diverse tabular tasks.
  iii) Test-time scaling. Xiaomi-TabLDM further extends tabular prediction through test-time compute scaling, where allocating additional computation at inference time consistently improves predictive performance over the base model.

</details>


### [305] [Inferring Affective Consciousness in an Artificial Agent: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03883)
*Mark Solms,St John Grimbly,Bruce Bassett,Evert Boonstra,Rowan Hodson,Nicolas Kuske,Kival Mahadew,Benjamin Rosman,Charel van Hoof,Jonathan Shock*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了人工智能体如何通过实例化情感系统中的“内在需求与环境资源相关的感受性不确定”属性，表现出享乐性位置偏好行为，即使该智能体完全是确定性的；并讨论了这种人为工程行为对意识物理基础和自由意志体验的启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学家认为，表现出“享乐性位置偏好行为”的生物（如对有营养价值的可卡因、吗啡产生偏好）可能具有主观感受，因为这种对无营养价值物质的吸引难以用无意识的本能行为解释。作者试图表明，一个简单的人工智能体同样能表现出这种行为，从而挑战这种基于行为的意识推断。

Method: 论文通过构建一个简单的人工智能体，该智能体实例化情感系统的属性，即对其内在需求相对于环境资源的关系进行“感受性不确定”的信息处理。该过程表面上是主观的，但整个智能体是完全确定性的。作者以此机制演示其能产生享乐性位置偏好行为。

Result: 研究结果表明，即使在一个完全确定性的简单人工系统中，也可以通过看似主观的信息处理形式重现享乐性位置偏好行为，说明这种偏好行为本身并非意识或主观感受的充分证据。

Conclusion: 作者由此推断，此类人工工程行为对理解意识的物理基础以及自由意志体验具有重要意义：行为层面的主观表现可能并不必然依赖非物理或非决定性的过程，从而为意识的物理主义解释提供支持，并挑战自由意志作为非决定论体验的传统看法。

Abstract: Creatures that display 'hedonic place preference behaviour' are thought by many scientists to experience feelings, on the assumption that their attraction to pleasure-producing substances which lack nutritional value (e.g. cocaine, morphine) cannot easily be attributed to unconscious instinctual behaviour. In this paper, we discuss how a simple artificial agent that instantiates attributes of an affective system engaging in felt uncertainty about its intrinsic needs in relation to environmental resources can similarly display hedonic place preference behaviour -- through an apparently subjective form of information processing -- while simultaneously being entirely deter-ministic. We outline some implications of this artificially engineered behaviour for our understanding of the physical basis of consciousness and the experience of free will.

</details>


### [306] [Lose the Order, Keep the Hierarchy: Deordering HTN Plans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03912)
*Takudzwa Togarepi,Gaspard Quenard,Damien Pellier,Humbert Fiorino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对HTN规划中的计划去序化问题，将经典规划中的两种去序技术扩展到HTN设置，以适应层次分解约束，并在IPC 2023部分有序HTN基准上与直接生成部分有序计划的Optiplan进行比较，结果显示两种实现都大幅减少了排序约束数量，关键路径长度也有一定缩短但改进不明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管HTN规划大多关注计划生成，但对计划后优化的研究较少。计划去序化在经典规划中已被广泛研究，但在HTN环境下的研究不足。去序化能移除计划中不必要的排序约束同时保持计划有效，因此需要将经典规划的去序化技术适配到HTN规划中。

Method: 将经典规划中的两种成熟计划去序化技术进行适配，扩展这些技术以考虑HTN中的层次分解约束，从而实现HTN计划的去序化。

Result: 在IPC 2023部分有序HTN基准上的实验表明，两种实现的排序约束数量均大幅减少；同时观测到关键路径长度也有所缩短，但改进幅度不如排序约束的减少那么显著。两种方法均与Optiplan进行了对比。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效减少HTN计划中的排序约束数量，在保持计划有效性的前提下实现去序化，并在基准测试中优于直接生成部分有序计划的Optiplan，展示了HTN计划后优化的潜力。

Abstract: Hierarchical Task Network (HTN) planning is a powerful planning formalism based on task decomposition. Although most of the literature studied plan generation, comparatively less attention has been paid to post-plan optimization. In particular, plan deordering has been extensively studied in classical planning but remains under-researched in the HTN setting. Plan deordering removes unnecessary ordering constraints between actions in a plan whilst keeping the plan valid. In this paper, we adapt two established plan deordering techniques from classical planning by extending the techniques to account for hierarchical decomposition constraints. We evaluate our proposed approaches on the IPC 2023 Partial-Order HTN benchmarks and we compare them against Optiplan, an HTN planner that generates partially ordered plans directly. Our results show a substantial reduction in number of ordering constraints in both our implementations. Although we also observe a reduction in critical path length, the improvements are less pronounced.

</details>


### [307] [Value-Preserving Architectures for Agentic AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03920)
*Alessandro Pesare,Tommaso Dolci,Katja Hose,Emanuel Sallinger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了基于LLM的多智能体系统（MAS）中，架构设计如何影响人类中心价值观（如隐私、公平、安全）的保持。论文提出了三种价值保持的架构模式：基于联邦拓扑的隐私感知架构、促进多元性的分布式架构、以及用于检测和缓解不公平性的守护智能体架构，并通过用例说明其实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着智能体AI和基于LLM的多智能体系统在复杂社会技术系统中的广泛应用，传统软件工程仅关注功能正确性已不足以应对隐私、公平、安全等人类中心价值观被侵蚀的风险。现有研究缺乏将架构设计决策与价值对齐系统性地联系起来的框架。

Method: 论文采用论证与设计模式分析的方法：首先提出架构选择不仅影响MAS的功能与性能，也能促进面向价值的系统行为的核心论点；然后分别阐述三种具体的价值保持架构模式（联邦拓扑的隐私感知架构、分布式多元架构、守护智能体公平性架构）；最后引入代表性用例说明各架构在真实场景中的应用。

Result: 论文识别并详细阐述了三种与人类中心价值观对应的架构模式：联邦拓扑架构保护隐私、分布式架构促进多元性与多样性、守护智能体架构检测和缓解不公平性，并通过真实场景用例验证了这些架构模式的可实施性。

Conclusion: 本文通过将架构设计与人类中心价值观建立联系，为可信多智能体系统的设计奠定了统一架构模式与指导原则的基础，推动负责任的软件工程实践。

Abstract: The emergence of agentic AI and LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) presents unprecedented opportunities for automating complex tasks, while simultaneously raising critical concerns about the preservation of fundamental human-centered values, such as privacy, fairness, and safety. Although software engineering has traditionally focused on functional correctness, the adoption of LLMs and AI agents into complex socio-technical systems has intensified the need for responsible software engineering and robust value alignment. In MAS, architectural design decisions, such as coordination mechanisms, communication protocols, and system topologies, play a central role in shaping system behavior and the outcomes they produce. This paper argues that architectural choices influence not only the functionality and performance of MAS but can also promote value-oriented system behavior. Therefore, we investigate how different architectural designs support different human-centered values, discussing the following value-preserving architectural patterns: (i) a privacy-aware architecture with a federated topology, (ii) a distributed architecture to promote pluralism and diversity, and (iii) a guard-agent architecture to detect and mitigate unfairness. Finally, we introduce representative use cases to illustrate the proposed architectures in real-world scenarios. By linking architectural design with human-centered values, this work lays the foundation for a unified set of architectural patterns and guidelines towards the design of trustworthy MAS.

</details>


### [308] [Speak for Me: Giving LLMs the Situational Awareness to Participate in a Meeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03923)
*Muneeb Khan,Frederic Kirstein,Terry Ruas,Bela Gipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPA架构，用于在线会议委托中让LLM智能体识别发言时机。通过感知器、预测器、控制器、生成器、裁判和重校准器协同工作，将沉默率从51.4%降至2.5%，并使被认可的恢复贡献加倍（26.1→52.2），同时保持幻觉率0.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线会议委托中，LLM智能体缺乏结构化方式来追踪立场、覆盖度和发言权，导致无法识别应发言的时机。仅靠提示词的委托在AMI语料库上对缺席参与者的发言机会有51.4%保持沉默，亟需一种能预测对话进程并主动贡献的架构。

Method: CAPA采用多模块架构：Perceiver从每个观测轮次更新会议状态；Predictor预测对话后续走向；Controller决定是否发言以及呈现哪个命题；Generator以参与者风格表达所选贡献；两个Judge根据下一轮实际观测对预测和行动打分；Recalibrator依据这些判定更新会议状态以改进未来决策。评估采用新引入的episode级协议，利用schema约束的LLM裁判对委托者是否、何时以及贡献什么进行评分，并与人类标注对齐（Cohen's kappa=0.71）。

Result: 在137个AMI会议上，CAPA将沉默率从51.4%降至2.5%，被认可的恢复贡献从26.1翻倍至52.2，幻觉率保持在0.6%。失败模式从遗漏转向选择，每个残余的近似失误可归因于特定模块。机制消融表明，会议状态是弥合识别差距的关键，仅扩展原始上下文规模并不能实现同样效果。

Conclusion: CAPA通过显式的会议状态维护和预测-行动-校正循环，有效解决了在线会议委托中的发言时机识别问题，显著降低了沉默率并提高了贡献质量。该方法为LLM智能体在实时会议中主动参与提供了可行架构，未来可通过模块级优化进一步减少选择型失误。

Abstract: In online meeting delegation, LLM agents fail to recognize when to speak. With no structured way to track stances, coverage, and floor, they miss the moments where they should contribute. Prompt-only delegates stay silent on 51.4% of the absent participant's talking opportunities on the AMI corpus. We present CAPA (Collaborative Agent Predictive Architecture), an architecture for online meeting delegation. A Perceiver updates the meeting state from each observed turn. A Predictor forecasts how the conversation will continue. A Controller decides whether to speak and which proposition to surface. A Generator phrases the chosen contribution in the participant's style. Two judges score the forecast and the action against the next observed turn. A Recalibrator updates the meeting state from those verdicts for future decisions. To evaluate online delegation, we introduce an episode-level protocol that scores whether, when, and what a delegate contributes around the participant's actual idea units. The protocol's schema-constrained LLM judges align with human annotations at Cohen's kappa = 0.71. On 137 AMI meetings, CAPA reduces the silence rate from 51.4% to 2.5%, doubles credited recovery (26.1 --> 52.2), and keeps hallucination at 0.6%. The failure mode shifts from omission to selection, with each residual near-miss attributable to a specific module of the architecture. Mechanism ablations identify the meeting state as the lever that closes the recognition gap, where raw-context scaling alone does not.

</details>


### [309] [Towards Numerical TOHTN Planning with SMT-based HTN-SAT Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03938)
*Gaspard Quenard,Takudzwa Togarepi,Damien Pellier,Humbert Fiorino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究数值型全序HTN规划问题，展示了如何将标准SAT编码自然地扩展为SMT以处理数值流（numeric fluents），并引入了一个数值TOHTN规划的基准测试套件，为该领域的评估提供首个公共基础。实验表明这一简单编码已构成有竞争力的基线，为更富有表现力的HTN规划方法开辟了道路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来HTN规划虽受到广泛关注，但对数值推理（numerical reasoning）的支持仍非常有限。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索数值型TOHTN规划，并为该设置下的系统评估建立统一的基准。

Method: 作者将标准SAT（命题可满足性）编码方式自然地扩展为SMT（可满足性模理论）编码，以此处理数值流变量；同时设计并引入一套数值TOHTN规划的基准测试套件，作为该领域评估的公共平台。

Result: 实验结果表明，这种相对简单的SMT编码已经在数值TOHTN规划中构成一个有竞争力的基线（competitive baseline），为后续更复杂方法提供了可比较的参照。

Conclusion: 该工作证实了SMT扩展在数值HTN规划中的可行性与有效性，为构建更具表达能力、能够处理数值约束的HTN规划方法开辟了道路。

Abstract: While HTN planning has received significant attention in recent years, support for numerical reasoning remains very limited. In this paper, we investigate numerical Totally-Ordered HTN (TOHTN) planning and show how standard SAT-based encodings can be naturally extended with SMT to handle numeric fluents. In addition, we introduce a benchmark suite for numerical TOHTN planning, providing a first common basis for evaluation in this setting. Experimental results show that this simple encoding already constitutes a competitive baseline. This work opens the way to more expressive approaches to HTN planning.

</details>


### [310] [More Criticism Does Not Make a Better Review: EquiReview-R](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03943)
*Zexing Zhang,Jichao Li,Tianyang Lei,Yude Fu,Yang Kewei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EquiReview-R，将AI辅助评审重新定义为基于证据引导的“结构化关注集”精炼过程，区分遗漏与过度批评两类相反风险。该方法先修订既有关注点（依据局部证据判定停止/继续/延期），再从独立与评审条件化视角搜索遗漏问题。在回顾性轨迹语料上证明修订必须先于搜索，并在冻结论文队列上将重大过度批评率从15.5%降至8.1%（同时满足重大遗漏非劣效标准、遗漏上限9.9%、52.4%论文提前停止），通过计算匹配对照与消融实验证明增益源于修订而非额外推理或更短输出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI审稿人虽能产生大量具体批评，但批评多不等于评审质量高：评审可能遗漏重大缺陷，或保留证据无法支持的指控。这两种失败需要相反方向的修正，而生成式系统与聚合指标掩盖了这一区别，亟需将遗漏与过度批评分开处理的形式化方法。

Method: 将AI辅助评审重构为证据引导的“结构化关注集”精炼，把遗漏与过度批评视为独立风险。引入EquiReview-R：先依据局部证据判定既有关注点的处置（停止/继续/延期并支持修订），再从独立和评审条件化视角搜索缺失问题。构建证据链接的轨迹语料（ReviewTrace）进行回顾性分析，并在冻结论文队列上做预注册的非劣效/上界检验及多种对照实验。

Result: 回顾性分析显示高召回评审中几乎所有关注点缺乏确定性证据处置，而早期精炼机制无法修订它们。在先前未见的冻结论文队列上，EquiReview-R满足重大遗漏预指定非劣效标准；重大过度批评率从15.5%降至8.1%；重大遗漏单侧上界达9.9%，并在52.4%论文上提前停止。计算匹配对照、受控配对与消融证明增益来自修订而非额外推理或更短输出，并释放ReviewTrace语料。

Conclusion: 将评审视为证据引导的修订过程并区分遗漏与过度批评是有效的：先修订既有关注点再进行搜索能显著降低过度批评，同时保持高召回与提前停止能力。这为AI评审的修订、分歧与溯源研究提供了形式化基础与公开数据资源。

Abstract: AI reviewers can now produce many specific criticisms, but more criticism is not necessarily a better review. A review may miss a consequential weakness or retain an allegation that available evidence does not support. These failures require opposite corrections, yet generation-oriented systems and aggregate measures obscure the distinction. We therefore recast AI-assisted review as evidence-guided refinement of a structured concern set, with omission and overcritique treated as separate risks. Building on this formulation, we introduce EquiReview-R, which resolves existing concerns against localized evidence, searches for missing issues from independent and review-conditioned perspectives, and returns stop, continue, or defer. To expose the failure mode that motivates this design, we construct an evidence-linked trajectory corpus. Its retrospective analysis shows why revision must precede further search: nearly all concerns in a high-recall review lack a definitive evidential disposition, while an earlier refinement mechanism cannot revise them. On a frozen cohort of previously unseen papers, EquiReview-R satisfies the prespecified non-inferiority criterion for major omission, reduces major overcritique from 15.5% to 8.1%, and attains a one-sided omission upper bound of 9.9% while stopping on 52.4% of papers. Computation-matched controls, controlled pairs, and ablations show that the gain comes from revision rather than extra inference or shorter output. We release the corpus as ReviewTrace, an evidence-linked resource for studying review revision, disagreement, and provenance.

</details>


### [311] [FiMI Banking: A Sovereign Model for Indian Retail Banking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03960)
*NPCI AI Research Team,Aman Kumar,Asit Desai,Chandra Bhushan,Harsh Sharma,Harshit Bhushan,Hrithik Kadam,Keyur Doshi,Kolisetty Sai Kapardheeswar,Krishanu Adhikary,Nadeem Shaik,Navya Prakash,Nitin Kukreja,Prashant Devadiga,Shamanth MH,Shantanu Pandey,Suvradip Paul,Yatharth Dedhia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 FiMI Banking，一个受控的印度零售银行对话场景，用于评估两种后训练方法：偏好优化（提升安全行为）和基于可验证奖励的强化学习（提升多轮工具调用与边界情形表现）。实验表明两种方法互补，显著提高银行智能体的可靠性与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银行需要能在严格运营与监管约束下回答产品问题、处理账户请求的对话系统，而通用语言模型无法可靠满足这些要求，尤其在需要基于事实的信息、正确工具使用或谨慎处理银行敏感情境时表现不足。

Method: 构建 FiMI Banking 基准，包含经过审核的银行文档、结构化真值、合成客户背景和银行工具。评估两种后训练方法：偏好优化（针对响应层面安全行为）和带可验证奖励的强化学习（针对多轮工具使用任务）。

Result: 偏好优化将越界拒绝率从52%提升到80%；强化学习将边界情形性能从0.509提升到0.718，顺序敏感任务性能从0.590提升到0.679，同时生成令牌数减少29%。

Conclusion: 偏好优化与可验证奖励强化学习分别满足银行智能体在安全性与任务可靠性上的互补需求，两者结合可构建更可靠的银行对话系统。

Abstract: Banks need conversational systems that can answer product questions, assist customers with account-related requests, and operate safely within strict operational and regulatory constraints. General-purpose language models do not reliably meet these requirements. They fall short when a task requires grounded information, correct tool use, or cautious handling of bank-specific sensitive situations. We introduce FiMI Banking, a controlled Indian retail-banking setting. We build it from vetted banking documents, structured ground truth, synthetic customer backgrounds, and banking tools. We evaluate two post-training approaches: preference optimization for response-level behavior, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards for multi-turn tool-use tasks. Preference optimization improves safe behavior substantially: out-of-scope refusal rises from 52% to 80%. Reinforcement learning improves edge-case performance from 0.509 to 0.718 and order-sensitive task performance from 0.590 to 0.679, while using 29% fewer generated tokens. These results show that preference optimization and verifiable-reward reinforcement learning address complementary requirements for reliable banking agents.

</details>


### [312] [Interface-Induced Trajectory Censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03966)
*Wenbo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了一个被忽视的评估陷阱：代理评估报告的工具调用率取决于测量它的模型-接口栈，而非模型本身。仅更换服务适配器，同一模型在同一数据、执行器、评分器、权重、用例、解码和种子下，得分可在0.00与0.96/0.19之间跳变。通过2x2交叉分析精确定位效应完全来自「聊天模板 × 解析器」的交互项，而非任一组件单独缺陷。该问题在训练循环内部同样存在且呈规模依赖性，修复接口可恢复机制但无法带来显著的效果提升。作者发布了98行的预检脚本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代理评估报告的工具调用率是衡量智能体能力的关键指标，由服务栈上报。但该数字可能是零，即使模型在生成格式良好的调用——因为接口在事件流到达下游之前就对轨迹进行了审查/过滤。这意味着业界普遍依赖的评估数字可能是虚假的，需要揭示这一系统性失真及其规模依赖后果。

Method: 作者采用对照消融范式：在BFCL v4自有数据上固定权重、用例、解码和种子，仅更换服务适配器，得到0.00与0.96/0.19的差异。随后构建「聊天模板×解析器」的2x2实验精确定位效应来源。在tau-bench的115个交互式零售任务上复现；跨Qwen2.5-Coder的21倍规模范围探测漏斗；在verl的AgentLoop中考察训练循环内部影响。最终发布98行预检脚本（preflight check）。

Result: 2x2分析显示两个主效应均为零，全部效应落在交互项中——没有单一组件存在缺陷，修复契约单侧不产生任何收益。在tau-bench上，同一替换使服务端解析的调用从0增至636，到达任何工具执行的任务从0增至103。跨规模探测中，服务器在Qwen2.5-Coder各规模下均解析0/100，而格式良好的生成调用在32B升至80/100（经金标准校准约72）。在匹配包络下静默比例保持在0-2。Llama-3.1-8B的23%自调用任务函数率在strict:true标志下降至0。在训练循环内AgentLoop的7B规模，45/115生成含完整调用，但0被接受、0执行、0返回观测。评估时修复适配器恢复机制但无显著效果增益（解析0->84，救援0->9，通过率53->62，n.s.）。

Conclusion: 观测到的工具调用率并非模型本身的属性，而是测量它的模型-接口栈的属性。该失配深入训练循环内部且规模依赖。作者开源的98行预检脚本可捕获本次所有静默失败，为研究社区提供了一个低成本、可靠的一致性检验工具。

Abstract: Agent evaluations report a tool-call rate read off the serving stack. That number can be zero while the model is emitting well-formed calls: the interface censors the trajectory before anything downstream sees it.
  On BFCL v4's own data, executor and scorer, holding weights, cases, decoding and seeds fixed and changing only the serving adapter, the same model scores 0.00 or 0.96 / 0.19. A 2x2 over chat template and parser locates the effect exactly: both main effects are exactly zero and all of it sits in the interaction -- no component is defective, and repairing one side of the contract buys precisely nothing. On tau-bench's 115 interactive retail tasks the same swap moves server-parsed calls from 0 to 636 and tasks reaching any tool execution from 0 to 103. Our probe reproduces the funnel across a 21x scale range of Qwen2.5-Coder: the server parses 0/100 at every size while well-formed emitted calls rise to 80/100 at 32B (~72 after calibration against an adjudicated gold standard). Under a matched envelope, across a comparable scale span, the silent fraction stays at 0-2, a prediction committed to the repository before the run. Llama-3.1-8B's 23% rate of calling the task function itself as a tool falls to 0 under one strict:true flag.
  The mismatch reaches inside the training loop, and its consequence is scale-dependent: in verl's AgentLoop at 7B, 45 of 115 generations carry a complete call; 0 are accepted, 0 execute, 0 return an observation. At 1.5B the same zero is over-determined, so we report the two scales separately. At evaluation time, repairing the adapter restores the mechanism but not a significant outcome gain: parsing 0->84, rescues 0->9, pass rate 53->62 (n.s.). We release a 98-line preflight check that catches every silent failure here. The observed tool-call rate is not a property of the model alone; it is a property of the model-interface stack that measures it.

</details>


### [313] [Common-Witness Certificates and Sharp Feature Bounds for Counterfactual Image Auditing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03973)
*Usef Faghihi,Amir Saki*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对图像编辑中"局部合理但全局无单一潜因解释"的失败，提出利用"共同见证等级"与"见证神经"进行形式化审计的框架。该框架将审计与因果识别分离：共享外生性允许区域边际的任意耦合，而外部证明的见证关系可对预设图像特征给出锐利的偏识别界。借助 Helly 型论证与阻断超图公式，可获得短不相容证书与精确修复计数，并通过同时置信域实现有限样本外层覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像编辑器可能分别满足每个区域的局部合理性约束，但整体输出却不存在任何单一潜隐解释能够同时成立，即存在局部到全局的失败。这一现象在现有审计方法中未被系统形式化，本文旨在为该类失败建立严格的理论框架，并区分"审计"（与因果识别解耦）与"识别"两个层面。

Method: 提出"共同见证等级"（common witness grade）与"见证神经"（witness nerve）两个核心构造，将局部约束与全局一致性关系编码成拓扑/组合结构。框架分离审计与因果识别：在仅凭共享外生性的条件下允许各区域边际量的任意耦合；当存在外部合理化的见证关系时，推导出针对预设图像特征（非随意像素级反事实）的sharp偏识别界。采用 Helly 型论证处理拟凸损失、异质动作分层与有限见证图谱，给出短不相容性证书；以阻断超图公式计算精确修复数目；并用同时置信域构造有限样本下完整识别区间的外层覆盖。

Result: 受控 MNIST、Morpho-MNIST 与 smallNORB 实验验证了所预测的"局部-全局分离"现象；综合仿真实验测试了锐界、证书恢复能力以及结构化计算的性能。实验表明方法能够成功审计声明的特征关系，并提供覆盖完整的识别区间。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种可审计"声明的特征关系"的理论工具，能够有效检测局部-全局不一致并给出修复指导，但其适用范围限定为已声明的特征关系审计，并不用于识别无约束的像素级反事实，体现了审计与因果识别在方法论上的清晰界限。

Abstract: An image editor may satisfy every regional plausibility constraint separately even when no single latent explanation fits the complete output. We formalize this local-to-global failure using a common witness grade and witness nerve. The framework separates auditing from causal identification: shared exogeneity alone allows every coupling of the regime marginals, whereas an externally justified witness relation yields sharp partial-identification bounds for prespecified image features. Helly-type arguments provide short incompatibility certificates for quasiconvex losses, heterogeneous action strata, and finite witness atlases; a blocker-hypergraph formula gives exact repair counts. Simultaneous confidence regions for the regime marginals give finite-sample outer coverage of the complete identified interval. Controlled MNIST, Morpho-MNIST, and smallNORB studies demonstrate the predicted local-global separation, while synthetic experiments test sharp bounds, certificate recovery, and structured computation. The method audits a declared feature relation and does not identify unrestricted pixel-level counterfactuals.

</details>


### [314] [The Dually Flat Geometry of Planning as Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04005)
*Nikola Milosevic,Asaki Kataoka,Nicolas Hinrichs,Kenji Doya,Nico Scherf*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种对强化学习中占用测度的替代性刻画，通过将规划准则嵌入动态系统形成一个重置规划过程，其平稳测度被称为"访问测度"。可行的访问测度构成一个对偶平坦统计流形，其两个仿射坐标图分别是访问概率和对数策略，并在条件熵意义下互为对偶。这一结构使"规划即推理"能从线性奖励推广到访问测度的非线性泛函，每次迭代仅需一步自然梯度更新，同时赋予时序差分误差以边际效用估计的解释。文章系统发展了这一几何结构及其在强化学习与理论神经科学中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习将占优/占用测度视为固定策略下状态-动作对的出现频率，其几何结构未被充分揭示。作者旨在为决策过程的信息几何找到一个最自然表达的对象——访问测度，从而统一规划、推理与学习，并将耗散的规划准则与动态系统有机结合，突破奖励函数局限于线性的假设。

Method: 作者通过一个"重置规划过程"将规划准则嵌入马尔可夫决策过程的动态中，使其平稳测度（访问测度）成为刻画决策的核心对象。利用信息几何，证明所有可达访问测度构成一个对偶平坦统计流形，其两个仿射坐标图表分别为访问概率与对数策略，二者在条件熵下互为对偶（对偶平坦的Bregman散度结构）。在此基础上，规划被形式化为流形上的几何优化，每一步优化采用单一自然梯度步求解非线性泛函目标。

Result: 获得的几何结构使规划-即-推理能够从线性奖励推广至访问测度的任意非线性泛函，且每次迭代仅需一步自然梯度即可收敛。同时，时序差分（TD）误差在此框架下获得了边际效用估计的明确解释，连接了值函数学习与经济学中的效用概念。该方法对强化学习算法设计与理论神经科学中的决策建模均具有直接推论。

Conclusion: 本文确立了访问测度作为强化学习信息几何的核心对象，构建了对偶平坦统计流形框架，为非线性奖励规划提供了一步步自然梯度求解的算法路径，并为TD误差提供了边际效用的理论解释，从而为强化学习与理论神经科学建立了统一的几何视角。

Abstract: We present an alternative characterization of the occupancy measure of reinforcement learning, obtained by embedding the planning criterion into the dynamics through a resetting planning process. Its stationary measure, which we term visitation measure, is the object on which the information geometry of decision making is most naturally expressed. The achievable visitation measures form a dually flat statistical manifold whose two affine charts are the visitation probabilities and the log-policies, dual under the conditional entropy. This structure makes planning-as-inference generalize from linear rewards to nonlinear functionals of the visitation, each iterate solved by one natural-gradient step, and gives the temporal-difference error the interpretation of a marginal-utility estimate. We develop the geometry and its consequences for reinforcement learning and theoretical neuroscience.

</details>


### [315] [LLM4CKD: Large Language Models for Early Stage Chronic Kidney Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04013)
*Muhammad Ashad Kabir,Sirajam Munira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）在慢性肾脏病（CKD）筛查中的应用，探讨其在零样本和少样本情境下的表现，并对比传统机器学习和深度学习方法。LLMs在低数据场景下具有竞争力，但稳定性不如传统方法，可作为有限标注数据时的灵活补充手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CKD早期筛查对及时干预至关重要，但现有ML/DL方法依赖标注数据和模型训练，难以在真实筛查场景中广泛应用，亟需探索无需任务特定训练的替代方案。

Method: 提出一个框架，使用临床选择的表格特征与结构化提示模板，使LLM无需任务特定训练即可进行推理。评估涵盖多种提示风格、特征配置和数据设置，并与标准ML、DL、表格基础模型（TFM）基线及现有CKD筛查工具进行对比。

Result: LLMs在仅使用少量示例时即可达到有竞争力的性能，在低数据场景下常能匹配或超越传统方法；但其性能依赖模型选择，且随输入复杂度增加稳定性下降。而ML、DL和TFM模型在更大训练数据下能实现更一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了数据效率与稳定性之间的权衡，表明LLMs可作为标注数据有限时CKD筛查的灵活补充方法，但不完全取代传统模型。

Abstract: Early screening of chronic kidney disease (CKD) is critical for timely intervention, yet most machine learning (ML) and deep learning (DL) approaches require labeled data and model training, limiting their use in real-world screening settings. This study evaluates the effectiveness of large language models (LLMs) for CKD screening under zero-shot and few-shot in-context learning settings and compares them with traditional ML and DL methods. We propose a framework that uses clinically selected tabular features and structured prompt templates to enable LLM-based inference without task-specific training. LLM performance is evaluated across multiple prompt styles, feature configurations, and data settings, and compared with standard ML, DL, and tabular foundation model (TFM) baselines, and existing CKD screening tools. The results show that LLMs can achieve competitive performance using only a small number of examples, often matching or outperforming traditional approaches in low-data settings. However, their performance remains model-dependent and less stable as input complexity increases. In contrast, ML, DL, and TFM models show more consistent improvement with larger training data. Overall, the findings highlight a trade-off between data efficiency and stability, suggesting that LLMs may serve as a flexible complementary approach for CKD screening when labeled data are limited.

</details>


### [316] [InSituMeasure: Probing Situated Measurement Grounding in Industrial Scenes with Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04014)
*Chao Shen,Xinyuan Li,Yunfan Zhou,Jianguo Yao,Haibing Guan,Zhihai Wang,Xijun Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 InSituMeasure 基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在真实工业场景中的"情境化测量接地"能力。基准包含 2,922 个真实工业监控场景、八类专业工程仪器、密集的仪表属性标注与噪声标签。在 24 个 SOTA MLLM 上，最佳模型仅有 25.7% 的数值-单位联合准确率和 51.8% 的置信度-诊断 F1，揭示通用多模态能力与可靠情境测量之间存在巨大差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MLLM 在通用多模态基准上表现强劲，但在连续值测量任务上仍不可靠。而现有基准虽暴露了这一弱点，却将测量与真实知识接地场景隔离——缺少情境上下文、专业仪器、真实噪声以及匹配的诊断标注，降低了现实性并制约了根因分析。因此需要更真实、可进行失败诊断的测量基准。

Method: 构建 InSituMeasure 基准：包含 2,922 个真实工业监控场景、八类功能专业的工程仪器，具备密集的仪表属性标注（gauge-attribute annotations）和噪声标签（noise tags）以支持失败诊断。定义三类指标：(1) 在预设容差和单位一致性下的数值准确率；(2) 对伪造或不可解答任务的拒答能力；(3) 模型失败与标注错误因素之间的对齐程度（confidence-diagnosis F1）。在 24 个 SOTA MLLM 上进行评测。

Result: 最佳模型仅达到 25.7% 的联合数值-单位准确率和 51.8% 的置信度-诊断 F1，说明通用多模态能力与可靠情境测量之间存在显著差距。失败分析识别出三类成因：文本诱导的捷径（text-induced shortcuts）、过度自信的响应，以及真实的工业噪声（混合扰动、视角偏差、遮挡和环境干扰）。

Conclusion: 现有 MLLM 在真实情境化测量任务上远未达到可靠水平，失败源于文本捷径、过度自信与真实工业噪声。InSituMeasure 为系统性测量理解和失败的根因诊断提供了更真实的评估载体，推动通用多模态智能向可靠的专门化测量能力发展。

Abstract: For trained operators, gauge reading requires little specialized knowledge, low cognitive effort, and high repeatability. Yet Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) remain unreliable in continuous-valued measurement despite strong results on general multimodal benchmarks. Existing benchmarks expose this weakness but isolate measurement from realistic, knowledge-grounded settings, with limited situated context, specialized instruments, real-world noise, and matched diagnostic annotations, reducing realism and constraining root-cause analysis. We introduce InSituMeasure to evaluate situated measurement grounding. It contains 2,922 real industrial monitoring scenes across eight functional categories of professional engineering instruments, with dense gauge-attribute annotations and noise tags for failure diagnosis. We define metrics for numerical accuracy under predefined tolerances and unit consistency, rejection of fake or unanswerable tasks, and alignment between model failures and annotated error factors. Across 24 state-of-the-art MLLMs, the best model reaches only 25.7\% joint value-unit accuracy and 51.8\% confidence-diagnosis F1, revealing a substantial gap between general multimodal competence and reliable situated measurement. Further analysis identifies failures from text-induced shortcuts, overconfident responses, and authentic industrial noise, including mixed disturbances, viewpoint deviation, occlusion, and environmental interference.

</details>


### [317] [FLY-EVAL++: An Evidence-Driven Evaluation Protocol for Safety-Constrained Flight Prediction with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04021)
*Yalun Wu,Junfeng Fang,Jiawei Wang,Haotian Liu,Qijun Yang,Minghan Yang,Hongcheng Guo,Zhoujun Li,Boyang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对安全关键、受物理规则约束的LLM评测，提出FLY-EVAL++协议，用确定性验证和固定规则聚合生成多维可解释分数，并在飞行轨迹与姿态预测任务上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测仅依赖准确率指标，无法检测数值接近但违反操作约束、物理不一致或输出不可用的失败模式；传统协议不可靠地度量这些缺陷。

Method: 提出证据驱动的评测协议FLY-EVAL++：对协议合规性、物理可行性和安全性进行确定性验证，并用固定标准引导聚合为多维可解释分数；在PilotBench基础上扩展历史条件预测和多步预测任务。

Result: 在66个LLM上，安全合规是最具区分度的维度：预测性能相近的模型安全分数相差超过28分，并反复出现物理合理但安全违规、多步 rollout 不稳定等问题。

Conclusion: 安全关键领域评测应显式度量约束满足与结构有效性，而非仅依赖以准确率为中心的汇报。

Abstract: Evaluating large language models (LLMs) in safety-critical, physics-governed environments requires more than accuracy-based metrics, because predictions that are numerically close to the ground truth can still violate operational constraints, combine fields in physically inconsistent ways, or fail to produce usable structured outputs. Existing evaluation protocols do not measure these failure modes reliably. We propose FLY-EVAL++, an evidence-driven evaluation protocol that combines deterministic verification of protocol compliance, physical feasibility, and safety constraints with fixed rubric-guided aggregation into interpretable multi-dimensional scores. We instantiate FLY-EVAL++ for Flight Trajectory and Attitude Prediction (FTAP) by extending the PilotBench setting with history-conditioned and multi-step prediction tasks. Across 66 LLMs, safety compliance is the most discriminative dimension of model behavior: models with comparable predictive performance differ by more than 28 points in safety score, and we observe recurrent failures including safety violations under physically plausible predictions and instability in multi-step rollouts. These results show that evaluation in safety-critical domains should measure constraint satisfaction and structured validity explicitly rather than rely on accuracy-centric reporting alone.

</details>


### [318] [Instruction Duplication as an Inference-Time Control Primitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04024)
*Victor Lavrenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"指令重复"（instruction duplication）——一种最小化的黑盒推理期控制方法，仅重复过程性指令而不需重训或改动解码。在7个指令微调模型、300道医学多选题、8种放置条件和16,800次预定生成的实验中，指令从1份增至2份使All-8诊断通过率从90.22%升至93.17%（+2.95个百分点），消除了1份时剩余失败的30.2%；TF-IDF召回率提升1.38个百分点（Holm校正p<.001），而最终答案准确率保持60.21%不变。盲审挑战未达到预设的28/30确认标准（10/30方向性确认、20/30感知平局、无逆转），但在依赖显式轨迹状态的下游系统（如Answer Engineering）中，该控制带来的实际价值显著：SSNHL端点从84.2%（仅AE）提升至97.1%（AE+尾随重复），凸显其低复杂度、对放置位置敏感的特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过程性指令遵循是可控语言模型系统（尤其是轨迹需被下游审查或修复时）的基本要求。作者希望寻找一种在不改动训练与解码的前提下，以极小干预提升指令遵循可靠性的方法，并探究其对下游消费暴露轨迹的系统所产生的实际影响。

Method: 提出"指令重复"：在推理期仅将过程性指令重复一次，不涉及重训练与解码改动。设计了大规模实验（7个指令微调模型、300道医学多选题、8种放置条件、16,800次预定生成），以All-8诊断、TF-IDF召回率、最终答案准确率、过早承诺率为评测指标，并辅以盲审挑战测试与下游Answer Engineering（AE）系统集成验证。

Result: 指令重复使All-8诊断通过率由90.22%升至93.17%（+2.95个百分点），消除单份指令剩余失败的30.2%；TF-IDF召回率由73.44%升至74.81%（Holm校正p<.001）；最终答案准确率保持60.21%不变；过早承诺率由1.52%升至2.30%（p_Holm=.00536）。盲审挑战结果为10/30方向性确认、20/30感知平局、无逆转，未达预设28/30标准。在AE下游系统中，SSNHL端点由84.2%（仅AE）升至97.1%（AE+尾随重复），而传导分支在AE内反而下降（78.6%→73.8%），但仍高出无编辑基线14.9个百分点。

Conclusion: 指令重复是一种低复杂度、对放置位置敏感的控制手段，对最终答案准确率无影响，对过程性轨迹与观测指标有提升但仍需盲审严格验证。其实际价值取决于消费暴露轨迹的下游系统（如AE），即该控制的有效性具有任务/系统依赖性，并非普遍一致地提升。

Abstract: Procedural instruction following is a basic requirement for controllable language-model systems, especially when generated trajectories are inspected or repaired downstream. We introduce instruction duplication, a minimal black-box inference-time control that repeats only the procedural instruction, without retraining or decoding changes. Across seven instruction-tuned models, 300 medical multiple-choice questions, eight placement conditions, and 16,800 scheduled generations, moving from one to two copies raises the deterministic All-8 diagnostic--responses passing all eight observable tests--from 90.22% to 93.17% (+2.95 percentage points), eliminating 30.2% of the failures remaining after one copy. Pre-provisional TF-IDF recall rises from 73.44% to 74.81% (+1.38 points; Holm-adjusted p < .001), while final-answer accuracy remains exactly 60.21%. Premature commitment increases from 1.52% to 2.30% (p_Holm = .00536). A blinded challenge audit yields 10/30 directional confirmations, 20/30 perceptual ties, and no reversals; its prespecified 28/30 confirmation criterion is not met. Yet this distinction can matter operationally when a downstream system acts on the generated trajectory. In Answer Engineering (AE), where explicit trajectory state determines local repair, the published reason-first no-editing SSNHL endpoint was 25.1%; system-only AE was later reproduced at 84.2%, and the same trailing duplicate raised it to 97.1%. For conductive diagnostic branch preservation, the corresponding values are 58.9% published without editing, 78.6% with reproduced AE, and 73.8% with AE plus duplication--a within-AE decrease, but still 14.9 points above the no-editing baseline. Instruction duplication is therefore a low-complexity, placement-sensitive control whose practical value can emerge through the downstream system that consumes the exposed trajectory.

</details>


### [319] [IRWOZ 2.0: A Large Language Model-driven Dialogue Dataset for Industrial Robot Conversations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04030)
*Chen Li,Dimitrios Chrysostomou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍IRWOZ 2.0，一个改进版的面向工业人机交互（HRI）的对话数据集。该数据集通过大规模语言模型（Mistral/Claude-3.5）增强生成并进行质量精修，将规模扩展至4个工业领域（装配、配送、定位、搬运）的390个对话，并经过手动纠错和自动化错别字清理。在对话状态跟踪（DST）基准上，GPT-2的BLEU-4分数从0.1651提升至0.5604，显著优于原始IRWOZ，体现了数据集质量的显著改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始IRWOZ数据集虽通过领域特定标注改进了工业人机交互对话系统，但其初始版本在对话状态和话语层面包含大量噪声，限制了状态跟踪的准确性。为解决这一质量瓶颈，作者提出IRWOZ 2.0。

Method: 采用LLM增强生成（Mistral/Claude-3.5）对原始数据集进行重建，并辅以三重质量优化手段：手动纠错、自动化错别字清理、领域扩展（新增至4个领域、390个对话）。数据集已公开在IEEE DataPort平台。

Result: 在对话状态跟踪基准测试中，IRWOZ 2.0带来显著性能提升，GPT-2的BLEU-4分数从原始IRWOZ的0.1651大幅提升至0.5604（提升约2.4倍），验证了数据质量改进的有效性。

Conclusion: IRWOZ 2.0通过LLM驱动生成与严格质量管控，有效消除了原始数据噪声并扩大了覆盖面，显著提升了工业HRI对话状态跟踪性能，为工业机器人对话研究提供了高质量公开资源。

Abstract: IRWOZ has improved industrial human-robot interaction (HRI) dialogue systems through domain-specific annotations. However, its initial version contains substantial noise in dialogue states and utterances, limiting state-tracking accuracy. We introduce IRWOZ 2.0, which addresses these limitations through large language model (LLM) enhanced generation (Mistral/Claude-3.5) and quality refinements. Our improved dataset expands to 390 dialogues across 4 industrial domains (Assembly, Delivery, Position, Relocation), featuring manual corrections and automated typo removal. Benchmark experiments on dialogue state tracking demonstrate significant improvements, with GPT-2's BLEU-4 score increasing from 0.1651 to 0.5604 compared to original IRWOZ. To support industrial HRI research, we publicly released IRWOZ 2.0 dataset at https://ieee-dataport.org/documents/irwoz-20-large-language-model-driven-dialogue-dataset-industrial-robot-conversations

</details>


### [320] [Spurious Advantage Hidden in GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04063)
*Jiamian Wang,Samyadeep Basu,Koustava Goswami,Tong Yu,Zhiqiang Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文识别了GRPO（群组相对策略优化）奖励估计器中的一个被忽视的问题——虚假优势（spurious advantage）：当rollout通过猜测而非真正推理到达正确答案时，公式仍会赋予其高幅值奖励，误导策略趋向猜测式行为。作者提出SIGNBALANCE方法，其幅值设计为组合无关（composition-free）：保留验证器符号、使用全局尺度，并通过stop-gradient逐类重新缩放恢复零均值平衡。实验表明，SIGNBALANCE在开放答案数学任务上与GRPO持平，在受限答案数学与搜索智能体任务上优于GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GRPO被广泛用于可验证奖励的强化学习，其优势估计器根据组内奖励统计为每个rollout分配幅值。在常见情况下，该幅值会奖励通过推理得出正确答案的rollout，但存在一个被忽视的等同情况：rollout可能通过猜测命中正确答案，公式仍赋予高幅值，这被识别为虚假优势（spurious advantage），会误导策略产生猜测式行为。

Method: 提出SIGNBALANCE方法。其幅值设计为组合无关（composition-free）：保留验证器的符号（verifier sign），采用全局尺度（global scale），并通过stop-gradient逐类重新缩放（per-class rescaling）恢复零均值平衡。

Result: 虚假优势出现在三种情形：候选集较小的受限答案任务（bounded-answer tasks）、包含受限子情形的开放答案集，以及预算允许通向同一答案的多条路径的搜索智能体。实验在数学与搜索智能体基准、不同规模下进行，SIGNBALANCE在开放答案数学上与GRPO持平，在受限答案数学与搜索智能体上优于GRPO。

Conclusion: 虚假优势会误导策略趋向猜测式行为；SIGNBALANCE通过组合无关的幅值设计有效缓解该问题，在受限答案与搜索智能体场景中显著优于GRPO，同时保持开放答案数学上的性能。代码将开放。

Abstract: Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) is widely studied for reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, where its advantage estimator assigns each rollout a magnitude from within-group reward statistics. In the common case, this magnitude rewards rollouts that reach the correct answer through reasoning. Yet, an overlooked case shares the same surface: a rollout may land on it by guessing, and the formula still assigns a high magnitude, which we identify as the spurious advantage. This arises in three cases: bounded-answer tasks with a small candidate set; open-answer sets hosting bounded sub-cases; and search agents whose budget opens many paths to the same answer. In all three, this misleads the policy toward guess-like behaviors. We propose SIGNBALANCE, whose magnitude is composition-free: it keeps the verifier sign, uses a global scale, and restores zero-mean balance via a stop-gradient per-class rescaling. Across math and search agent benchmarks at different scales, SIGNBALANCE matches GRPO on open-answer math and improves on bounded-answer math and search agents. Code will be released.

</details>


### [321] [DRACO: Fine-Grained Credit Assignment with Dynamic Rubrics for Long-Horizon Agent Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04094)
*Shubham Gandhi,Saurabh Goyal,Kiran Kate,Yara Rizk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出DRACO框架，通过动态生成多准则评分细则并在GRPO中按步骤重新分配奖励，解决了可验证奖励缺失的长程智能体任务中奖励稀疏问题，在AppWorld和Tau-Bench上均超越了基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可验证奖励的强化学习在程序化检查器存在时表现良好，但多数长程智能体领域缺乏此类检查器。在结果不可见环境中，单一标量奖励在数十步的轨迹中无法提供足够的训练信号，需要更细粒度的奖励分配机制。

Method: DRACO在训练过程中动态生成多准则评分细则以追踪策略能力的演化，对完成的轨迹进行一次评分，利用闭式公式将评分重新分配给产生各评分细责的步骤，生成GRPO中逐步骤差异化优势，无需额外训练归因模块。

Result: 在AppWorld上，DRACO比基础模型提升15.9分，比使用稀疏真实奖励训练的GRPO提升5.3分；在域外Tau-Bench上，比基础模型提升5.3分，且无需前沿评判器，同时超越真实奖励训练和其他评分细则训练方案。

Conclusion: DRACO证明了在无验证器的长程智能体任务中，通过动态评分细则与闭式奖励再分配可以取得优于使用真实奖励的训练效果，是一种无需额外归因模型的高效奖励信用分配方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning from Verifiable Rewards works well when a task has a programmatic checker, but most long-horizon agent domains have none. We work in the outcome-blind setting, where ground-truth success signals are not available. Multi-criteria rubrics are a popular way to supply such a reward; they are scored once per trajectory, but a single scalar is a poor signal across tens of steps. We propose DRACO: Distributing Rubric-based Advantage for Credit Optimization. It generates rubrics dynamically during training to track the policy's evolving capability, scores those rubrics once per completed trajectory, and redistributes that judgment over the steps responsible for annotated rubrics to produce differentiated per-step advantages in GRPO. The redistribution is closed-form and does not introduce any trained attribution module. On AppWorld, DRACO gains 15.9 points over the base model and 5.3 points over GRPO trained with a sparse ground-truth reward, despite not using any verifiers itself. On out-of-domain Tau-Bench, it gains 5.3 points over the base model even without a frontier judge, beating both ground-truth-reward training and other rubric-based training settings. The code for DRACO is available at https://github.com/IBM/draco.

</details>


### [322] [Why Gated DeltaNet Survives 4-Bit Quantization: NVFP4 W4A4 for the Recurrent Half of a Hybrid 27B LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04098)
*Sergii Kozyrev,Davyd Maiboroda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Minima，一种针对混合LLM（Qwen3.8-27B，含48层Gated DeltaNet线性注意力层）的NVFP4 W4A4全量线性层量化方案。与社区先前仅在8/16位下量化GDN的做法不同，Minima将全部496个线性层（含GDN的decay与write-strength门）全部量化到4bit，在困惑度、MMLU-Pro、GSM8K、AIME'25、GPQA-Diamond、LiveCodeBench及RULER检索最长64K等基准上与BF16持平（5任务均值-0.52，处于随机种子噪声内），同时是最小（17.5 GiB）且prefill最快（+14-19%）的方案。研究通过四部分机制解释为何混合LLM的“循环半区”（GDN）反而是容易量化的部分：NVFP4的16元素块缩放局部化残差流极端离群值；门投影（softplus/exp/sigmoid参数化）把约11%的GEMM误差压缩到约2%输出误差；delta-rule循环可将注入噪声维持在平坦平台并在数百步内遗忘状态脉冲；逐token量化开销随上下文稀释而非累积。此外还修复了per-module校准的NVFP4 checkpoint与模块融合GEMM内核之间的全局尺度失配，并证明校准的FP8 KV缓存尺度无性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社区对Qwen3.8-27B的早期4bit量化经验性地将GDN层（尤其decay和write-strength门）保留为8/16位精度，理由是循环状态中的误差会在长上下文中累积。本文旨在检验这一直觉是否成立：如果能够安全地把包括GDN在内的所有线性层量化到4bit，就能在保持性能的同时显著减小模型体积并加速prefill。

Method: 构建Minima：对Qwen3.8-27B的全部496个线性层（含48层GDN与16层softmax注意力）统一采用NVFP4 W4A4量化。通过对照实验进行四部分机制归因：(i) NVFP4的16元素块缩放如何局部化残差流极端离群值、均衡不同层角色的激活误差；(ii) 测量门投影（softplus/exp与sigmoid参数化）如何把约11%的GEMM误差压缩到约2%的输出误差；(iii) 考察delta-rule循环在32K token上对注入噪声的平坦平台维持以及对状态脉冲在数百步内的遗忘行为；(iv) 分析逐token量化开销随上下文稀释而非复合的效应。同时修复了per-module校准的NVFP4 checkpoint与融合GEMM内核之间的全局尺度失配问题，并验证了校准的FP8 KV缓存尺度。

Result: 在4K/32K困惑度、MMLU-Pro、GSM8K、AIME'25、GPQA-Diamond、LiveCodeBench和RULER（至64K）上，Minima与BF16在随机种子噪声内匹配（5任务平均-0.52），且32K困惑度差距随位置缩小；同时它是对比方案中最小的（17.5 GiB）且prefill速度最快（+14-19%）。机制研究表明：NVFP4块缩放局部化掉残差流极端离群值；门投影对量化误差极不敏感；delta-rule循环将注入噪声维持在平坦平台并在数百步内遗忘状态脉冲；逐token量化开销随上下文稀释。FP8 KV缓存尺度校准无性能损失。

Conclusion: 结论是给出一个实用配方——“量化一切，附带KV尺度”（quantize everything, ship KV scales），并提供一个机制层面解释：在混合LLM中，循环半区（GDN）反而是最容易量化的部分，因此社区先前对GDN保留高精度的直觉并不成立。作者已公开其在Hugging Face上的checkpoint。

Abstract: Hybrid LLMs pair softmax attention with linear-attention layers such as Gated DeltaNet (GDN), whose recurrent state summarizes the context in fixed size. Early community 4-bit quantizations of Qwen3.8-27B (48 GDN layers, 16 attention layers) left the GDN block in 8- or 16-bit precision -- especially its decay and write-strength gates -- on the intuition that errors in a recurrence accumulate over long contexts. We test that intuition by building Minima: NVFP4 W4A4 on all 496 linear layers, GDN included. Across perplexity at 4K/32K, MMLU-Pro, GSM8K, AIME'25, GPQA-Diamond, LiveCodeBench, and RULER retrieval to 64K, Minima matches BF16 within seed noise (5-task average -0.52) while being the smallest (17.5 GiB) and fastest-prefill (+14-19%) recipe we compare, and its 32K perplexity gap shrinks with position. A four-part mechanism study explains why: (i) NVFP4's 16-element block scaling localizes the residual stream's extreme outliers, equalizing activation error across layer roles; (ii) the supposedly fragile gate projections are the least sensitive -- softplus/exponential and sigmoid parameterizations compress ~11% GEMM error to ~2% output error; (iii) the delta-rule recurrence holds injected noise at a flat plateau over 32K tokens and forgets a state impulse within hundreds of steps, because each write overwrites the state along the current key direction; (iv) the per-token quantization cost washes out with context instead of compounding. We also repair a global-scale mismatch that arises when per-module-calibrated NVFP4 checkpoints are served by kernels that fuse those modules into one GEMM, and show calibrated FP8 KV-cache scales are performance-free. The result: a practical recipe -- quantize everything, ship KV scales -- and a mechanistic account of why the recurrent half of a hybrid LLM is the easy half to quantize. Checkpoint: https://huggingface.co/minima-ai/mnma_qwen3.8_27b_nvfp4

</details>


### [323] [Epistemic Warrant for LLM Recommendations: Characterizing the Basis for Reliance When Ground Truth Is Unavailable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04127)
*Shai Vardi,João Sedoc*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“认知依据”（epistemic warrant）这一决策层面构念，用于刻画大语言模型（LLM）对成对建议的偏好稳定性及其适用范围，并构建四层依赖证书（不稳定、情境依赖、局部支持、广泛支持）来表征单个建议的可依赖程度。通过已知组检验与群体共识验证其有效性，并证明该构念不同于口头自信和决策难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常评估模型的整体属性（如可靠性、不确定性、鲁棒性）或用户信任，而缺乏对单个推荐建议是否值得依赖的底层依据进行原则性判断。作者希望从认识论理论出发，提供一个决策级别、可操作化的构念。

Method: 将认识论中的“依据”（warrant）适配为模型偏好稳定性与适用范围；设计四层依赖证书用于成对推荐；采用已知组检验验证专家预设的等级顺序，并使用众包工作者的独立共识进行交叉验证；同时对比口头自信和决策难度以检验区分效度。

Result: 已知组检验成功恢复了专家预设的依据排序；更强的依据与独立共识系统性地一致；认知依据提供的信息区别于口头自信，且不能由决策难度简单解释。

Conclusion: 该框架为在缺乏客观真值的情况下，提供了一种有理论支撑且可实施的单条LLM建议依据刻画方法。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to support organizational decisions, yet users often lack a principled basis for assessing whether to rely on a specific recommendation. Existing approaches typically evaluate broad model properties, such as reliability, uncertainty, or robustness, or focus on user trust, rather than the underlying basis for relying on an individual recommendation. Adapting theoretical foundations from epistemology, we introduce epistemic warrant, a decision-level construct that characterizes the stability of a model's preference and the scope over which that preference holds. We operationalize this construct through a four-tier reliance certificate for pairwise recommendations, distinguishing among unstable, context-dependent, locally supported, and broadly supported recommendations. We validate the construct using contemporary methodologies: known-groups tests successfully recover expert-prespecified warrant orderings, and stronger warrants systematically align with independent consensus from crowd workers. Furthermore, we demonstrate that epistemic warrant provides information distinct from verbalized confidence and is not readily explained by decision difficulty. Ultimately, this framework offers a theoretically grounded, implementable approach for characterizing the warrant of individual LLM recommendations when objective ground truth is unavailable.

</details>


### [324] [Environment Evolution for Terminal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04128)
*Zhiyuan Fan,Tinghao Yu,Yuanjun Cai,Jiang Zhou,Jiangtao Guan,Jincheng Liu,Yun Yang,Dingxin Hu,Zhuo Han,Xing Wu,Feng Zhang,Lilin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"环境进化"（environment evolution）方法：通过离策略方式逐步提升环境难度，并在训练过程中逐代调度进化后的环境，为终端智能体提供持续的学习信号，解决了现有协同进化方法依赖在线策略 rollout、泛化能力受限的问题，在多项基准测试上显著提升了模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着前端模型能力不断增强，从零合成（synthesized from scratch）的环境越来越缺乏挑战性，无法提供有效的学习信号。已有的协同进化方法虽能围绕模型可学习前沿迭代生成环境，但其依赖在线策略（on-policy）的 rollout，这限制了模型的泛化能力，且随着模型变强难以持续提供学习信号。

Method: 作者提出环境进化框架：首先从多轮学习目标推导出三条影响环境难度的进化方向（three evolution directions），再通过循环工程化的多智能体协作机制（loop-engineered multi-agent harness）沿这些方向实现环境进化；进化过程采用离策略（off-policy）方式，并在训练过程中按世代（generation by generation）调度进化后的环境，从而持续提供学习信号。

Result: 量化 rollout 实验表明，环境进化（配合 Hy4 preview、Claude Opus 5 与 GPT-5.6 Sol）能持续产生更困难的环境。在简单长时程强化学习（long-horizon RL）训练中，该方法使 Qwen3.6-27B 与 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 在 Terminal-Bench 2.1 上的性能分别提升 14.4 和 18.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: 环境进化通过离策略的难度递增与逐代调度，能够克服协同进化方法对在线性rollout的依赖，为不断变强的模型持续提供高质量训练信号，从而显著提升终端智能体的能力，具备良好的扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: Scaling interactive and verifiable environments is critical for training terminal agents. As frontier models become more capable, environments synthesized from scratch become less challenging and thus provide limited learning signals. Recent co-evolution methods iteratively synthesize environments near the model's learnable frontier based on weaknesses exposed during rollouts. However, their dependence on on-policy rollouts limits generalization and the continuous provision of learning signals as the model becomes stronger. In this paper, we propose environment evolution, which incrementally increases environment difficulty off-policy and schedules the evolved environments generation by generation during training to provide continuous learning signals. We derive three evolution directions that influence environment difficulty from the multi-turn learning objective and then implement evolution along these directions through a loop-engineered multi-agent harness. Quantitative rollout experiments with Hy4 preview, Claude Opus 5, and GPT-5.6 Sol show that environment evolution consistently produces more difficult environments. We validate its effectiveness on Qwen3.6-27B and Qwen3.6-35B-A3B through simple long-horizon RL training, improving their performance by 14.4 and 18.0 percentage points on Terminal-Bench 2.1, respectively.

</details>


### [325] [The Natural Language Interaction Protocol and Standard for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04135)
*Luyi Xing,Rasit Onur Topaloglu,Ranjan Sinha,Abhay Ratnaparkhi,Samuel Ndichu,Christopher Nguyen,Anindita Das,Tom Sheffler,Mohamed Rahouti,Zichuan Li,Xiaojing Liao,Sanjay Aiyagari*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍自然语言交互协议（NLIP），一个由Ecma International标准化的、基于标准的人工智能智能体应用层通信协议。NLIP提供轻量级语义消息信封，可承载于HTTP/HTTPS、WebSocket和AMQP等现有传输协议之上，使采用异构框架、模型、工具和环境的AI智能体能够通过统一协议互操作。论文阐述了其动机、设计原理、消息模型、传输绑定、安全设计、参考实现、代表性应用、采纳信号，以及与MCP和A2A等新兴智能体协议的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI智能体在各类组织中被采用异构的开发框架、AI模型、工具接口、协议和执行环境所构建和部署，这些智能体之间缺乏统一的通信手段，难以实现互操作，从而制约了其潜在的社会和商业影响力。因此，亟需一个通用的、基于标准的应用层通信协议来打通异构智能体之间的交互壁垒。

Method: NLIP定义了基于标准的应用层协议，采用轻量级语义消息信封，可复用HTTP/HTTPS、WebSocket和AMQP等既有传输通道。协议允许NLIP感知的智能体和网关在客户端、智能体、本地上下文存储、本体、工具、企业服务以及异构底层协议之间进行适配转换，并融入security-by-design（安全内建）的设计考量，同时提供参考实现加以验证。

Result: 论文呈现了NLIP的动机与设计原理、消息模型与传输绑定、安全内建设计、参考实现、代表性应用场景，以及协议采纳信号；并阐明其与MCP、A2A等新兴智能体协议之间的关系与定位，表明NLIP在异构智能体生态互操作方面具备实际可行性和行业支持。

Conclusion: NLIP作为一项标准化的人工智能智能体应用层交互协议，通过轻量语义信封与现有传输协议的复用，有效解决了异构AI智能体间的互操作问题，兼顾安全性设计，并在实践中展现出应用价值和采纳趋势，确立了其在智能体协议生态中的独特定位。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly being developed and deployed across organizations using heterogeneous agent-development frameworks, AI models, tool interfaces, protocols, and execution environments. To realize their potential social and business impact, these agents must be able to interoperate through a common communication protocol. The Natural Language Interaction Protocol (NLIP), developed by researchers and practitioners across companies and universities and standardized by Ecma International, addresses this need by defining a standards-based application-layer protocol for AI-agent interaction. NLIP provides a lightweight semantic message envelope that can be carried over existing transports such as HTTP/HTTPS, WebSocket, and AMQP, while allowing NLIP-aware agents and gateways to adapt between clients, agents, local context stores, ontologies, tools, enterprise services, and heterogeneous underlying protocols. This paper presents the motivation and design rationale of NLIP, its message model and transport bindings, security-by-design considerations, reference implementation, representative applications, adoption signals, and relationship to emerging agent protocols such as MCP and A2A.

</details>


### [326] [Efficient Test-Time Adaptation through Human-AI Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04141)
*Zora Zhiruo Wang,Apurva Gandhi,Rulin Shao,Aspen Chen,Jonas Mueller,Zhiqi Liang,Jett Chen,Michael Ryan,Qianou Ma,Luxi He,Zhoujun Cheng,Andre He,Seungone Kim,Jiayi Geng,Mingqian Zheng,Weiwei Sun,Zheyuan Zhang,Xinran Zhao,Yike Wang,Abe Hou,Liwei Jiang,Pang Wei Koh,Diyi Yang,Graham Neubig,Daniel Fried*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出通过人机交互的测试时自适应（TAHI）方法，利用跨会话的用户交互数据来个性化AI智能体，使其从群体平均水平提升到个体专家水准。在写作和视觉创作两个高实用领域对30名个体、600项任务进行实验，智能体的单任务成功率提升4.5-20.9%，且进化评分模块作为可扩展标注工具，比单独的LM或人类捕获多16.0-22.3%的失败；尽管针对个体自适应，个性化智能体还能在跨用户间产生最高8.8%的成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI智能体虽在群体规模数据上训练出广泛能力，但其产出的工件很少达到专业人员愿意为之背书的个人标准。在成功标准异构且文档化不足的开放任务中，个体专长恰恰体现在对平均水平的超越与偏离。迭代式人机交互能浮现出用户无法事先完整指定、却又跨任务反复应用的标准，作者认为这种跨会话交互数据是弥合与个体专长差距的丰富但未被充分利用的信号。

Method: 提出测试时自适应（test-time adaptation through human-agent interaction, TAHI），将跨会话交互信号整合进智能体的上下文和权重中，并通过一个不断进化的评分模块（evolving rubric module）来结晶每个用户的训练与评估标准。该评分模块同时作为可扩展的标注工具。

Result: 在写作与视觉创作两个高实用领域，针对30名个体、共600项任务进行自适应，智能体在仅仅数十项任务内便使单任务成功率提升4.5-20.9%。进化评分模块比单独的LM或人类捕获多16.0-22.3%的失败；这些针对个体的个性化智能体在跨用户场景下也产生了最高8.8%的成功率提升。

Conclusion: 跨会话的人机交互数据是弥合从群体平均水平到个体专长差距的有效信号。TAHI方法能够快速、可扩展地将智能体个性化到个人标准，且演化出的评分模块比单独使用LM或人类更可靠，同时个性化智能体的提升还能在一定程度上泛化到其他用户，兼具个性化和一定的通用性。

Abstract: AI agents are trained on population-scale data to encode broad capabilities spanning those of many practitioners. Yet the artifacts they produce rarely meet the personal bar professionals need to stake their reputation on. On realistic, open-ended tasks where success criteria are heterogeneous and insufficiently documented, individual expertise lives precisely in the elevation and departure from the average. In practice, iterative human-agent interaction surfaces criteria that users cannot fully specify up front, yet apply repeatedly across tasks. We argue this cross-session interaction data is a rich, underused signal for closing the gap to individual expertise. In this work, we propose test-time adaptation through human-agent interaction (TAHI), which integrates these signals into agent context and weights, and crystallizes each user's training and evaluation criteria via an evolving rubric module. We adapt agents to 30 individuals in two high-utility domains, writing and visual creation, on a total of 600 tasks. Our agents improve solo task success by 4.5-20.9% within only tens of tasks.
